### [1] [5G NR Sensing of Robotic Arm Motion in Indoor Factory Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28053)
*Neeraj Varshney,Anuraag Bodi,Steve Blandino,Jian Wang,Camillo Gentile,Nada Golmie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文针对工业工厂(InF)环境中单站ISAC系统跟踪机械臂运动学的性能展开研究，构建了基于部件级准确定性信道模型与分层运动学变换的任意关节运动轨迹生成框架，结合3GPP InF标准信道模型与5G NR PRS信号进行系统级评估，量化了工业杂波对感知可靠性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业场景中机械臂等运动目标的感知与通信一体化是智能制造的关键需求，但现有研究缺乏高保真的机械臂目标模型，且工业杂波（尤其密集杂波）对ISAC感知性能的影响尚不明确，需要系统级的定量评估。

Method: 先基于自主的上下文感知信道探测仪与数字孪生获得的高分辨率机械臂测量，建立部件级准确定性信道模型；再通过分层运动学变换生成任意关节运动轨迹；随后将该高保真目标模型与3GPP标准InF信道模型融合，采用5G NR定位参考信号(PRS)作为感知波形，并配置3GPP合规的天线方向图与模拟波束赋形，分别在稀疏杂波(InF-SH)与密集杂波(InF-DH)场景下进行仿真。

Result: LOS条件下可获得较高估计精度；但InF-DH中密集杂波造成严重遮挡，使有效感知覆盖急剧收缩，即使机械臂位于TRP正下方，检测概率也降至约 P_d≈10%；InF-SH中要实现高检测概率（如 P_d≥90%），目标须靠近TRP，有效覆盖半径约为13 m。

Conclusion: 工业杂波尤其是密集杂波是制约单站ISAC机械臂跟踪可靠性的主导因素，实际部署需结合场景杂波等级谨慎规划TRP位置与覆盖范围；所提出的高保真机械臂运动建模与标准信道融合框架可为工业ISAC系统设计与评估提供有效工具。

Abstract: This paper investigates the performance of monostatic Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC) systems for tracking robotic arm kinematics in industrial factory (InF) environments. Building on a part-specific quasi-deterministic channel model previously derived from high-resolution robotic-arm measurements using our context-aware channel sounder and digital twin, we develop a framework for generating arbitrary articulated motion profiles through hierarchical kinematic transformations. By integrating this high-fidelity target model with the standardized 3GPP InF channel model, we employ 5G New Radio (NR) Positioning Reference Signals (PRS) as the sensing waveform to enable comprehensive system-level evaluation. Simulations are conducted for both sparse clutters (InF-SH) and dense clutters (InF-DH) scenarios using 3GPP-compliant antenna radiation patterns and analog beamforming, allowing us to assess the impact of industrial clutter on sensing performance. The results show that while high estimation accuracy is achievable under Line-of-Sight (LOS) conditions, dense clutter in InF-DH environments introduces severe blockage that significantly limits sensing reliability. In particular, the effective sensing coverage in InF-DH is drastically reduced, with detection probability dropping to approximately \(P_d \approx 10\,\%\) even when the robotic arm is located directly beneath the Transmission Reception Point (TRP). Furthermore, in InF-SH, achieving a high detection probability (e.g., \(P_d \geq 90\,\%\)) requires the target to remain in close proximity to the TRP, with an effective coverage radius of approximately 13 m.

</details>


### [2] [FlowPAC: Flow-Guided Visual Pruning and Action Caching for Efficient VLA Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28256)
*Jintao Cheng,Yipu Zhang,Haozhe Wang,Lin Jiang,Jiang Xu,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlowPAC：利用任务感知光流作为共享物理信号，联合优化视觉token与动作计算的免训练加速框架，在LIBERO上实现2.13倍端到端加速且成功率基本保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型推理延迟高、内存占用大，难以边缘部署；现有免训练加速方法（视觉token剪枝、动作缓存）采用静态资源分配，与动态机器人交互不匹配，既损害控制性能，又导致KV-cache布局碎片化和非关键区域算力浪费等硬件低效问题。

Method: 提出基于局部性的FlowPAC框架，以任务感知光流为共享物理信号：Direction-Aware Trajectory Propagation（DATP，方向感知轨迹传播）与Locality-Aware Contiguous Token Selection（LACT，局部性感知连续token选择）联合处理视觉token动态并保证空间局部性，降低内存碎片、实现高效KV-cache压缩；辅以Flow-Conditioned Action-Block Refresh（FCAR，光流条件动作块刷新）按运动动态自适应计算动作。

Result: 在LIBERO上的大量闭环评测显示，FlowPAC相较稠密基线取得2.13倍端到端加速，并在30%视觉token保留率下基本维持原有成功率。

Conclusion: 将计算资源分配同时对齐物理动态与硬件约束，是VLA在边缘系统上高效部署的关键。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a powerful paradigm for embodied AI, yet their high inference latency and memory footprint remain major barriers to edge deployment. Existing training-free acceleration techniques, such as visual token pruning and action caching, typically rely on static resource allocation. This mismatch with dynamic robotic interactions not only degrades control performance but also causes severe hardware inefficiencies, including fragmented KV-cache layouts and wasted compute on non-critical regions. To address this, we propose FlowPAC, a locality-aware framework that leverages task-aware optical flow as a shared physical signal to jointly optimize visual and action computation. Specifically, we introduce Direction-Aware Trajectory Propagation (DATP) and Locality-Aware Contiguous Token Selection (LACT) to jointly handle visual token dynamics and enforce spatial locality, reducing memory fragmentation and enabling efficient KV-cache compression. This is complemented by Flow-Conditioned Action-Block Refresh (FCAR) for adaptive action computation based on motion dynamics. Extensive closed-loop evaluations on LIBERO demonstrate that FlowPAC achieves a 2.13$\times$ end-to-end speedup over the dense baseline while essentially maintaining the success rate with 30% visual-token retention. These results validate that aligning computation allocation with both physical dynamics and hardware constraints is essential for efficient VLA deployment on edge systems.

</details>


### [3] [Neural Fidelity Calibration: Turning Field Anomalies into Simulation Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28260)
*Youwei Yu,Lantao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出神经保真度校准（NFC）框架，用条件流匹配从机器人在线执行数据中同时推断仿真物理参数与残余保真度（未建模动态+感知/真实环境几何差异），并配合异常检测触发重校准与基于乐观探索的主动不确定性缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真到真机迁移的策略常在实际场景失败：传统仿真校准只调少数人工挑选参数，即便校准后仍无法刻画未建模动态；而基于机载感知重建的环境又带有传感噪声与伪影。因此需要一种能同时覆盖参数化误差与非参数化残差、并可在线运行的校准方法。

Method: 核心为基于条件流匹配的神经网络，从机器人执行数据直接在线推断（1）仿真器物理参数与（2）残余保真度，即剩余动态偏差以及感知环境与真实环境几何之间的差距（可含128x128残余地形图）。此外，用仿真训练的异常检测器仅凭机载传感信号判断何时需重校准或微调；并用乐观面对不确定性（OFU）的探索策略主动调制后验不确定性。

Result: 在五个机器人平台（最多39个校准参数、128x128残余地形图）上，NFC给出的后验分布精度优于现有基于仿真的推断基线。在实际户外实验中，一辆发生轴断裂的轮式机器人在雪、泥、湿草地形上，粗地形导航成功率由26%提升至70%，朝向抖动降低88%。

Conclusion: 将仿真校准从"少数参数点估计"扩展为"参数+残余保真度"的在线贝叶斯后验推断，并由异常检测与主动探索闭环驱动，可显著提升仿真到现实迁移在退化硬件与复杂地形下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: A policy that transfers from simulation to a lab robot often fails in the field. Calibrating a simulator from real-world data typically targets only a few hand-picked parameters, and even calibrated simulators fail to capture unmodeled dynamics, while environments reconstructed via onboard perception inherit sensing noise and artifacts. We propose Neural Fidelity Calibration (NFC), a framework based on conditional flow matching that infers online, directly from a robot's execution data, both simulator physical parameters and residual fidelity - the remaining dynamics discrepancy and the gap between perceived and true environment geometry. A simulation-trained anomaly detector determines when recalibration or fine-tuning is required using onboard sensory signals alone, while an exploration policy actively modulates posterior uncertainty using optimism in the face of uncertainty. Across five robot platforms with up to 39 calibrated parameters and a 128x128 residual terrain map, NFC achieves the most accurate posterior distributions compared to established simulation-based inference baselines. In physical outdoor experiments with a wheeled robot suffering an axle failure across snow, mud, and wet grass, NFC increases rough-terrain navigation success from 26% to 70% while reducing orientation jerk by 88%.

</details>


### [4] [Intervention-Robust VLA: Diverse Demonstrations for Robot Deployment with Safety Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28289)
*Tzu-Yuan Huang,Shao-Hsuan Chang,Dai-Jie Wu,Ralf Römer,Yung-Hui Li,Stefan Sosnowski,Angela P. Schoellig,Kenneth Marino,Sandra Hirche*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 InteR-VLA，一个面向视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）的干预鲁棒框架，利用多样化完成任务演示扩大任务相关状态覆盖，使训练分布更贴合安全过滤后的部署分布，从而在安全干预下保持任务能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接从数据中学习安全行为需要大量覆盖安全关键场景，现实采集不切实际；测试时安全过滤器虽可纠正不安全动作，但会把机器人推入训练数据覆盖不足的状态，导致任务性能下降（干预引发的分布漂移）。

Method: 在同等数据预算下，使用多样化的完成任务演示来拓宽任务相关状态覆盖，使训练分布与"经安全过滤的部署"分布更好对齐，从而缓解干预诱导的分布漂移，同时保留任务性能。在两种 VLA 模型上进行了验证。

Result: 真实世界实验涵盖分布内与分布外设置：InteR-VLA 保持安全性，将 Safe Success Rate 从名义策略的 0% 提升至最高 90%，并在两个被评估的 VLA 上持续优于名义数据训练。

Conclusion: 通过数据层面的分布对齐而非改进安全过滤器本身，可在不牺牲任务性能的前提下实现安全部署，说明训练数据多样性与部署时干预分布的匹配是提升 VLA 安全性的关键。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) have shown strong potential for learning complex robotic behaviors from demonstrations. However, learning safe behavior directly from data requires extensive coverage of safety-critical situations, which is often impractical in real-world settings. Test-time safety filters provide an alternative by correcting unsafe actions during deployment, but these interventions can drive the robot into states poorly covered by the training data, thereby degrading task performance. We introduce InteR-VLA, an intervention-robust framework for VLAs that leverages diverse task-completing demonstrations to broaden task-relevant state coverage and maintain competence under safety interventions. By better aligning the training distribution with safety-filtered deployment under the same data budget, our approach mitigates intervention-induced distribution shift while preserving task performance. Real-world experiments across in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings show that InteR-VLA maintains safety, increases Safe Success Rate up to 90% from 0% for nominal policies, and consistently outperforms nominal-data training on both evaluated VLAs.

</details>


### [5] [Learning a Food World Model That Predicts the Next State from Production Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28319)
*Inkyu Sa,Gaurav Kuppa,Steve Van der Hoeven,Konstantin Stulov,Rajat Bhageria,Luis Rayas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文利用61,773次产线抓取数据，以自监督方式学习食物抓取的世界模型，从抓取前的锅内高度图、指令动作、食物与餐具预测抓取后的高度图和抓取质量（带不确定性）。在9种食物、12,294次留出抓取上，质量预测RMSE为10.93 g，比指令目标或按食物均值低9%，比控制器内部估计低24%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 食物装配机器人每次抓取都需达到目标重量，而产线控制器缺乏对抓取结果（尤其抓取后状态）的可靠预测。已有手工特征方法只能预测质量，无法提供规划与策略学习所需的下一状态，因此需要可预测完整状态的世界模型。

Method: 从61,773次生产抓取中，借助相机与秤自动打标签（自监督学习）训练世界模型。输入为2.5 mm网格的抓取前高度图、指令抓取动作、食物种类和餐具，输出为抓取后高度图与抓取质量，并给出不确定性估计。用预测不确定性拒答最不确定的抓取，并与梯度提升树（20个手工特征，仅预测质量）对比。

Result: 质量预测RMSE为10.93 g，分别比指令目标和按食物均值低9%，比控制器内部质量估计低24%；梯度提升树RMSE再低0.2 g但只能预测质量。拒答最不确定抓取后，可对89.4%的抓取给出RMSE 10.28 g的预测。模型对指令体积与深度几乎不敏感（闭环生产中变化小），且在新目标重量上会无预警失效。

Conclusion: 从生产数据自监督学习世界模型可行且优于现有基线，能同时预测下一状态与质量，支撑规划与策略学习。未来方向包括动作基元条件化，以及跨食物、餐具的更通用模型；论文还给出了从生产数据学习世界模型的实践建议。

Abstract: Food assembly robots pick ingredients from a pan, and every pick must deliver a target weight. We learn a world model of food picking from 61,773 production picks, labeled automatically by a camera and a scale (self-supervised learning). From the pre-pick heightmap of the pan on a 2.5 mm grid, the commanded pick action, the food and the utensil, the model predicts the post-pick heightmap and the picked mass with uncertainty. We evaluate the mass prediction on 12,294 held-out picks of nine foods. The production controller keeps each pick near its commanded target. The model's root-mean-square error (RMSE) of 10.93 g is 9% lower than that of the commanded target or of a per-food mean. This RMSE is 24% lower than that of the controller's internal mass estimate. Gradient-boosted trees on 20 hand-crafted features have a 0.2 g lower RMSE, but they predict only the mass, not the next state that planning and policy learning need. Using the predicted uncertainty, we decline the most uncertain picks and answer 89.4% of picks with an RMSE of 10.28 g. The prediction barely responds to the commanded volume and depth, because they vary little in closed-loop production. The model also fails without warning at target weights not seen in training. Future directions include action-primitive conditioning and a more general model across foods and utensils. We give recommendations for learning world models from production data.

</details>


### [6] [Defending Vision-Language-Action Models against Transferable Attacks via Visual-Proprioceptive Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28346)
*Jiahao Wang,Ming F. Li,Han Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于本体感知的VLA模型防御框架，用可信本体感知状态建立空间参照，与视觉特征推断的机器人自身位置对比，检测并缓解可迁移对抗攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人任务中应用广泛，但对抗攻击具有跨任务的可迁移性，对机器人系统构成严重威胁。观察发现，尽管攻击策略不同，这些可迁移攻击存在共同后果：破坏VLA模型对机器人自身的感知。

Method: 利用机器人本体感知状态建立可信的空间参照，与由视觉特征推断出的机器人感知位置进行比较；两者间的偏差作为检测对抗攻击的可靠信号，据此进行检测与缓解。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法能有效检测可迁移对抗补丁，并提升闭环任务性能；真实机器人实验进一步验证了防御在真实任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 本体感知可作为可信锚点，用于检测和抵御VLA模型中的可迁移对抗攻击，在仿真与真实机器人场景下均有效。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong capabilities in a wide range of robotic tasks. However, adversarial attacks in VLA models have shown strong transferability across robotic tasks, posing significant threats to robotic systems. Despite their different attack strategies, we observe that these transferable attacks share a common consequence: they disrupt the VLA model's perception towards the robot itself. Motivated by this observation, we propose a proprioception-grounded defense framework that leverages trusted proprioceptive information to detect and mitigate transferable adversarial attacks. Specifically, we utilize the robot's proprioceptive states to establish a trusted spatial reference, and compare it with the robot's perceived location inferred from visual features. Discrepancies between these two sources provide a reliable signal for detecting adversarial attacks. Extensive experiments demonstrate effective detection of transferable adversarial patches and improved closed-loop performance. Real-robot experiments further demonstrate the effectiveness of our defense on real-world robotic tasks.

</details>


### [7] [A Hybrid Multi-Goal Path Planning Framework for Powered Wheelchairs in Dynamic Environments: From Simulation to Real-World Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28394)
*Damla Leblebicioğlu,Ege Özgül,Taşkın Padır*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出一种融合TSP全局任务排序、势场(PF)引导与局部RRT/A*规划的混合框架，用于自主电动轮椅在人本环境中的多目的地导航与碰撞避免。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主电动轮椅在人本环境中需同时兼顾高效的多目的地路径规划与静态/动态障碍下的持续避碰，单一规划器难以两者兼得。

Method: 用TSP确定多目的地访问顺序，PF引导将全局路径信息传递给局部规划器以保持路径一致性；局部采用方向感知、扩展邻域的A*或带环境感知采样与两步扩展的RRT；在正常、拥挤、超拥挤仿真环境中以点模型与椭圆（平台尺寸）模型对比评估，并在实体轮椅上通过动作捕捉与车载LiDAR/RGB-D进行验证。

Result: 引入轮椅几何形状会改变观测到的碰撞行为；在椭圆模型下，TSP-PF-RRT与TSP-PF-A*两种混合规划器避碰性能相当。

Conclusion: 将全局任务排序、势场引导与局部采样/图搜索结合的混合框架可支撑电动轮椅的多目标任务与实时避碰，且机器人几何建模对避碰评估结果有显著影响，应纳入规划与评价体系。

Abstract: Autonomous powered wheelchairs (PWs) operating in human-centered environments must combine efficient multi-destination path planning with continuous collision avoidance strategies in the presence of static and dynamic obstacles. This study presents a hybrid framework integrating Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP)-based global task ordering, Potential-Field (PF) guidance, and local Rapidly-exploring Random Tree (RRT) and A-star (A*) planners. PF guidance transfers global path information to local planners, helping the robot stay aligned with the global path during obstacle avoidance. Local planning is enhanced with direction-aware, extended-neighborhood A* or RRT using environment-aware sampling and two-step extension. The TSP-PF-RRT and TSP-PF-A* methods are evaluated in normal, crowded, and extra-crowded simulation environments using point-based and elliptical robot models. The elliptical model accounts for platform dimensions during collision avoidance phase. Hardware tests on the physical PW use motion capture (MoCap) for ground-truth measurements, while outside-MoCap experiments rely on onboard LiDAR and RGB-D sensing. Results show that incorporating the wheelchair geometry changes the observed collision behavior, yielding comparable collision avoidance performance between the two hybrid planners under the elliptical model.

</details>


### [8] [Occupancy Planar Patch: Incremental Probabilistic Bounded-Plane Reconstruction from LiDAR Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28403)
*Jiahao Wang,Chaehyeon Song,Dongjiao He,Jan Quenzel,Lintong Zhang,Ayoung Kim,Maurice Fallon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出占用平面片（OPP），一种在距离空间中直接建模LiDAR测量几何的增量有界平面表示，统一更新平面几何、边界与影像融合，在真实时间下实现高精度紧凑地图重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR扫描重建常借用无序点集或通用体素/面元表示，忽略了LiDAR回波沿已知射线具有各向异性距离噪声，且该射线信息隐含标定了自由空间。作者希望把传感器物理测量几何（噪声、平面不确定性、射线穿越）显式纳入重建过程，从而在噪声与遮挡条件下获得更可靠的表面判定。

Method: 提出Occupancy Planar Patch（OPP）：一种增量式有界平面片表示，在距离空间（range-space）直接评估表面似然与自由空间。以单一的“射线—面片”关联权重同时驱动三件事：平面几何更新、表面边界细化、相机影像混合。将传感器距离噪声、平面不确定性、射线净空（ray clearance）作为显式建模项加入观测模型，并以保守策略仅在几何证据确定处接纳表面。

Result: 在Oxford Spires与Multi-Campus共6个序列上评估：OPP在对比方法中取得最高精度与最低重建误差，同时存储地图属于最紧凑之列。几何重建达到实时，代表性序列每帧81 ms；纹理投影额外耗时134 ms。

Conclusion: 基于射线距离空间的观测建模能有效提升LiDAR在线重建的精度与地图紧凑度，代价是牺牲无约束完整性：OPP以结构精度优先，适合建筑建模等对几何保真度要求高的场景，但可能漏检证据不足的表面区域。

Abstract: Online surface reconstruction from LiDAR scans requires observation models that respect the sensor's physical measurement geometry. Unlike unordered point sets, LiDAR returns carry anisotropic range noise along known rays, which also implicitly identify free space. We introduce the Occupancy Planar Patch (OPP), an incremental bounded planar patch representation that evaluates surface likelihoods and free space directly in range-space, allowing sensor noise, plane uncertainty, and ray clearance to be explicitly modelled during reconstruction. A single ray-to-patch association weight jointly updates plane geometry, refines surface boundaries, and blends camera imagery. The method is evaluated using six sequences from the Oxford Spires and Multi-Campus datasets. OPP achieves the highest precision and lowest reconstruction error among evaluated methods, while producing stored maps among the most compact. By conservatively admitting surfaces only where geometric evidence is certain, OPP prioritises structural precision over unconstrained completeness, which is well suited for architectural modelling. Geometric reconstruction operates in real time, requiring 81 ms per frame on a representative sequence, with texture projection adding 134 ms.

</details>


### [9] [ScaleEvals: Scaling Generalist Policy Evaluation through Reset-Free Autonomous Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28411)
*Amitoj Sandhu,Ethan Sick,Omkaar Buddhikot,Nadia Laswi,Wesley Durbano,Nadun Ranawaka,You Liang Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ScaleEvals，一种基于机器人集群"连续物理玩耍"的无重置评测协议，用 VLM 自动生成任务与自动评分，结合 soft-win Bradley-Terry 聚合与标签分层 ANOVA 诊断，在 DROID 和 YAM 上以极低人力成本实现对通用机器人策略的可扩展、可解释评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界中对通用机器人策略的评测受三大瓶颈制约：需要人工手动重置环境、依赖人工打分、任务集小而固定导致诊断覆盖不足。这些限制使评测难以规模化，也无法细粒度地说明策略"强在哪、弱在哪"。

Method: 1) 无重置协议：在机器人集群上通过持续"物理玩耍"采集 rollout，避免逐回合人工重置；2) VLM AutoPrompter：生成场景接地任务，附带难度估计、语言指令与技能标签；3) VLM AutoGrader：判定任务进度（与人类评分者一致率 90.7%）；4) 统计层：用 soft-win Bradley-Terry 模型聚合比分，并用按标签分层的 ANOVA 给出可解释的能力诊断；5) 行为组合性分析：重组动词/技能/物体条件下的证据以预测未见提示上的策略排序。

Result: 在 DROID 与 YAM 上完成 3000+ 次物理自主 rollout，单站达 23.0 次净 rollout/小时，主动人力投入降低 98%。对五个 SOTA 通用策略排序时，与 DROID 上的留出人工评测排名高度相关（r=0.94，rho=0.90，p=0.018），在第二种本体 YAM 上内部一致性良好（r=0.839，rho=0.80）。在 10 个任务、240 次前瞻性物理 rollout 中，恢复三策略排序的成对一致度达 96%。

Conclusion: ScaleEvals 说明通用机器人策略的评测可以在几乎不依赖人工的情况下实现规模化，并且不仅能预测"哪个策略领先"，还能预测"排序在何处反转"，即提供可解释、可预测细粒度能力差异的评测信号。

Abstract: Real-world evaluation of generalist robot policies is bottlenecked by manual resets, human grading, and small fixed task suites with limited diagnostic coverage. We present ScaleEvals, a scalable reset-free protocol that evaluates policies through continuous physical play across a robot fleet. A VLM AutoPrompter generates scene-grounded tasks with difficulty estimates, language prompts, and skill tags, enabling performance analysis across diverse rollouts. A VLM AutoGrader assesses task progress, achieving 90.7% agreement with human graders. Scores are aggregated via a soft-win Bradley-Terry model, while tag-stratified ANOVA provides interpretable capability diagnostics. Across 3,000+ physical autonomous rollouts on DROID and YAM, ScaleEvals achieves 23.0 net rollouts/hr per station while reducing active human labor by 98%. By ranking five SOTA generalist policies, ScaleEvals correlates with held-out manual evaluation ranking on DROID (r=0.94, rho=0.90, p=0.018), and remains internally consistent on a second embodiment, YAM (r=0.839, rho=0.80). Beyond aggregate rankings, we observe behavioral compositionality: recombining verb-, skill-, and object-conditioned evidence predicts policy order on unseen prompts. Across 10 tasks and 240 prospective physical rollouts, we recover three-policy orderings of 96% pairwise concordance, demonstrating ScaleEvals can predict not just which policy leads, but where rankings reverse.

</details>


### [10] [Belief-Driven Quantum Magnetometer Control for GPS-Denied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28416)
*Murtuza Lemonwala,Laxima Niure Kandel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出"信念驱动传感"闭环规则，用滤波器预测的磁场分布实时设定Ramsey干涉磁力仪的相位与 interrogation time，在真实飞行数据上显著降低磁异常导航定位误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型无人机可携带的低SWaP量子磁力仪灵敏度弱，且现有系统以固定参数开环运行；干涉式磁力仪存在"长询问时间更灵敏但更易相位模糊"的权衡，需要根据导航滤波器自身的位置信念来自适应调节，而非固定设置。

Method: 将导航滤波器的预测磁场分布通过异常图映射，得到各历元的期望场与不确定性；据此用闭式规则在"模糊极限"与"退相干极限"之间选取Ramsey相位和询问时间，形成感知—滤波闭环；在美国空军真实飞行数据（真实轨迹、惯导漂移、实测场、飞行18个月后使用的测绘图）上只对Ramsey传感器建模，与多组固定设置基线对比，并做消融实验（分离相位对齐与询问时间自适应）；另在IBM超导量子比特上验证询问时间规则接近经验最优，并与信息感知路径规划结合评估任务误差。

Result: 72个独立飞行窗口中有63个优于固定设置基线（p=2×10⁻¹¹）；低信噪比传感器误差下降20–27%，中等对比度传感器下降30–44%；消融显示稳态飞行增益主要来自相位对齐，且规则无需事后调参即可匹配最佳固定设置；丢失初始对准后，询问时间自适应使中位误差再降37–42%；对高质量传感器与精确地图增益较小；在IBM超导量子比特上询问时间处于经验最优的1.3倍以内；与信息感知路由结合使任务误差降低15–40%。

Conclusion: 将传感器参数设置纳入导航滤波信念闭环是低成本量子磁异常导航的有效增益来源，尤其对弱/中等传感器与初始失锁场景；高精度传感器和高质量地图下收益有限。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles increasingly need to navigate without satellite positioning. Magnetic anomaly navigation (Mag-Nav) with quantum magnetometers offers a passive, unjammable fix, but the low size-weight-and-power (SWaP) sensors that small drones can carry are weak, and they are operated open loop at a fixed setting. Interferometric (Ramsey) magnetometers face a trade-off: long interrogation times are more sensitive but become ambiguous when the expected field is uncertain. The navigator's own position belief, pushed through the anomaly map, predicts that field. We close this loop. The filter's predictive field distribution sets the Ramsey phase and interrogation time at every epoch, through a closed-form rule that moves between an ambiguity limit and a decoherence limit. We evaluate it on real U.S. Air Force flight data (real tracks, inertial drift, measured fields, and a survey map tested 18 months after it was flown), with only the Ramsey sensor modelled. Against fixed-setting baselines, 63 of 72 independent flight windows favour belief driven sensing ($p = 2 \times 10^{-11}$). Error falls by 20-27% for low-SNR sensors and 30-44% for mid-contrast sensors. An ablation separates the two ingredients. Phase alignment drives the gain in steady flight, and the rule matches the best fixed setting without the hindsight tuning that setting requires. After a lost start, interrogation time adaptation is essential, cutting median error by 37-42% even against hindsight-tuned phase-aligned settings. Gains are small for high-quality sensors with accurate maps. On an IBM superconducting qubit, the rule's interrogation time stays within $1.3\times$ of the empirical optimum. Coupled with information-aware routing, belief-driven sensing lowers mission error by 15-40%.

</details>


### [11] [LoKiTO: Local Kinodynamic Trajectory Optimization for UAVs based on Memory-Efficient Neural Distance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28438)
*Guillermo Gil-Garcia,J. A. Cobano,L. Merino,F. Caballero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 LoKiTO——一种结合 Chebyshev 多项式参数化与在线神经 ESDF 的 3D 局部动力学轨迹优化器，用于空中机器人，在拥挤环境中实现超过 99.4% 的动力学可行性并显著降低地图内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于梯度的规划器难以兼顾两方面：显式体积地图内存开销大；且往往无法严格满足动力学约束（动态极限）。因此需要一种内存高效、且能显式处理动力学可行性的局部重规划方法。

Method: 1）采用 Chebyshev 多项式参数化轨迹，利用其数值条件良好的特性；2）将动力学约束直接嵌入模块化代价函数；3）碰撞感知依赖由 LiDAR 测量在线更新的连续神经 ESDF 表示，替代显式体积地图。

Result: 地图内存消耗降至约 1.0 MB；在高杂乱仿真环境中与 Fast-Planner、EGO-Planner 对比，动力学可行性达标率超过 99.4%，显著优于基线，并保持更高的障碍物中位间隙。

Conclusion: 将良态连续参数化与显式动力学推理相结合，使 LoKiTO 成为空中机器人局部重规划的一种实用且可扩展的基础框架。

Abstract: This paper introduces LoKiTO, a 3D local kinodynamic trajectory optimizer that combines a Chebyshev polynomial parameterization with an online neural Euclidean Signed Distance Field (ESDF) representation for aerial robotics. While current gradient-based planners often struggle with the high memory requirements of explicit volumetric maps or fail to strictly enforce dynamic limits, LoKiTO embeds kinodynamic constraints directly into a modular cost function and exploits the numerical conditioning properties of the Chebyshev basis. Collision awareness is provided by a continuous, memory-efficient neural ESDF updated online from LiDAR measurements, which drastically reduces map memory consumption to approximately 1.0 MB. Benchmarking in highly cluttered simulated environments against state-of-the-art baselines (Fast-Planner and EGO-Planner) demonstrates the superiority of our approach. Experimental results show that LoKiTO achieves over 99.4% dynamic feasibility compliance, significantly outperforming the baselines, and maintaining higher median clearance from obstacles. By coupling a well-conditioned continuous parameterization with explicit kinodynamic reasoning, LoKiTO provides a practical and extensible basis for local replanning in aerial robotics.

</details>


### [12] [AlpaSafe: Risk-Aware Trajectory Selection for Vision-Language-Action Models in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28509)
*Qiren Wang,Mingyu Guo,Weisong Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 AlpaSafe，一个风险感知的轨迹选择器，复用冻结的驾驶 VLA 在提议候选轨迹时已计算出的场景表示，无需额外 rollout 或 VLA 训练，即可在候选轨迹中选出风险更低的一条。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于推理的驾驶 VLA 每次决策生成多条候选轨迹但只执行一条。标准评估按"最优候选"打分，隐含了一个部署中并不存在的 oracle 选择器，且被执行候选的风险未被考察。现有在线评估器通常需要专门训练的场景/世界模型，并在推理时对每个候选做 rollout，代价高。

Method: AlpaSafe 读取 VLA prompt prefill 的最后一个隐藏 token，用一个轻量头（仅 1.7M 参数）将该 token 与每条候选轨迹融合，预测相对分数以及碰撞/越界两个风险 logits。训练监督来自候选轨迹的闭环仿真结果。推理时用固定规则剔除在两个风险 logits 上被支配的候选，再在剩余候选中选分数最高者。

Result: 在 525 个 AlpaSim 场景上，AlpaSafe 在选择遗憾值和 top-1 一致率上均优于 Alpamayo 默认的首样本规则与均匀随机选择；将有责碰撞或越界率从 6.41% 和 6.10% 降至 4.93%。每决策耗时 0.75 ms。

Conclusion: 复用 VLA 已有的场景表示即可实现高效、低开销的风险感知轨迹选择，在不训练 VLA、不增加推理 rollout 的前提下显著降低执行轨迹的风险，代价仅为极小参数与亚毫秒级延迟。

Abstract: Reasoning-based driving vision-language-action (VLA) models propose multiple candidate trajectories at each decision, yet only one is executed. Standard evaluation scores the best candidate, assuming an oracle selector that deployment lacks, and leaves the risk of the executed candidate unexamined. Existing online evaluators typically close this gap with a purpose-trained scene or world model, often rolling out each candidate through it at inference time. We introduce AlpaSafe, a risk-aware trajectory selector that reuses the scene representation a frozen driving VLA computes while proposing its candidates. AlpaSafe reads the last hidden token of the VLA's prompt prefill. A lightweight head, trained on the closed-loop simulation outcomes of the candidates, fuses this token with each candidate trajectory and predicts a relative score and collision/offroad logits. A fixed rule removes candidates dominated on the two risk logits and selects the highest-scoring candidate among those remaining, adding neither rollouts nor VLA training. Experiments on 525 AlpaSim scenes show that AlpaSafe outperforms Alpamayo's default first-sample rule and uniform random selection in selected regret and top-1 agreement, and reduces the collision-at-fault or offroad rate from 6.41% and 6.10% to 4.93%. The selector has 1.7M parameters and takes 0.75 ms per decision.

</details>


### [13] [Lifted Multiple Shooting for Parallel-in-Time Contact-Rich Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28512)
*Wei-Chen Li,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出"提升多重打靶法"（lifted multiple shooting），将接触冲量作为优化变量引入轨迹优化转录，使接触一致性与状态转移约束解耦，可直接用非线性规划求解器求解；并提出时间并行原始-对偶内点求解器 IP-PAS，实现 O(log T) 并行深度，在六个动态接触密集任务上显著提升成功率与速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的机器人规划需考虑机器人与环境的耦合动力学（惯性、接触力、摩擦），但接触动力学的非光滑性、多模态性以及接触几何使基于梯度的优化难以奏效；同时长时域与高维系统下的规划效率也是瓶颈。

Method: 1）lifted multiple shooting：把接触冲量提升为独立优化变量，使优化器可用满足摩擦锥可行性的冲量满足状态转移约束，而接触模型一致性作为单独约束施加；支持广泛的接触本构关系，灵敏度通过隐式微分计算。2）IP-PAS：一种时间并行的原始-对偶内点法求解器，达到 O(log T) 的并行计算深度，以支持长时域与高维系统的规划。

Result: 在六个全动态、接触密集的规划任务（包括非抓取推动与灵巧手中操作）上评估，且不使用接触引导代价项：相比标准多重打靶法成功率最多提升 81.5 个百分点；IP-PAS 的解质量与串行方法相当，最多获得 3.1 倍加速，并可扩展到 40,000 时间步的时域。

Conclusion: 通过将接触冲量提升为优化变量并配合时间并行内点求解器，可在无需接触引导项的情况下稳健、高效地求解接触丰富的轨迹优化问题，兼顾鲁棒性与长时域可扩展性。

Abstract: Planning through contact requires accounting for the coupled dynamics of a robot and its environment, including inertial effects, contact forces, and friction. However, the non-smoothness and multimodality of contact dynamics and contact geometry makes gradient-based optimization challenging. In this work, we propose lifted multiple shooting, a trajectory optimization transcription that can be solved directly using nonlinear program solvers. By introducing contact impulses as optimization variables, our formulation allows the optimizer to satisfy the state-transition constraints using friction-cone-feasible impulses while separately enforcing their consistency with the contact model. Our formulation supports a broad class of contact constitutive relations, with sensitivities computed through implicit differentiation. To enable efficient planning over long horizons and for high-dimensional systems, we further introduce IP-PAS, a parallel-in-time primal-dual interior-point solver that achieves O(log T) parallel computational depth. We evaluate the proposed methods on six fully dynamic contact-rich planning tasks, including nonprehensile pushing and in-hand manipulation, without contact-guiding costs. Lifted multiple shooting improves success rates by up to 81.5 percentage points over standard multiple shooting, while IP-PAS achieves solution quality comparable to serial methods with up to a 3.1x speedup and scales to horizons of 40,000 timesteps.

</details>


### [14] [Environment-Aware Probabilistic State Estimation of Continuum Robots from Planar Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28521)
*Aedan Mangan,Spencer Teetaert,Chengnan Shentu,Michael G. Brandel,Jeremy J. Heit,Alexander Norbash,Timothy D. Barfoot,Nikolay Atanasov,Tania K. Morimoto,Jessica Burgner-Kahrs*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种概率状态估计器，将已知环境表示为符号距离场并融合异步测量与运动相机图像，实现无内置传感器的连续体机器人全状态连续估计，在仿真与实物中显著降低末端误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人整体柔性、在受限环境中不可避免与周围结构接触，导致建模远比刚性机器人复杂，其状态往往只能依靠运行过程中的信息来估计；同时许多应用（如医学术前影像、工业检测设计模型）中环境是先验已知的，这一信息尚未被充分利用。

Method: 构建概率状态估计器：把已知环境编码为符号距离场（SDF）以约束/校正估计形状；估计量在弧长与时间上连续，可融合机器人任意位置上的异步测量；并融合来自移动相机的机体固定标记平面图像与指令驱动量，使机器人无需任何嵌入式传感器。

Result: 在仿真主动脉弓与硬件体模实验中，估计器成功跟踪机器人进入目标分支；仅用单视角时，借助已知环境将末端误差分别由机器人长度的2.5%降至1.6%、由20.0%降至11.3%。

Conclusion: 连续的全状态及其不确定性估计可为无传感器连续体机器人的导航与控制提供支持，说明利用先验已知环境信息可有效弥补柔性建模与传感的不足。

Abstract: Continuum robots are compliant along their entire length, making them well suited to navigating confined environments where contact with surrounding structures is often unavoidable. However, this compliance makes their modeling more complex compared to rigid robots, especially under contact, so their state must often be estimated from information available during operation. In many applications, the environment is known in advance, for example from preoperative imaging in medicine or from design models in industrial inspection. We present a probabilistic state estimator that represents the known environment as a signed distance field, informing the estimated shape. Continuous in arclength and time, it estimates the full state from asynchronous measurements anywhere along the robot. It also fuses planar images of body-fixed markers from a moving camera with commanded actuation, so the robot needs no embedded sensors. In a simulated aortic arch and a hardware phantom, the estimator tracked the robot into the target branch, and with a single view, using the known environment lowered the tip error from 2.5% to 1.6% and from 20.0% to 11.3% of the robot length, respectively. The continuous estimate of the full state and its uncertainty can support navigation and control of sensorless continuum robots.

</details>


### [15] [SBTenv: A Literature-Validated Split-Belt Treadmill Simulation Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28547)
*Philip Byrne,John Drazan,Nidhi Seethapathi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并发布开源的分带跑步机环境 SBTenv（基于 MuJoCo），并通过无轮圈轮（rimless wheel）的被动行走实验数据验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分带跑步机是研究人类运动适应的关键工具，物理仿真可互补实验并直接检验适应机制假说；随着模型复杂度上升，高通量仿真需求增加，但 MuJoCo 中尚缺乏开源的分带跑步机环境。

Method: 在 MuJoCo 中构建 SBTenv 分带跑步机环境，对轮辐端点建模柔性（compliant）接触，并以已发表的无轮圈轮在分带跑步机上被动行走的实验数据为基准进行验证。

Result: 柔性接触建模复现了实验的平均稳态速度（R²=0.643，RMSE=0.0162 m/s），且无需以往仿真中所用的经验性几何修正。

Conclusion: SBTenv 作为公开发布的开源环境，有望支持分带跑步机上肌肉骨骼与机器人运动的高通量仿真研究。

Abstract: The split-belt treadmill is an important tool for studying human locomotor adaptation. Physics-based simulations complement such experiments by allowing hypotheses about the mechanisms of adaptation to be tested directly, and high-throughput simulation is increasingly needed as these models grow in complexity. However, no open-source split-belt treadmill environment exists in MuJoCo, a physics engine widely used for high-throughput simulation. Here, we present SBTenv, a split-belt treadmill environment built in MuJoCo and validated against published experimental data from a rimless wheel walking passively on a split-belt treadmill. By modeling compliant contacts at the spoke tips, SBTenv reproduces the experimental average steady velocity ($R^2=0.643$, RMSE $=0.0162$ m/s) without the empirical geometric correction required in prior simulations. We expect SBTenv, openly shared, to enable high-throughput simulation of musculoskeletal and robotic locomotion on a split-belt treadmill.

</details>


### [16] [PhysPPF: Learning Robotic Manipulation with Past, Present, and Future Physical Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28551)
*Junjie Li,Dawei Yan,Guoting Wei,Ruike Song,Lu Wang,Qingguo Chen,Zhenbo Luo,Haokui Zhang,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PhysPPF，在紧凑的物理token空间内统一进行时空建模的VLA框架，通过WWH空间蒸馏与PPF时序世界模型，在SimplerEnv、CALVIN、LIBERO上显著超越现有空间/历史/世界模型类VLA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂操作任务需同时具备精确3D接触感知与长程时序协调，但现有VLA研究多将空间增强与时序推理割裂：空间方法只向当前帧注入几何或可供性先验、不跟踪多步状态转移；历史感知与世界模型类VLA多在2D特征或像素空间建模，缺乏显式3D接触线索或生成开销高。

Method: 在紧凑物理token空间做统一时空建模。空间上，WWH（What-Where-How）仅在训练时从预训练感知教师蒸馏，形成三组紧凑token，分别编码场景中的3D结构、交互位置与夹爪接近方式，测试时无需3D编码器。时序上，PPF（Past-Present-Future）直接在该WWH空间运行、不做像素重建，将已执行状态压缩为有界的过去物理记忆，并用非对称世界模型联合推断当前与未来物理token。随后用并行门控分支将过去、现在、未来表征注入流匹配动作专家，避免跨时序干扰。

Result: 在SimplerEnv、CALVIN、LIBERO上的大量实验显示，PhysPPF以适度延迟与参数开销稳定优于现有空间类、历史感知类与世界模型类VLA，相较StarVLA基线分别提升+17.2（SimplerEnv）、+29.3（CALVIN）、+3.5（LIBERO）个点。

Conclusion: 在紧凑物理token空间统一时空建模是可行且高效的路线：免测试时3D编码器的空间蒸馏加上无像素重建的轻量世界模型，能同时获得3D接触感知与长程协调能力，且延迟与参数开销可控。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have advanced robotic manipulation, yet complex tasks requiring both precise 3D contact and long-horizon coordination remain challenging. Most existing studies enhance spatial perception or temporal reasoning in VLAs separately. Spatial methods primarily inject geometric or affordance priors into current-frame perception without tracking multi-step transitions, while history-aware and world-model VLAs typically capture past context or future dynamics in 2D feature or pixel spaces, either lacking explicit 3D contact cues or incurring high generation overhead. To address this, we propose PhysPPF, a VLA framework that performs unified spatiotemporal modeling in a compact physical token space. Spatially, WWH (What-Where-How) distills pretrained perception teachers exclusively during training into compact token groups capturing what 3D structures occupy the scene, where to interact, and how the gripper should approach, without test-time 3D encoders. Temporally, operating directly in this WWH space without pixel reconstruction, PPF (Past-Present-Future) consolidates executed states into a bounded past physical memory and jointly infers present and future physical tokens via an asymmetric world model. Parallel gated branches then inject these past, present, and future representations into a flow-matching action expert to guide manipulation without cross-temporal interference. Extensive experiments on SimplerEnv, CALVIN, and LIBERO show that PhysPPF consistently outperforms existing spatial, history-aware, and world-model VLAs with modest latency and parameter overhead, improving over the StarVLA baseline by +17.2 points on SimplerEnv, +29.3 points on CALVIN, and +3.5 points on LIBERO.

</details>


### [17] [Selective ROM Weight Storage for Responsive LLM Control on Edge Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28563)
*Boxiang Song,Zhihong Chen,Xiaoyu Hao,Jinhong Huang,Chengkai Zhu,Jiaqi Yang,Hongyang Pan,Changhao Yan,Keren Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出异构内存架构与调度协同设计，缓解边缘机器人上LLM推理的内存、带宽与实时控制瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘机器人需同时运行LLM推理与实时控制，但内存容量有限、解码期权重反复搬运挤占激活与KV cache的外部带宽，逐步生成动作还导致决策延迟并干扰共享硬件上的周期控制。

Method: 采用分体ROM存放部分固定权重并经专用数据通路供给标准计算阵列，LPDDR保存其余权重与可变状态以保持灵活性；通过协同操作数供给与输出切片边界处的抢占实现推理加速且降低对周期控制的干扰；并设计结构化决策接口，在单次前向中为候选阶段打分，避免常规选择时的顺序token生成。

Result: 基于Qwen3-8B的实验显示，峰值解码加速1.56倍，P99任务响应时间降低约227.27倍，测试的抓取与搬运任务完成率达100.00%。

Conclusion: 内存、计算访问与决策执行三方面的协同优化可有效支撑边缘机器人LLM工作负载的算力效率与实时性需求。

Abstract: Edge robots increasingly use large language models (LLMs) for complex tasks, demanding efficient inference alongside timely control. Limited memory capacity constrains model deployment, while repeated weight transfers during decoding compete with activations and KV cache for external bandwidth. Sequential generation of robot actions further delays decisions and periodic control on shared hardware. We present a hybrid memory architecture with heterogeneous scheduling to address these bottlenecks jointly. Banked ROM stores selected fixed weights and supplies a standard compute array through a dedicated datapath. LPDDR retains the remaining weights and mutable state, preserving model flexibility. Coordinated operand delivery and preemption at output slice boundaries accelerate inference while limiting interference with periodic control. A structured decision interface scores candidate phases in a single forward pass, avoiding sequential token generation for routine choices. Together, these mechanisms improve memory service, compute access, and decision execution for edge robotic workloads. Experiments with Qwen3-8B achieve a peak decode speedup of 1.56$\times$ and reduce P99 task response time by a factor of approximately 227.27$\times$, while completing 100.00\% of the tested grasping and transport tasks.

</details>


### [18] [AsyncMixVLA: Runtime Asynchronous Robot Policy Switching for Adaptation to Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28585)
*Tamoghno Das,Keyon Jazayeri,Yerin Cho,Nazmul Takbir,Arshia Aravinthan,Nikil Dutt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AsyncMixVLA，一种面向异构VLA策略的异步主动切换推理框架，通过学到的触发器平衡恢复收益与干预风险，并将高算力策略的推理与已提交动作重叠、对齐到预期接管状态，从而在扰动下提升成功率并大幅降低交接延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型将视觉观测与语言指令映射为机器人动作，但执行中的物体移动等环境扰动会破坏其可靠性；不同策略在rollout中期扰动下呈现互补失败，因此需要选择性切换策略以实现恢复。然而切换会带来两类问题：同步等待新策略推理使机器人停滞，异步执行又会基于过期观测行动。

Method: 设计自适应推理框架AsyncMixVLA：1）学习一个触发器，在“恢复潜力”与“干预危害”之间权衡，选择性升级到更高算力的VLA策略；2）将高算力策略的推理与已提交的后续动作并行重叠，避免停机；3）将策略上下文对齐到预期接管时刻的状态，避免基于陈旧观测决策。整体实现了跨异构策略的主动异步切换。

Result: 在带mid-rollout扰动的LIBERO-Long上，扰动任务成功率由轻量策略的69.5%提升至77.1%；总体成功率84.9%，与同步切换的84.5%相当，而暴露的中位交接延迟从226.5 ms降至0.1 ms。在无需重训练或重校准的情况下，在三个未见扰动族上取得75.1%成功率，高于较强组成策略的71.3%。

Conclusion: 异步、主动的策略切换可在不牺牲成功率的前提下近乎消除交接延迟，并具备对未见扰动的泛化能力，说明“触发式升级+推理重叠+状态对齐”是提升VLA鲁棒性的有效且可即插即用的方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models translate visual observations and language instructions into robot actions, enabling general-purpose robotic control. However, environmental perturbations, such as objects moving during execution, can undermine their reliability. Different policies exhibit complementary failures under mid-rollout perturbations, motivating selective switching between policies for recovery. Such switching can stall the robot during inference of the newly invoked policy or, if performed asynchronously, act on stale observations. We present AsyncMixVLA, an adaptive inference framework for proactive, asynchronous switching across heterogeneous VLA policies. A learned trigger balances recovery potential against intervention harm to selectively escalate to a higher-compute policy. AsyncMixVLA overlaps its inference with committed outgoing actions and aligns its context to the anticipated takeover state. On LIBERO-Long with mid-rollout perturbations, AsyncMixVLA improves perturbed task success from 69.5% to 77.1% over the lightweight policy. It achieves 84.9% overall success, comparable to 84.5% for synchronous switching, while reducing median exposed handoff latency from 226.5 ms to 0.1 ms. Without retraining or recalibration, it achieves 75.1% success across three unseen perturbation families, compared with 71.3% for the stronger constituent policy in aggregate.

</details>


### [19] [Fast World Planner: Advantage-Aware Action-Chunk Policy for Online Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28616)
*Jiangtao Kong,Yuchen Wang,Yandong Luo,Rongchao Xu,Guang Wang,Yanbing Mao,Lu Gan,Tianyi Zhou,Huajie Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SWAN 提出一种优势感知的 MPPI 类基于模型的强化学习框架，通过混合因子分析器动作块策略显式表示完整的 H 步规划提案，并用优势引导的提案优化改进目标，在 31 个高维连续控制任务上以少 50% 迭代次数取得更优性能并实现真机零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPPI 是 MBRL 在线规划的核心，但迭代优化带来规划延迟；减少迭代次数会降低性能，因此需要从规划器学习更强的策略引导。然而现有方法多采用动作级对齐，无法直接刻画完整的 H 步规划提案；且策略模仿之后进一步的性能提升仍依赖在线优化。

Method: 提出 SWAN 框架：(1) 提案表示上，将多模态 MPPI 扩展 k-MPPI 与混合因子分析器（MFA）动作块策略结合，建模多模态及跨时间、跨动作维度的相关性；(2) 提案优化上，提出优势引导的提案优化（APO），复用 MPPI 的评估结果，用优势估计构造改进后的目标，在保持与规划器对齐的同时训练策略朝该目标靠近。

Result: 在 DMControl、HumanoidBench、MyoSuite 的 31 个高维连续控制任务上，SWAN 显著提升控制性能与样本效率，且相比先进规划类 MBRL 方法少用 50% 的 MPPI 细化迭代次数；在实体 Unitree Go2 上实现零样本 sim-to-real 迁移，完成高频、稳定的速度跟踪运动控制。

Conclusion: 显式表示并优化完整 H 步规划提案、结合优势引导目标，是提升 MPPI 类 MBRL 策略引导质量与在线规划效率的有效途径，并具备实际机器人部署可行性。

Abstract: Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control is central to online planning for model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL), but its iterative refinement incurs planning latency. Reducing refinement iterations often degrades performance, motivating stronger policy guidance learned from the planner. However, existing methods typically learn from the planner through action-level alignment, which does not directly capture the complete $H$-step planner proposal. Moreover, planner imitation leaves further target improvement to online refinement. To address these limitations, we propose FaSt World PlANner (SWAN), an advantage-aware MPPI-based MBRL framework that explicitly represents and improves the complete $H$-step planner proposal to improve policy guidance for efficient online planning. For proposal representation, SWAN combines k-MPPI, a multimodal MPPI extension, with a mixture of factor analyzers (MFA) action-chunk policy to capture multiple modes and correlations across time and action dimensions. For proposal optimization, we introduce Advantage-Guided Proposal Optimization (APO), which reuses MPPI evaluations to construct an improved target using advantage estimates and trains the policy toward this target while preserving planner alignment. Experiments across 31 high-dimensional continuous-control tasks from DMControl, HumanoidBench, and MyoSuite show that SWAN substantially improves control performance and sample efficiency while using 50% fewer MPPI refinement iterations than state-of-the-art planning-based MBRL methods. Finally, SWAN demonstrates zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on a physical Unitree Go2, achieving stable velocity-tracking locomotion with high-frequency control. (see our demo at https://swanfastworldplanner.github.io/)

</details>


### [20] [Doing More with Less: Complex Manipulation with a Contact-Adaptive Robotic Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28675)
*Hyung Gon Shin,Sanghoon Jung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种具备接触自适应手指运动、细长手指、大抓取范围与持续抓握力的仿人机器人手，并配合基于四元数摆动-扭转分解的动作手套与 forearm 接口，实现低自由度、无触觉传感条件下的复杂操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿人机械手在形态、自由度、力输出、耐久性与感知能力之间存在固有权衡，难以同时满足；作者希望用机构设计化解这些权衡，并让人机接口更自然地传递人类操作意图。

Method: 设计具有接触自适应手指运动的机械手机构（细长手指、宽抓取范围、可持续抓握力）；提出基于四元数 swing-twist 分解的动作手套，用于提取人类操作意图并映射为机械手指令；并设计前臂佩戴式接口以增强操作者的具身感。

Result: 实验演示表明，在主动自由度有限且无触觉传感的条件下，该机械手结合所提机构与有效的双臂协调，可完成智能手机取放与充电、手内操作、物体开箱与重新包装、以及剪切并系绳等复杂任务。

Conclusion: 通过机构设计、意图映射接口与双臂协调的结合，可以在简化机械手硬件（低自由度、无触觉）的前提下实现较复杂的日常操作任务，为实用化仿人机械手提供可行路径。

Abstract: Anthropomorphic robotic hands offer the potential to manipulate objects designed for human hands in environments adapted to human use. However, developing a robotic hand that replicates the human hand in terms of morphology, degrees of freedom, force output, durability, and sensing capabilities remains a formidable challenge, as these requirements inherently involve trade-offs. Here, we propose a robotic hand mechanism designed to address several of these trade-offs by enabling contact-adaptive finger motion, slender fingers, a wide grasping range, and sustained gripping force. We also introduce a motion glove based on quaternion swing-twist decomposition to extract human manipulation intentions and translate them into robotic hand commands, along with a forearm-mounted interface designed to enhance the operator's sense of embodiment. Experimental demonstrations show that a robotic hand with limited active degrees of freedom and no tactile sensing can perform complex tasks, including smartphone pickup and charging, in-hand manipulation, object unboxing and repackaging, and cutting and tying cords, through the combination of the proposed mechanism and effective bimanual coordination.

</details>


### [21] [Distilling Touch into Vision-Only Policies for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28697)
*Chen Yang,Linhao Wang,Linzhe Shi,Guohui Zhang,Changjie Wu,Hang Zhang,Haoyu Li,Mu Xu,Jiansheng Fan,Chen Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacitTouch 是一种从视觉-触觉教师策略向纯视觉学生策略蒸馏的框架，让学生"像能感知触觉一样行动"，而无需重建触觉观测，部署时仅保留视觉策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉反馈有利于接触密集型操作，但部署触觉传感器会带来额外硬件与维护成本。现有触觉想象方法（如 TacImag）虽去除了硬件依赖，却保留了触觉观测的重建阶段，其误差会传导到动作生成中。

Method: 提出 TacitTouch 蒸馏框架：核心思路由"学习想象触觉感受"转变为"学习表现得像能感知触觉"。冻结的视觉-触觉教师在同一带噪动作与流时间下，监督学生去噪预测及中间动作表征；训练同时使用示范动作监督；部署时仅保留视觉策略，不含触觉编码器、专家或生成器。

Result: 在 4 个仿真 UniVTAC 任务和 4 个真实任务上，TacitTouch 相比纯视觉控制平均成功率分别绝对提升 16.0% 和 22.5%；与使用实测触觉输入的教师相比，绝对差距仅为 2.0% 和 3.75%。鲁棒性测试中优于 TacImag，平均成功率最高绝对增益达 20.0%。

Conclusion: 绕过触觉观测重建、直接在动作层面蒸馏触觉引导行为，可在部署阶段完全移除触觉硬件依赖，同时保持接近触觉教师的性能并优于触觉想象方法。

Abstract: Tactile feedback benefits contact-rich manipulation, yet deploying tactile sensors introduces additional hardware and maintenance requirements. Tactile experience can still provide effective supervision for policies that operate without tactile sensors at deployment. Tactile imagination methods such as TacImag remove this hardware dependency by predicting tactile observations, but retain a reconstruction stage whose errors can propagate into action generation. We present TacitTouch, a distillation framework that transfers tactile-guided behavior directly into a vision-only policy. The core shift is from "learn to imagine what touch would feel like" to "learn to behave as if you could feel touch": the student directly learns tactile-guided action patterns without reconstructing tactile observations. A frozen visuotactile teacher supervises the student's denoising predictions and intermediate action representations at the same noisy action and flow time. Training also uses demonstrated-action supervision. Deployment retains only the visual policy, with no tactile encoder, expert, or generator. Across four simulated UniVTAC tasks and four real-world tasks, TacitTouch achieves absolute improvements of 16.0% and 22.5% in mean success rate over vision-only control, respectively. The absolute gaps to the teacher using measured tactile input are only 2.0% and 3.75%, respectively. In robustness tests, it also outperforms TacImag, with absolute gains of up to 20.0% in mean success rate. Project page: https://pier-lab.github.io/tacittouch/

</details>


### [22] [IRIS: Inverse Dynamics from Random Exploration with Eye-in-hand Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28707)
*Xiaoxiong Zhang,Zhiqiang Zhou,Tze Ho Elden Tse,Linfang Zheng,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IRIS 是一个基于视频的机器人操作框架，核心是学习一个高泛化、执行感知的逆动力学模型（IDM）：用任务无关的随机探索数据预训练 IDM 以扩大动作接地规模，并通过两阶段训练引入眼在手上（eye-in-hand）感知，从而无需眼在手上的视频规划。仿真与真机实验表明该方法提升了动作接地与数据效率、实现更精细执行，并与当前先进策略性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "先想象后执行"范式（视频规划器预测未来视觉子目标 + IDM 将其落地为动作）存在两大瓶颈：一是 IDM 通常只在少量任务特定示范上训练，即使给定理想视觉子目标泛化也很差，说明动作接地本身是独立于视觉规划误差的瓶颈；二是精细执行受益于眼在手上观测，但眼在手上视频规划既难学又难扩展（视角随机器人移动、此类视频在互联网尺度上稀缺）。

Method: 提出 IRIS 框架：1）在任务无关的随机探索数据上预训练 IDM，获得多样的"视觉状态转移—机器人动作"配对，无需人工任务标注即可扩展动作接地的数据规模；2）通过两阶段训练方案将眼在手上感知扩展进 IDM，从而不需要眼在手上的视频规划器。

Result: 大量仿真与真实世界实验显示：IRIS 改善了动作接地质量与数据效率，支持更精确的执行，并在与当前最先进操作策略的比较中取得有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 动作接地而非视觉规划是"想象后执行"范式的关键瓶颈；通过无标注随机探索数据预训练 IDM 并两阶段引入眼在手上观测，可在不增加视频规划难度的前提下显著提升 IDM 的泛化性与执行精度，为可扩展的视频驱动机器人操作提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Imagine-then-execute methods offer a promising paradigm for video-based manipulation, where a video planner predicts future visual subgoals and an inverse dynamics model (IDM) grounds them into robot actions. Despite their promise, these methods remain limited by action grounding. First, the IDM is typically trained on limited task-specific demonstrations and can generalize poorly even when given oracle visual subgoals, indicating that action grounding remains a bottleneck beyond visual planning errors. Second, precise execution benefits from eye-in-hand observations, but eye-in-hand video planning is both hard to learn and hard to scale: the viewpoint moves with the robot, and such videos are scarce at internet scale. To overcome these challenges, we propose IRIS, a video-based manipulation framework that learns a highly generalizable, execution-aware IDM. First, IRIS scales action grounding by pretraining the IDM on task-agnostic random exploration data, which provides diverse visual state transitions paired with robot actions without human task annotation. Second, it extends the IDM with eye-in-hand sensing through a two-stage training scheme, without requiring eye-in-hand video planning. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments show that IRIS improves action grounding and data efficiency, enables more precise execution, and achieves competitive performance against state-of-the-art manipulation policies.

</details>


### [23] [Data-Driven Trajectory Prediction via Probabilistic Modes and Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28720)
*Danyang Li,Ana Theodora Balaci,Matthew Cleaveland,Roberto Tron*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ProMo-CoOp两阶段数据驱动轨迹预测框架：设计阶段将轨迹聚类为行为模式并用高斯混合模型(GMM)给出概率化模式分配；预测阶段针对每个模式求解凸优化问题生成候选轨迹，并以预计算模式分配引导预测朝向选定模式，同时支持基于条件流匹配(CFM)估计谓词满足概率的STL行为约束。实验表明其优于SOTA基线，且可靠谓词预测下STL约束可进一步提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需预测周边智能体未来轨迹，而智能体意图本身具有不确定性，因此必须进行多模态预测以覆盖所有合理的未来行为。现有方法在多模态性、与观测轨迹的一致性以及可解释的行为约束融合方面仍有不足。

Method: 两阶段框架。设计阶段：轨迹聚类为行为模式，GMM提供概率化模式分配。预测阶段：对每个模式求解凸优化问题生成候选轨迹，约束其与观测轨迹一致，并利用预计算模式分配将预测引导至目标行为模式；此外支持STL导出的行为约束，谓词满足概率由条件流匹配(CFM)估计。

Result: 实验结果显示ProMo-CoOp优于当前最先进基线；在预测的谓词满足概率可靠时，引入STL约束可进一步提升预测性能。

Conclusion: 将概率化模式建模（GMM聚类）与凸优化轨迹生成相结合，可有效实现多模态且与观测一致的轨迹预测，并能以STL约束形式灵活融入行为先验；其收益依赖于谓词满足概率估计的可靠性。

Abstract: Predicting the future trajectories of surrounding agents is a fundamental requirement for safe autonomous driving. Since agent intent is inherently uncertain, multimodal prediction is essential to account for the full range of plausible future behaviors. We introduce Probabilistic Modes and Constrained Optimization trajectory prediction (ProMo-CoOp), a two-stage data-driven framework. At design time, trajectories are clustered into behavioral modes and a Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM) provides probabilistic mode assignments. At prediction time, a candidate trajectory is generated for each mode by solving a convex optimization problem that enforces consistency with the observed trajectory and uses the precomputed mode assignments to guide prediction toward the selected behavioral mode. The framework also supports Signal Temporal Logic (STL)-derived behavioral constraints, with predicate satisfaction probabilities estimated using Conditional Flow Matching (CFM). Experiments show that ProMo-CoOp outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, and that STL-based constraints can further improve performance when the predicted predicate satisfactions are reliable.

</details>


### [24] [Entangle: Scaling Human-Video In-Context World-Action Models across Unseen Tasks and Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28732)
*Chendong Xiang,Chengwen Zhang,Ruowen Zhao,Zeyuan Wang,Shuhe Huang,Rongxu Cui,Min Zhao,Fan Bao,Hengkai Tan,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Entangle，一种上下文世界-动作模型（WAM），以单段人类视频和当前机器人观测为输入，输出未来视觉状态与可执行动作；通过视频到视频生成构建时序对齐的人-机器人配对数据，并用变长视频窗口加任务内对比目标抑制捷径学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频可为机器人操作提供"如何做"的上下文，但在世界-动作模型上规模化人类视频 ICL 面临两个障碍：任务级人-机器人配对与机器人动作在时间上不对齐；标准动作损失使策略依赖初始场景或任务身份等捷径，而非真正跟踪上下文。

Method: 1）数据侧：将已录制的机器人视频通过视频到视频生成转换为匹配的人类上下文，并辅以真实配对录制，以规模化获取时序对齐的训练数据；2）训练侧：结合变长视频窗口与任务内对比目标，使配对动作轨迹区别于同一任务的其他执行，从而阻断任务身份捷径；3）模型将单个人类视频与当前机器人观测映射到未来视觉状态和可执行动作，无需任务特定微调。

Result: 在不做任务特定微调的情况下，性能随对齐训练数据规模稳定提升；在 Astribot S1 机器人上六个未见任务平均得分 83.5/100；在七个未见 RoboTwin 任务上平均成功率 69.5%，高于 Zero-WAM 22.6 个百分点；在 Context-OOD 设定下（仅用场景对齐配对训练）仍能将上下文指定的行为迁移到重排布局与替换工具。

Conclusion: 时序对齐的数据规模化与抑制捷径的对比训练是提升人类视频 ICL 世界-动作模型可扩展性的关键；Entangle 展示了良好的数据缩放性、跨任务泛化与上下文层面的分布外迁移能力。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) enables foundation models to perform unseen tasks from context without parameter updates. In robotic manipulation, a human video provides the context that specifies how a task should be performed. However, scaling human-video ICL on world-action models (WAMs) faces two challenges: task-level human-robot pairs are not temporally aligned with robot actions, and standard action losses allow policies to rely on initial-scene or task-identity shortcuts rather than tracking the context. We present Entangle, an in-context world-action model that maps a single human video and current robot observations to future visual states and executable actions. To scale temporally aligned training data, we convert recorded robot videos into matched human contexts via video-to-video generation, supplemented by real paired recordings. For model training, we combine variable-length video windows with an intra-task contrastive objective that distinguishes the paired action trajectory from other executions of the same task, preventing task-identity shortcuts. Without task-specific fine-tuning, Entangle improves steadily as aligned training data scales, reaching an average score of 83.5/100 across six unseen tasks on an Astribot S1 robot, and achieves a 69.5% average success rate on seven unseen RoboTwin tasks, outperforming Zero-WAM by 22.6 percentage points. Under Context-OOD, although trained only on scene-aligned pairs, Entangle transfers context-specified behaviors across rearranged layouts and substituted tools.

</details>


### [25] [VTLA-RL: Learning Visuotactile Policies for Load-Adaptive Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28752)
*Qiwei Wu,Qixuan Li,Xin Xiang,Xiangyu Chen,Xiang Xiao,Daochen Shi,Ruihua Zhang,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VTLA-RL框架，在视觉编码器共享的前提下联合生成运动与双手力目标，用混合真实/仿真示范建立先验，再通过两阶段多任务仿真RL后训练，实现变负载下的视觉-触觉抓取并直接部署到真实机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅靠视觉难以推断物体质量与局部滑移，负载变化需要接触驱动的抓取调整，而固定条件下采集的示范难以充分覆盖这些情况，因此需要研究视觉-触觉抓取策略及其RL后训练。

Method: 在视觉编码器上共享触觉图像历史与RGB输入，在统一动作空间中同时生成并执行运动目标与双侧力目标；先用真实与仿真混合示范建立运动与接触先验；再用两阶段多任务RL，仅依赖仿真交互、以任务成功回报优化策略，不继续使用真实示范监督；训练后策略直接部署到真实机器人。仿真中比较不同触觉表征与RL后训练路径，硬件上分别评估负载变化与放置扰动。

Result: 完整方法在仿真与硬件上的抓取成功率高于监督初始化；不同表征与后训练路线的RL增益存在差异，为任务中的表征与训练方式选择提供了经验依据。

Conclusion: 共享视觉编码器+统一动作空间+仿真RL后训练的组合能有效提升变负载抓取表现，且孪生式"混合示范预训练→纯仿真RL精调→零样本实机部署"流程可行；触觉表征与RL路径的选择对最终收益影响显著。

Abstract: Vision-language-action foundation models learn general manipulation priors, but object mass and local slip are difficult to infer from vision alone. Changing loads require contact-driven grasp adjustments that demonstrations collected under fixed conditions may not adequately cover. We present VTLA-RL to study visuotactile grasping policies and their reinforcement learning (RL) post-training under varying loads. Our main approach shares a visual encoder between tactile-image histories and RGB inputs to jointly generate and execute motion and bilateral force targets in a unified action space. Mixed real and simulated demonstrations first establish motion and contact priors. Two-stage multi-task RL then uses only simulated interaction to optimize the policy through task-success returns without continued real-demonstration supervision, and the trained policy deploys directly to real robots. We compare tactile representations and RL post-training routes in simulation and separately evaluate load variation and placement perturbations on hardware. The full method achieves higher grasping success than the supervised initialization, while RL post-training gains differ across approaches, providing empirical evidence for choosing representations and training approaches for this task.

</details>


### [26] [DeRail: Escaping Trajectory Priors via Counterfactual Action Steering for Mid-Execution Instruction Switching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28803)
*Yitong Hong,Xiangdong Feng,Xiaoxi Sun,Senqiao Yang,Boyao Han,Zhejian Yang,Shiyang Zhou,Yongcai Wang,Pengguang Chen,Shu Liu,Li Jiang,Zhuotao Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文研究机器人策略在执行中指令切换时的失败现象，提出"轨迹劫持"这一关键失效模式，并给出免训练、模型无关的推理期方法Counterfactual Action Steering (CAS)，通过反事实指令的动作差异引导策略偏离干扰轨迹，显著提升切换成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令条件机器人策略（VLA与World-Action Models）大多在固定指令的episode下评估，而真实部署中用户指令常在执行中途改变。作者发现此类策略在切换后会部分或完全被非目标任务的轨迹先验"拉回"，即轨迹劫持（trajectory hijacking），但该问题缺乏系统研究与方法。

Method: 作者构建了LIBERO-Goal-Switch与CALVIN-Custom-Switch两个中途切换评测设定，并分析失效模式。方法CAS属推理期干预：先用策略识别出产生干扰的非目标任务，再用对应反事实指令查询同一策略，计算目标动作与反事实动作之差，据此将执行动作从干扰轨迹先验方向推开；全程无需训练、无需修改模型，可直接套用于各类VLA/WAM策略。

Result: 在五个VLA与WAM策略上均有一致提升；例如CALVIN-Custom-Switch在[15,21]切换区间内，CAS将π0.5的成功率从5.8%提升到54.6%。此外，CAS引导的成功轨迹作为自训练监督，在相同轨迹数量下优于普通成功轨迹，训练出的策略对更晚出现的未见指令切换具有更强泛化能力。真机实验同样验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 中途指令切换是当前机器人策略被忽视的重要评测维度，轨迹劫持是其主要瓶颈；CAS以简单、免训练、模型无关的方式有效缓解该问题，并能通过提升自训练数据质量实现持续改进，代码将公开。

Abstract: Robots operating in the real world must often adapt when user instructions change mid-execution, yet current instruction-conditioned robot policies, including Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models and World-Action Models (WAMs), are largely evaluated under fixed-instruction episodes. We study mid-execution instruction switching and identify trajectory hijacking as a key failure mode, where the policy fails to redirect sufficiently toward the switched target, with its post-switch behavior remaining fully or partially drawn toward trajectories associated with non-target tasks. We propose Counterfactual Action Steering (CAS), a training-free, model-agnostic inference-time method that identifies interfering non-target tasks, queries the same policy with corresponding counterfactual instructions, and uses target--counterfactual action differences to steer execution away from interfering trajectory priors. Experiments on LIBERO-Goal-Switch and CALVIN-Custom-Switch across five VLA and WAM policies, together with real-robot evaluation, show that CAS consistently improves switching success; for example, on CALVIN-Custom-Switch in the $[15,21]$ switching range, CAS improves $π_{0.5}$ from 5.8\% to 54.6\%. Successful CAS-guided rollouts further provide higher-quality self-training supervision than vanilla successful rollouts with the same number of trajectories, yielding stronger policies that generalize to unseen later instruction switches. Code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [27] [LIBERO-MOR: A Multimodal Robustness Benchmark Across the Vision-Language-Action Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28867)
*Qihang Chen,Tengjie Li,Hongwei Lu,Jason Wu,Chyna Qu,Shikui Tu,Lei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出多模态鲁棒性基准 LIBERO-MOR，覆盖视觉、语言、本体感知、动作执行与环境五个部署相关域，含22类扰动×3个严重程度×40个任务，采用配对干净-扰动协议评测7个代表性VLA模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人操作基准上表现优异，但落地部署需要系统评估策略在交互全流程中对各类变化的响应能力；现有评测多关注干净任务成功率，难以揭示鲁棒性缺口。

Method: 基于标准LIBERO构建，覆盖视觉、语言、本体感知、动作执行、环境五大域，共22种部署启发式扰动，每种扰动设3个严重程度，在40个任务上采用配对干净-扰动协议进行控制可复现的比较；评测7个涵盖不同动作生成机制的VLA模型。

Result: 高干净任务成功率背后存在显著鲁棒性缺口；模型间存在跨域的共同脆弱点，也有各自的特定失效模式；严重程度分析区分出渐进退化、强干预下崩溃、以及在最轻扰动下即失效三类情形。

Conclusion: LIBERO-MOR将总体性能与流水线域、单项扰动及干预强度相连接，为诊断VLA鲁棒性、指导更可靠机器人策略开发提供系统框架。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have achieved strong performance on robot manipulation benchmarks. Translating this progress into reliable deployment calls for systematic evaluation of how policies respond to variations throughout the robot interaction loop. We introduce LIBERO-MOR, a multimodal robustness benchmark across the vision-language-action pipeline. Built on standard LIBERO, it covers five deployment-facing domains: vision, language, proprioception, action execution, and environment. The benchmark comprises 22 deployment-inspired perturbations, each evaluated at three severity levels across 40 tasks under a paired clean-perturbed protocol, enabling controlled and reproducible comparisons. We evaluate seven representative VLA models spanning diverse action-generation mechanisms. Our experiments reveal substantial robustness gaps behind high clean-task success, together with shared vulnerabilities and distinct model-specific failure patterns across domains. Severity analysis further distinguishes progressive degradation, collapse at stronger interventions, and failures triggered at the mildest tested level. By connecting aggregate performance to pipeline domains, individual perturbations, and intervention strength, LIBERO-MOR provides a systematic framework for diagnosing VLA robustness and guiding the development of more reliable robot policies.

</details>


### [28] [NAT: Scaling Causal Neural Action Tokenizers Across Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28873)
*Ahad Jawaid,Alexander Swerdlow,Yu Xiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出神经动作分词器（NAT），一个因果式动作分词器，其解码器可在token生成过程中同步执行动作；在4.9万小时多本体数据上训练后，NAT在未见机器人本体的分词、VLA模型下游任务和真机执行上优于FAST及以往神经动作分词器，且仅用人类手部运动数据训练的tokenizer性能接近使用机器人数据的版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作分词器使机器人策略可将控制建模为自回归的下一token预测。随着策略训练数据规模与多样性增长，需要一个通用的动作分词器，能够编码来自多种本体和数据源（包括未见过的本体）的动作。作者因此研究大规模动作分词问题。

Method: 在来自真实机器人、手持夹爪、仿真和人类的总计4.9万小时动作数据上训练动作分词器。提出NAT（Neural Action Tokenizer），一种因果分词器，其解码器支持在动作token生成过程中即时执行动作。在四个仿真基准上评估跨本体分词效果，并集成到视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中对比FAST+，同时在真机上验证。

Result: 在四个仿真基准上，NAT在分词未见机器人本体时优于FAST和先前的神经动作分词器；在VLA模型中，NAT在四个基准以及真机执行上均优于FAST+。NAT token在某些基准上缩小了与flow-matching动作头的性能差距，同时降低了首个动作的推理延迟。此外，仅用人类手部运动训练的NAT性能接近使用机器人数据训练的tokenizer，说明机器人数据可能并非学习有效动作空间的必要条件。

Conclusion: NAT展示了大规模、跨本体动作分词的有效性，支持因果式（流式）动作执行，并在分词质量、下游VLA性能与推理延迟上取得优势；同时提示人类动作数据可作为构建通用动作空间的重要且低成本来源。

Abstract: Action tokenizers let robot policies treat control as autoregressive next-token prediction. As these policies are trained on larger and more diverse datasets, they will need a universal tokenizer that can encode actions from many embodiments and data sources, including ones it has not seen. We study action tokenization at scale, training on 49k hours of action data from real-world robots, handheld grippers, simulation, and humans. We introduce Neural Action Tokenizer (NAT), a causal tokenizer whose decoder lets actions be executed as their tokens are generated. Across four simulation benchmarks, NAT outperforms FAST and prior neural action tokenizers when tokenizing unseen robot embodiments, and in vision-language-action models it outperforms FAST+ across four benchmarks and on a real robot when acting through its tokens. NAT tokens also close the performance gap to a flow-matching action head on some benchmarks while lowering the inference latency for first actions. Our experiments also find that robot data may not be necessary to learn an effective action space: a NAT trained only on human hand motion performs close to tokenizers trained on robot data.

</details>


### [29] [T-Tac: A Soft and Thin Active Thermal Tactile Sensor for Robotic In-Grasp Material Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28939)
*Jiankun Li,Yu Feng,Yehui Liu,Meng Chen,Rui Luo,Guanglie Zhang,Jianshu Zhou,Wen Jung Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出T-Tac，一种柔性薄膜式主动热触觉传感器，通过材料的瞬态热响应进行识别，并结合热学描述子与多尺度CNN特征的混合分类框架，在12种材料上取得92.69%的平均分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作需要准确的材料识别，但外观相似的材料难以仅靠视觉区分，因此需要一种可集成到机器人夹爪上的紧凑主动热触觉传感方案。

Method: T-Tac将聚酰亚胺薄膜加热器与离子导电薄膜温度传感器集成于薄膜组件中，实现可控热激励与基于电阻的温度测量；采用单面瞬态平面热源建模与有限元仿真建立热响应与材料热物性的关系，并进行标定、重复测量以及室温与高温下的加载实验；分类上融合提取的热学描述子与多尺度CNN特征，用ExtraTrees分类器进行材料预测。

Result: 在12种材料上，使用3 s加热脉冲与10 s观测窗口，取得92.69%的平均分类准确率；标定与重复测量、加载实验验证了加热与传感性能及受力下的响应特性。

Conclusion: T-Tac为将主动热材料感知集成到机器人末端执行器提供了一种紧凑可行的途径，为机器人材料识别开辟了新思路。

Abstract: Accurate material identification is important for robotic manipulation, yet vision-based methods face challenges when materials have similar appearances. Herein, we report T-Tac, a soft, thin, active thermal tactile sensor that identifies materials through their transient thermal responses. T-Tac integrates a polyimide film heater with an ionic conductive film temperature sensor, providing controlled thermal excitation and resistance-based temperature measurement in a film assembly suitable for mounting on robotic grippers. Single-sided transient plane source modeling and finite element simulations relate the thermal response to material thermal properties, while calibration and repeated measurements characterize the heating and sensing performance. Loading experiments at room and elevated temperatures further characterize the sensor response under an applied force. A hybrid classification framework concatenates extracted thermal descriptors with multiscale convolutional neural network features and uses an ExtraTrees classifier for material prediction. Experiments on 12 materials achieve a mean classification accuracy of 92.69\% using a 3 s heating pulse and a 10 s observation window. These results support T-Tac as a compact approach to integrating active thermal material sensing into robotic end effectors. This work shed a light on the new approach for robotic material identification.

</details>


### [30] [SANA-Robot: Efficient World Action Model with Hybrid Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28943)
*Yuyang Zhao,Zekai Li,Yicheng Pan,Jiaming Tang,Zhuoyang Zhang,Jiaxu Xing,Moritz Reuss,Quanquan Peng,Junsong Chen,Enze Xie,Zhijian Liu,Song Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SANA-Robot 是一个 4.5B 参数的世界动作模型（WAM），通过常数开销的混合记忆机制与统一策略/世界模拟器设计，实现边缘设备上 30 Hz 实时闭环机器人控制，并在 RoboDojo 记忆维度与多个 LIBERO 变体上取得领先成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型借助视频生成模型的世界知识，将视觉预测与动作预测对齐来提升动作生成能力，但预测开销大导致难以在边缘设备实时部署，且普遍缺乏有效的长期记忆，无法支撑需要整段任务记忆的长时程操作。

Method: 三项核心设计：(1) 常数成本混合记忆——用线性注意力维护固定尺寸状态的全局记忆，配合滑动窗口 softmax 注意力层传播历史信息；(2) 统一模型——对动作 token 与视频 token 动态施加不同噪声水平，使同一模型既可作策略也可作世界模拟器；(3) 全栈基础设施——面向记忆训练与推理的高效数据打包与打乱，以及基于记忆阶段的异步执行。

Result: 在中等规模（约 5B）模型中，SANA-Robot 在 RoboDojo 的记忆维度上表现最佳，并在 LIBERO-Plus、LIBERO-Pro、LIBERO-Max 上取得最高成功率。真实部署中，RTX 5090 上配合 FP8 GEMM 每次动作块耗时 135 ms、NVFP4 GEMM 下为 115 ms，在边缘设备上实现 30 Hz 的闭环实时异步控制。

Conclusion: SANA-Robot 证明了通过混合记忆架构与统一建模范式，可以在保持视频世界模型能力的同时大幅降低推理开销，使具备分钟级记忆的世界动作模型真正落地于边缘端实时机器人控制，兼顾效率与有效性。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) leverage the world knowledge of video generation models to improve action generation by aligning visual prediction with action prediction. However, their prediction overhead prevents real-time deployment on edge devices, and they usually lack an effective long-term memory. We introduce SANA-Robot, a 4.5B WAM with minute-level memory that runs in real time on edge devices, built on the following core designs: (1) hybrid memory at constant cost: our WAM relies on linear attention to maintain a global memory with a fixed-size state, and on sliding window softmax attention layers that propagate previous information, enabling high success rates on tasks requiring whole-episode memory while keeping an efficient and constant inference speed; (2) a unified model: by dynamically applying different noise levels to action and video tokens, SANA-Robot can serve not only as a policy but also as a world simulator; (3) a full-stack infrastructure for both memory training and inference, including efficient data packing and shuffling as well as memory-phase-based asynchronous execution. SANA-Robot achieves the best performance on the memory dimension of RoboDojo among models of medium size (about 5B) and the highest success rates on LIBERO-Plus, LIBERO-Pro and LIBERO-Max. In real-world deployment, SANA-Robot takes 135 ms per action chunk on an RTX 5090 with FP8 GEMMs and 115 ms with NVFP4 GEMMs, and achieves closed-loop, real-time asynchronous robot control at 30 Hz on edge devices, demonstrating both the efficiency and the effectiveness of SANA-Robot. Project page: https://nvlabs.github.io/Sana/Robot/

</details>


### [31] [World Models for Ocean Engineering: A Critical Review of Maritime Autonomy, Adaptive Latent Prediction, Digital Twins, and Biodiversity Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28978)
*Basma Dokkara,Esteban W. G. Cluab,Luiz M. G. Goncalves,Paolo Spagnolo,Cosimo Distante*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文是一篇面向海洋工程的"世界模型"（WM）综述，按功能提出分类体系，重点考察联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）及 AdaJEPA 在模型预测控制中的测试时自适应，并将其映射到导航、自适应采样、数字孪生与生物多样性监测四类任务，区分已验证证据与前瞻性应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋机器人面临部分可观测性、非平稳动力学与稀疏通信等挑战，需要学习紧凑预测状态的世界模型来支撑规划；但该领域证据分散、缺少统一的功能视角，亟需厘清哪些能力已被验证、哪些仍属设想。

Method: 采用文献综述与综合分析方法，构建面向海洋工程的世界模型功能分类框架，以 JEPA 与 AdaJEPA 的测试时自适应机制为核心线索，将方法与典型海洋任务进行对应映射，并对证据强度进行分级梳理。

Result: 识别出三类关键缺口：缺乏标准化基准、不确定性校准不足、安全自适应机制欠缺；同时归纳出物理信息融合与部署自适性两条有前景的技术路线。

Conclusion: 提出研究议程：发展物理信息驱动、可部署自适应的海洋世界模型，并通过仿真与实地任务双重验证，以弥合方法研究与真实海洋部署之间的差距。

Abstract: Marine robots face partial observability, non-stationary dynamics, and sparse communication, motivating WMs that learn compact predictive states for planning. This review develops a functional taxonomy of WMs for ocean engineering, focusing on Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) and AdaJEPA's test-time adaptation within model predictive control, mapping them to navigation, adaptive sampling, digital twins, and biodiversity monitoring, and separating demonstrated evidence from prospective applications. The synthesis identifies key Gaps: benchmarks, uncertainty calibration, safe adaptation and proposes a research agenda for physics-informed, deployment-adaptive marine WMs validated through simulation and field missions.

</details>


### [32] [DMPT-Swarm: A Directional Mass-Preserving Transform for Decentralized Swarm Navigation in Obstacle Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28999)
*Jacob Lee,Samuel Sutphin,Haroon Malik,Chang Liu,Pingping Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种去中心化微观集群导航控制框架：基于GMM宏观轨迹，利用Wasserstein-2度量下的仿射输运与方向性质量保持变换（DMPT）生成无通信的避障参考点，再由局部APF控制器实现导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集群导航需要在障碍环境中同时兼顾宏观轨迹引导与微观个体避障；传统方法依赖智能体间通信或集中计算，通信与计算负担大，且常规APF易受障碍影响导致效率低。

Method: 1) 以GMM表示预计算的宏观轨迹；2) 在Wasserstein-2度量下对多变量正态分布做仿射输运，为每个智能体生成参考位置，无需智能体间通信；3) 提出方向性质量保持变换（DMPT），通过修改径向输运、保留方向结构来融入障碍影响；4) 在变换后的参考点基础上采用局部人工势场（APF）控制器进行导航，各智能体独立运行。

Result: 仿真结果表明，与传统APF方法相比，该框架在效率和避障性能上均有提升。

Conclusion: 将宏观GMM轨迹的概率输运与微观局部势场控制相结合，可在去中心化、低通信条件下实现高效的障碍环境集群导航。

Abstract: This paper presents a decentralized microscopic control framework for swarm navigation in obstacle environments, given a precomputed macroscopic trajectory represented by Gaussian mixture models (GMMs). To generate obstacle-aware reference locations without inter-agent communication, we first employ affine transport between multivariate normal (MVN) distributions under the Wasserstein-2 metric. We then propose a directional mass-preserving transform (DMPT) to incorporate obstacle effects by modifying radial transport while preserving directional structure. Based on the transformed references, a local artificial potential field (APF)-based controller is applied for navigation. Each agent operates independently, reducing communication and computational burden. Simulation results demonstrate improved efficiency and obstacle avoidance compared to conventional APF-based methods

</details>


### [33] [graspit-py: A Python Interface to GraspIt! for Grasp Planning Under Pose and Friction Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29011)
*Clinton Enwerem,John S. Baras,Calin Belta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 graspit-py：面向 GraspIt! 原生操作的 Python 接口，支持位姿与摩擦不确定性下的接触-力旋量分析、可配置接近规划，并配套 MuJoCo 导出器（串联铰链链、仿射关节耦合、非零参考角、独立碰撞组件）与导出-规划-重构闭环工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指抓取仿真中，位姿误差会改变手闭合过程中形成的接触，摩擦不确定性会改变这些接触所承担的载荷；现有 GraspIt! 的 C++ API 与 ROS 1 服务在 Python 使用时需原生绑定或服务传输层，且接触几何与不确定性采样需按应用定制集成，缺少统一流程。

Method: 1) 构建 Python 接口封装 GraspIt! 原生场景访问、接触-力旋量分析与可配置接近规划；2) 设计抓取记录结构，将物体相对掌心位姿与闭合独立坐标同名义接触及采样位姿/摩擦条件下的质量评分关联；3) 开发 MuJoCo 导出器，生成串联铰链链与仿射关节耦合（支持非零参考角）并保留独立碰撞组件；4) 以 75 组配置（5 种手模型）对比源与导出掌心系顶点集，进行参考角与碰撞组件的受控消融，并用独立笛卡尔力线性规划校验 1920 次方向性任务力旋量容量计算；5) 在通用 GraspIt! 工作负载下对比直接 Python 与持久化 ROS 1 服务的复位-闭合-评分-状态循环延迟。

Result: 75 组配置下源与导出掌心系顶点集最大偏差约 1.3 微米，远低于 100 微米几何容差；1920 次方向性任务力旋量容量计算与独立线性规划结果一致；通用工作负载中经直接 Python 的中位循环延迟比持久化 ROS 1 服务低 10.7%；在六种手模型上完成导出-规划-重构可执行工作流与抓取示例。

Conclusion: graspit-py 将原生闭合与接触同调用者定义的力旋量评分、不确定性采样与指令重构连通，导出几何精度高、力旋量容量计算经独立方法验证、延迟优于 ROS 1 服务，其接触、评分与关节表示可支撑数值分析与指令重构。

Abstract: In simulated multifingered grasps, pose error can change contacts formed during hand closure, while friction uncertainty changes the loads supported by those contacts. Existing C++ APIs and ROS 1 services provide programmatic GraspIt! access but require native bindings or a service transport layer for Python use and application-specific integration of contact geometry with uncertainty sampling. A Python workflow for these studies must connect native closure and contacts to caller-defined wrench scores, uncertainty samples, and command reconstruction. We present graspit-py, a Python interface to native GraspIt! operations that supplies this workflow. The interface provides native scene access, contact-wrench analysis, and configurable approach planning under pose and friction uncertainty. A grasp record associates the object-relative palm pose and closed independent coordinates with nominal contacts and quality scores evaluated under sampled pose and friction conditions. Our MuJoCo exporter derives serial hinge chains and affine joint couplings, including nonzero reference angles, and preserves individual collision components. Across 75 configurations of five hand models, maximum discrepancy between source and exported palm-frame vertex sets was approximately 1.3 micrometers against a 100-micrometer geometry tolerance. We isolated reference-angle and collision-component effects through controlled ablations and validated 1,920 directional task-wrench capacity calculations against independent Cartesian-force linear programs. In a common GraspIt! workload, median reset-close-score-state cycle latency through direct Python was 10.7% lower than through persistent ROS 1 services. An executable export-plan-reconstruct workflow and grasp examples on six hand models demonstrate how the contact, score, and joint representations support numerical analysis and command reconstruction.

</details>


### [34] [Distributed Interactive Conformal Prediction for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29037)
*Daniel M. Cherenson,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分布式多智能体交互式共形预测框架MA-ICP，用于异构多智能体系统在依赖关系存在时计算满足任务要求的高概率鲁棒裕度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统依赖学习型组件与分布式算法，引入不确定性，难以保证任务要求；现有共形预测方法未处理机器人间依赖性、分布式校准以及校准后的不确定性边界改变闭环系统所导致的分布偏移这一组合难题。

Method: MA-ICP将机器人级非一致性分数聚合成每个机器人类别的任务级分数，允许任务内机器人间存在依赖；通过有限轮数的邻居间通信计算任务级分数与分位数；利用类别相关的分数敏感性上界化更新鲁棒裕度后任务级分数的偏移，进而给出更新裕度下每类机器人的校准条件覆盖保证。

Result: 在协同负载控制、带避障的编队控制以及分布式目标跟踪任务上进行了演示，展示了该方法的应用效果与覆盖保证。

Conclusion: MA-ICP为异构多智能体系统提供了一种分布式、对分布偏移稳健的共形预测校准方案，能在机器人间存在依赖时给出校准条件覆盖保证，适用于多种协同任务。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems increasingly rely on learning-enabled components and distributed algorithms for perception, estimation, and control, introducing uncertainty that makes it challenging to satisfy mission requirements. Conformal prediction quantifies uncertainty with distribution-free guarantees, but existing methods do not address the challenging combination of dependence among robots, distributed calibration, and the distribution shift induced when calibrated uncertainty bounds modify the closed-loop system. We introduce Multi-Agent Interactive Conformal Prediction (MA-ICP), a distributed framework for computing robustness margins in heterogeneous systems to satisfy mission requirements with high probability. MA-ICP aggregates robot-level nonconformity scores, which measure errors in modeled or estimated quantities, into one mission-level score per robot class, allowing dependence among robots within each mission. Mission-level scores and quantiles are computed using neighbor-to-neighbor communication in a finite number of communication rounds. Updating the robustness margins changes robot trajectories and distributed algorithm outputs, so we use class-dependent score sensitivities to upper bound the shift in mission-level scores. We then establish calibration-conditional coverage guarantees per robot class for the updated margins. We demonstrate MA-ICP on cooperative payload control, formation control with obstacle avoidance, and distributed target tracking missions.

</details>


### [35] [BO-COMM: Bidirectional Optical System for Simultaneous Localization and Communication in Swarm Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29044)
*Cheng Hu,Haotian Wu,Qi Lin,Manrui Zheng,Shigang Yue,Tao Zou,Jigen Peng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BO-COMM：用圆形LED阵列+相机实现群体机器人位姿估计与动态状态上报，并用投影仪和红外模块构成光学下行交互通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体机器人实验需同时获取机器人位姿与运行状态，并把环境信息回传给机器人；共享信道无线通信在并发上报增多时易出现竞争与拥塞，而静态视觉标记本身无法传递机器人变化的状态。

Method: 将Colias IV机器人（配CEU-PLUS扩展单元）安装圆形LED阵列，由相机观测；用环形投票估计机器人中心，用空间LED图案编码朝向与状态；投影仪提供空间环境提示，红外模块提供独立指令通道。

Result: 处理时延测量、路径跟随与颜色上报实验以及多信息素演示，在平面场地中验证了该架构的运行；结果表明在测试硬件与场地配置下，将光学位姿与状态观测同环境反馈相结合是可行的。

Conclusion: BO-COMM以光学方式统一了位姿估计、动态状态上报与下行环境交互，可缓解无线拥塞问题；其可行性限于所测试的硬件与场地配置。

Abstract: Swarm robotics experiments require observations of robot pose and operational state, together with mechanisms for returning environmental information to the robots. Shared channel wireless communication can face contention and congestion as concurrent reporting demands increase, while a static visual marker does not itself convey changing robot states. We present BO-COMM, an optical system that combines pose estimation and dynamic state reporting with optical downstream interaction. A camera observes circular LED arrays mounted on Colias IV robots with CEU-PLUS extension units.Ring-based voting estimates robot centers, and the spatial LED pattern provides orientation and an encoded state. A projector supplies spatial environmental cues, while an infrared module provides a separate command channel. Processing-time measurements, path-following and color-reporting experiments, and a multi-pheromone demonstration illustrate the operation of this architecture in a planar arena. The results demonstrate the feasibility of combining optical pose and state observations with environmental feedback in the tested hardware and arena configuration.

</details>


### [36] [ViewPACT: Multi-View Geometric Augmentation and Pose-Action Co-Training for Viewpoint-Robust Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29068)
