*Chenglang Tu,Feng Chen,Ruizhe Liu,Pei Zhou,Binbin Huang,Chen Xu,Yanchao Yang,Yi Ma,Shenghua Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ViewPACT，用相机坐标系下末端执行器与任务物体位姿的几何辅助监督训练图像策略，部署时仅需 RGB，显著提升未见视角下的视角鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像策略可扩展但对相机视角变化脆弱。作者在 LIBERO 上发现本体感知（机器人状态）构成输入捷径：遮蔽机器人状态会显著降低多个策略的性能，而仅用图像重训可恢复大部分损失，说明现有策略未真正从图像中学习空间几何信息。

Method: ViewPACT：以特权几何监督训练图像策略。引入辅助目标，在相机坐标系下回归末端执行器位姿和任务相关物体位姿；部署时不需要任何特权信息，仅用 RGB 观测。真实机器人实验中结合仿真与真实图像的几何监督。

Result: 在未见外部相机视角下，平均仿真成功率由 37.4% 提升至 78.4%；在物体、场景和相机内参变化下增益较小。四个真实机器人任务上，仿真+真实图像几何监督平均成功率为 68.1%，固定视角行为克隆为 3.1%，最强对比方法为 30.6%。

Conclusion: 在评测的固定视角示教设定下，几何监督有效提升视角鲁棒性；该结论为图像策略的视角泛化提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Image policies are scalable but remain brittle to camera viewpoint changes. On LIBERO, we identify proprioception as an input shortcut: masking robot states sharply degrades several policies, while retraining from images alone recovers much of the loss. We introduce ViewPACT, an image policy trained with privileged geometric supervision. Auxiliary objectives recover end-effector and task-relevant object poses in the camera frame, while deployment requires only RGB observations. Joint geometric supervision improves mean simulation success from 37.4% to 78.4% under unseen external-camera viewpoints, with more modest gains under separately evaluated object, scene, and camera-intrinsic shifts. Across four real-robot tasks, combining simulated and real-image geometric supervision achieves 68.1% mean success, versus 3.1% for fixed-view behavior cloning and 30.6% for the strongest evaluated alternative. These results support geometric supervision for viewpoint robustness in the evaluated fixed-view demonstration setting.

</details>


### [37] [JEPA-Tuner: From Predictive Fine-Tuning to Asynchronous Execution of Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29096)
*Jingxiang Qu,Tian Xie,Youngjin Hong,Changhyun Choi,Yang Katie Zhao,Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: JEPA-Tuner 通过对 VLA 原生策略引导做动作条件下的未来预测正则化，提升策略学习与异步动作块推理的精度、平滑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准动作监督微调没有显式建模动作条件的时间依赖（执行动作→改变观测与状态→影响后续决策），引导表征对控制的信息量不足；异步推理时块边界处还需要重叠校正或额外训练。

Method: 在微调阶段引入 JEPA 式预测器：给定当前引导与已执行动作，预测未来观测编码的引导；用该目标约束跨模态引导适配器学习场景与机器人状态的动作条件演化，同时让动作专家基于观测引导与预测引导联合训练。部署时利用预训练预测器提前推断下一块边界所需引导，从而提前生成下一动作块并从首个动作直接执行。

Result: LIBERO 与 Meta-World 上同步推理任务成功率提升，多模态引导嵌入质量更好；异步控制在不同延迟设置下性能与平滑性均提升，并在 LIBERO 仿真与多种尺寸真机演示上验证。

Conclusion: 动作条件未来预测是一种有效且通用的 VLA 微调正则，可同时改善表征与异步执行，无需重叠校正或额外预测器训练。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models generate sequences of actions from visual observations, proprioceptive states, and language instructions. In embodied interaction, executing these actions influences subsequent observations and robot states, providing a natural predictive signal for policy learning. However, standard action-supervised fine-tuning does not explicitly capture these action-conditioned temporal dependencies in the guidance consumed by the action predictor. We introduce JEPA-Tuner, a predictive fine-tuning framework that regularizes the VLA's native policy guidance through action-conditioned future prediction. During fine-tuning, a predictor in JEPA-Tuner learns to match guidance encoded from a future observation given the current guidance and committed actions. This objective encourages a cross-modal guidance adapter to capture how the scene and robot state evolve under those actions, while jointly training the action expert on both observed and predicted guidance keeps the representation informative for control. In addition, when deploying the VLA model with asynchronous action-chunk inference, the pre-trained predictor will anticipate the guidance needed at the next chunk boundary. The policy can thus generate the next chunk in advance and execute it from its first action without overlap correction or additional predictor training, enabling efficient and high-quality asynchronous execution. Experiments on LIBERO and Meta-World show that JEPA-Tuner improves task success under synchronous inference with better multi-modal guidance embeddings. For asynchronous control, JEPA-Tuner improves performance and smoothness across different latency settings on both LIBERO simulator and multi-size real robot demonstrations. The project website with video demos is at https://qujx.github.io/JEPA-Tuner-Website.

</details>


### [38] [DUO Policy: Bridging Human Video Coverage and Robot Precision for Fine-Grained Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29101)
*Mingxi Jia,Patton Jiyang Yin,Haojie Huang,Ivy Xiao He,Xuanru Liu,Gefei Liu,Dian Wang,Stefanie Tellex*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出双执行器策略 DUO Policy，将操作技能解耦为人类视频预训练的自由空间运动策略与机器人稀疏数据训练的精密修正策略，并由门控切换，显著提升精度任务成功率并减少数据采集成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频可低成本扩展机器人数据，但存在具身差距（embodiment gap），导致策略只能学会粗粒度行为，在插入等精密任务上失败。现有方法对两个数据源一视同仁地联合训练/微调，使策略要么在精度关键步骤上吸收具身误差，要么浪费宝贵机器人数据去重学自由空间运动。作者观察到数据源价值依赖于状态：人类数据在低精度需求处有用，机器人数据在精度关键状态附近价值更高。

Method: DUO Policy（Dual-Actor Policy）将操作技能解耦为两部分：一个在人类视频上预训练的"手部策略"负责全局自由空间运动；一个在稀疏手内数据上训练的"机器人策略"负责局部精密修正；并学习一个由人类干预信号驱动的门控（gate）在二者之间切换。

Result: 在K-cup咖啡制作、桌面清理、杂乱糖果分拣等任务上，DUO Policy 在四个仿真任务上平均成功率最高，比基线高12.9个百分点；在三个真实世界任务上高51.7个百分点；并且在采集时间为机器人单独训练的约1/2.75（2.75倍更少）的情况下达到相当性能。

Conclusion: 按状态区分数据源价值、并解耦粗粒度运动与精密修正，可使策略对人类数据中的中等具身误差更鲁棒，并能泛化到机器人数据覆盖范围之外的场景布局，同时显著降低机器人数据采集成本。

Abstract: Human videos promise a scalable way to reduce expensive robot data collection, but the embodiment gap limits the resulting policies to coarse behaviors, and they fail at precise tasks such as insertion. Existing methods co-train or finetune a single policy on both data sources, treating each source as equally useful in every state: the policy either absorbs the embodiment error at precision-critical steps or spends scarce robot data relearning free-space motion. We observe that the value of each source depends on the state: human data is useful where precision requirements are low, while robot data is disproportionately valuable near precision-critical states. We therefore propose \textbf{DUO Policy} (Dual-Actor Policy), which decouples a manipulation skill into a hand policy, pretrained on human videos for global free-space motion, and a robot policy, trained on sparse in-hand data for local precise corrections, with a gate learned from human interventions switching between them. This design is robust to moderate embodiment error in human data and generalizes to scene layouts beyond the coverage of the robot data. On tasks such as K-cup coffee making, desk cleanup, and cluttered candy sorting, DUO Policy outperforms the baseline with the highest average success by 12.9 percentage points over four simulated tasks and by 51.7 points over three real-world tasks, and matches robot-only training with 2.75$\times$ less data-collection time. Project Webpage: https://mingxi-jia.github.io/duo_policy_site/

</details>


### [39] [Your Diffusion-Based Driving Planner Only Needs a Nudge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29123)
*Zilin Wang,Guozheng Ma,Saeed Rahmani,Jiong Yang,Antyanta Bangunharcana,Yiluan Guo,Jakob Nicolaus Foerster,Shimon Whiteson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PlanNudge：冻结预训练的扩散规划器，用轻量 MLP「nudge 策略」在每步输出速度与曲率的二维微调量，可用标准 off-policy RL 在闭环仿真中训练，在 nuPlan 六个设置上以约 1/6 环境步数和不到 1/5 模型算力持平或超越此前最优 RL 微调方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散规划器能刻画人类驾驶的多模态性，但闭环执行中的协变量偏移会使其生成导致碰撞或驶出道路的轨迹。强化学习微调有望缓解该问题，然而扩散规划器的迭代去噪过程与滚动时域执行方式使常规 RL 微调复杂、代价高。作者观察到：只需对规划轨迹做小幅调整，即可避免许多不安全结果。

Method: 基于上述观察，冻结预训练的扩散规划器以保留其学习到的规划能力，另行训练一个轻量「nudge 策略」：在每个规划步，该策略一次前向即以二维向量输出对规划器轨迹在速度与曲率上的调整量。由于两个维度都直接作用于车辆运动，可用标准 off-policy RL 在闭环仿真中训练该 MLP。论文还给出了使用 off-policy RL 后训练自动驾驶规划器时在奖励塑形、价值学习和探索方面的实践指南。

Result: 在基于真实驾驶数据的大规模自动驾驶基准 nuPlan 上，六个设置中 PlanNudge 均持平或超过此前最优 RL 微调方法的最终得分，同时环境步数约为其 1/6、模型计算量不到其 1/5。结果以六次训练运行的均值与标准差报告。

Conclusion: 在冻结扩散规划器之上学习低维、单次前向的调整策略，是一种高效且有效的 RL 后训练方案，可在显著降低采样与算力开销的同时提升闭环安全性表现，并为该范式下的奖励设计、价值学习与探索提供可复用的经验准则。

Abstract: Diffusion-based planners capture the multimodality of human driving behavior well, yet covariate shift in closed-loop driving can lead them to generate trajectories that result in collisions or road departures. Reinforcement learning (RL) finetuning offers a promising way to tackle this issue, but applying it to diffusion-based planners is complicated by their iterative denoising process and receding-horizon execution. We find that small adjustments to the planned trajectories can prevent many of these unsafe outcomes. Motivated by this observation, we propose PlanNudge, which preserves the learned planning capabilities of a pretrained diffusion-based planner by freezing it and trains a lightweight policy that we call the nudge policy. At each planning step, the nudge policy outputs a nudge, a two-dimensional adjustment of the speed and curvature of the plan from the frozen planner. The nudge policy is a multilayer perceptron that predicts the nudge in a single forward pass, and both dimensions directly change the motion of the vehicle, so standard off-policy RL can train it in closed-loop simulation. We evaluate PlanNudge on nuPlan, a large-scale autonomous driving benchmark based on real-world driving data. In all six settings, PlanNudge matches or exceeds the final scores of the previous state-of-the-art RL finetuning method while using about one sixth as many environment steps and less than one fifth as much model compute. We also provide guidelines for reward shaping, value learning, and exploration when using off-policy RL to post-train autonomous driving planners. Our results are reported with means and standard deviations over six training runs. Project page: https://zerlinwang.github.io/plannudge/

</details>


### [40] [Implementation and Software-in-the-Loop Evaluation of a Trans-Domain Digital Twin Architecture for Autonomous Guidance of the Phoenix VTOL UAV Without Online GNSS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29158)
*Mansoorali Amiri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究设计并评估了一套基于跨域数字孪生（TDDT）的 Phoenix 垂直起降无人机自主导航系统，在无卫星定位条件下通过多模型协同闭环实现自主引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在应急响应、基础设施巡检和货运等任务中需要可靠导航，但卫星定位在封闭空间、自然障碍与建筑遮挡的盲区、电子战中的射频干扰与信号欺骗环境下可能失效或不可信，因此需要不依赖卫星定位的自主导航方案。

Method: 构建基于TDDT的系统，将独立导航模型、飞行动力学模型与气象模型纳入"状态估计—预测—决策—指令执行—反馈"的循环中协同运行；利用惯性、磁、气压、空速传感器数据及环境信息进行估计与预测；通过共享状态、时间同步、信息来源保留与指令可追溯性保证模型与决策之间的连续性；在软件在环环境中以仿真物理孪生体进行垂直起降、向目标点移动、着陆与自主停止试验，并依据传感器反馈对动力学模型做有限调整；将评估参考与导航输入分离以独立评估定位误差与计算性能。

Result: 记录了垂直起飞、向目的地移动、着陆与自主停止等飞行过程，动力学模型可基于传感器反馈进行有限范围调整；评估参考与导航输入分离后，可独立评估定位误差与计算性能；结果证实了在所测条件下系统具备运行协同性、决策可追溯性与闭环引导能力。

Conclusion: TDDT 架构能够在无卫星定位条件下实现可协同、可追溯的闭环自主引导，为不依赖卫星定位的导航系统的开发与评估提供了可复现的平台；但实验限于软件在环仿真条件，实际飞行验证仍需后续开展。

Abstract: Autonomous UAV guidance in emergency response, infrastructure monitoring, and cargo transportation requires reliable navigation. However, satellite-based positioning may become unavailable or unreliable in enclosed spaces, blind spots caused by natural obstacles and structures, and environments affected by radio interference and signal spoofing during electronic warfare. This study designs, implements, and evaluates a system based on a Trans-Domain Digital Twin (TDDT) for autonomous guidance of the Phoenix VTOL UAV. Independent navigation, flight dynamics, and meteorological models are coordinated within a cycle of state estimation, prediction, decision-making, command execution, and feedback. Data from inertial, magnetic, pressure, and airspeed sensors, together with environmental information, form the basis for estimation and prediction. Furthermore, a shared state, temporal synchronization, preservation of information provenance, and command traceability establish continuity between models and decisions. The system was tested with a simulated physical twin in a software-in-the-loop environment. Vertical takeoff, movement toward the destination, landing, and autonomous stopping were recorded, and the dynamic model was adjusted to a limited extent based on sensor-derived feedback. Separating the evaluation reference from navigation inputs enabled independent assessment of positioning error and computational performance. The results confirm operational coordination, decision traceability, and closed-loop guidance under the tested conditions, providing a reproducible platform for the development and evaluation of navigation without satellite-based positioning.

</details>


### [41] [FunGrasp-Zero: Field-Conditioned Graph Attention Policy for Zero-Shot Cross-Embodiment Dexterous Functional Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29195)
*Yanhan Lin,Yuliang Wu,Yihao Guo,Wei-Jin Huang,Jianwen Li,Jiawei Tang,Xiaolong Huang,Yuquan Wang,Ancong Wu,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 FunGrasp-Zero，一个面向零样本跨本体功能抓取的强化学习框架，用物体中心的接触图表示功能意图并通过形态映射适配异构灵巧手，配套建立 X-FunBench 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手形态差异巨大（硬件异构），而现有功能抓取方法以重定向后的手部关节姿态作为引导，任务表示与特定本体绑定，无法显式表达以物体为中心的功能性接触意图，导致跨手泛化能力受限，跨本体功能抓取问题基本未被探索。

Method: 1) 以物体为中心、跨本体一致的接触图（contact map）刻画功能意图，避免依赖具体关节配置；2) 通过形态学映射将接触区域迁移到异构手；3) 由映射后的接触区域构建手-物势场，产生吸引与排斥引导向量；4) 提出 Field-Conditioned Graph Attention Network (FC-GAT) 利用该向量协调多指运动；5) 设计势场奖励，引导手指趋向目标区域并惩罚非目标碰撞，以兼顾功能对齐与抓取稳定性；6) 统一策略在四种本体上联合训练；7) 构建 X-FunBench，同时评测物理抓取成功率与功能意图保持度。

Result: 在 X-FunBench 上，联合训练的统一策略在五种未见手、新物体上平均抓取成功率 86%；在四款未见真实手（Leap、Inspire、Sharpa、Wuji）上对新物体抓取，无需微调平均成功率 80%。

Conclusion: FunGrasp-Zero 是首个在仿真与真实世界中均实现跨未见手的零样本跨本体功能抓取的强化学习框架，说明以物体为中心的功能接触表示可作为与本体解耦的任务接口，实现跨异构灵巧手的功能抓取迁移。

Abstract: Dexterous functional grasping is critical for robotic tool use, yet current dexterous hands exhibit substantial morphological diversity. This hardware heterogeneity makes cross-embodiment functional grasping a fundamental but largely unexplored problem. Existing functional grasping methods use retargeted hand poses as guidance, tying task representation to a specific embodiment and failing to explicitly capture object-centric functional contact intent, limiting generalization across hands. To address this, we propose FunGrasp-Zero, a reinforcement learning framework for zero-shot cross-embodiment functional grasping. Instead of relying on embodiment-specific joint configuration, FunGrasp-Zero captures functional intent via an object-centric, cross-embodiment-consistent contact map, adapted to heterogeneous hands through morphological mapping. We construct a hand-object potential field from mapped contact regions, yielding attractive and repulsive guidance vectors. A Field-Conditioned Graph Attention Network (FC-GAT) leverages these vectors to coordinate multi-finger motion across hands, while a potential field reward guides fingers toward target regions and penalizes non-target collisions to enforce functional alignment and stability. We further establish X-FunBench for cross-embodiment functional grasping, evaluating both physical grasp success and functional intent preservation across hands. Evaluated on X-FunBench, our unified policy, co-trained on four embodiments, achieves 86% average grasp success rate across five unseen hands on novel objects. Deployed on four unseen physical hands (Leap, Inspire, Sharpa, Wuji) for novel object grasping, the policy achieves 80% average success rate without fine-tuning. To our knowledge, FunGrasp-Zero is the first RL-based framework to achieve zero-shot cross-embodiment functional grasping across unseen hands in both simulation and the real world.

</details>


### [42] [DriveTTO: Understanding Search and Selection in Test-Time Trajectory Optimization for End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29205)
*Xinyao Rao,Zecheng Tang,Zheyuan Lai,Liuxuan Sun,Minjun Yu,Xun Cao,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出无需训练的DriveTTO方法，在原生轨迹提案周围做邻域搜索与校验，融合轨迹与控制两条分支，提升冻结评分器的选轨质量，在Navtest、Navhard、HUGSIM上取得稳定增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在困难驾驶场景中，固定候选池内的学习型评分器可能选出较差轨迹，即使存在更优候选；周边车辆与道路边界又使高评分可行机动范围很窄，且这些约束随场景变化、难以在训练中穷尽覆盖。

Method: 一种免训练（training-free）方法：围绕原生提案进行邻域搜索，并把每个细化提案与保留轨迹比对。用冻结评分器在其实际到达的端点处评估完整路径，指导逐步收缩的邻域重采样。从同一参考出发，一支利用规划器提案、另一支扰动车辆控制量；在对应时刻混合二者位姿，仅当两个冻结评分器都预测有增益时才接受分支更新或融合更新。

Result: Navtest上平均在51.35%的原生零分案例中恢复出非零PDMS；计入新增失败后，零分率由1.63%降至1.13%。针对基准的配置达到Navtest 95.54 PDMS、92.30 EPDMS，Navhard 57.10 EPDMS，HUGSIM观测指令下40.1 HD-Score。单一冻结评分器下，DriveTTO-S在Navtest与Navhard上均改进全部六个被测规划器。

Conclusion: 通过免训练的邻域搜索与双分支融合机制，DriveTTO可在不重训练评分器的情况下稳定提升轨迹选择与规划质量，且对多种规划器具有通用性。

Abstract: In difficult driving scenes, a learned scorer can select a poor trajectory from a fixed pool even when a better candidate is available. Nearby vehicles and road boundaries can also leave only a narrow range of high-scoring maneuvers. These constraints vary with the scene and are difficult to cover exhaustively during training. We introduce DriveTTO, a training-free method that searches around native proposals and checks each proposed refinement against a retained trajectory. A frozen scorer evaluates completed paths at the endpoints they actually reach, guiding resampling in progressively smaller neighborhoods. From a shared reference, one branch draws on planner proposals and another perturbs vehicle controls. DriveTTO blends their poses at matching times and accepts a branch or fusion update only when both frozen scorers predict a gain. On Navtest, DriveTTO recovers nonzero predictive driver model scores (PDMS) in 51.35% of native zero-score cases on average. Accounting for new failures, the zero-score rate falls from 1.63% to 1.13%. Benchmark-specific configurations achieve 95.54 PDMS and 92.30 extended PDMS (EPDMS) on Navtest, 57.10 EPDMS on Navhard, and 40.1 HD-Score on HUGSIM under observed commands. With a single frozen scorer, DriveTTO-S improves all six evaluated planners on both Navtest and Navhard.

</details>


### [43] [Force2Act: A Force-Aware Visuomotor Policy for Generalizable Compliant Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29208)
*Keerthi Vasan Murugan,Fangguo Zhao,Jiafei Duan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Force2Act混合力-位策略：全局力策略按动作块规划力（相对测得接触力表示，使规划力随负载缩放），块内用Mixture of Force Experts反应式调节接触力；并发布Force2Act-Bench基准，仿真成功率93.8%（最强基线77.0%），真机四任务平均94.2%（基线≤27.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触密集操作不仅需要控制位置，还需调节施加的力。现有force-aware策略从示教中学习力，只能复现训练时的力水平；一旦交互所需力超过任何示教，就会失败。因此需要一种能在示教力之外按需求缩放并可在线调节的力控制策略。

Method: 双机制混合力-位策略：1）全局力策略每个动作块规划一次运动与交互力，力以相对已测得交互力的形式表示，使规划力随负载缩放；2）在规划步之间，反应式柔顺策略（Mixture of Force Experts）在每个控制步识别主导当前交互的力专家并经由其调节力，保证块内力变化时接触仍能保持。此外构建Force2Act-Bench仿真基准，含五项接触密集任务，用于可重复评测力感知策略的力调节能力。

Result: 仿真中Force2Act总体成功率93.8%，最强基线为77.0%；反应式调节将block pivoting失败从150次试验中的30次降至10次。真机上四个任务平均成功率94.2%，基线最高仅27.5%。

Conclusion: 通过全局力规划（相对力表示）与块内反应式力专家选择的结合，Force2Act突破了示教力水平的限制，在仿真与真机上均显著优于基线；Force2Act-Bench为社区提供了统一的力感知策略评测手段。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires a robot to regulate the force it applies, not only where it moves. Existing force-aware policies learn this force from demonstrations and therefore reproduce the force levels they were trained on, which fails once the interaction demands more force than any demonstration. We present Force2Act, a hybrid force-position policy built on two complementary mechanisms. A global force policy plans the motion and the interaction force once per action chunk, with force represented relative to the measured interaction, so that the planned force scales with the load. Between planning steps, a reactive compliance policy, termed Mixture of Force Experts, identifies at every control step the force expert that governs the interaction and regulates the force through it, so that the contact holds when it changes within a chunk. We further introduce Force2Act-Bench, a simulation benchmark of five contact-rich tasks that gives the community a common and repeatable way to test force-aware policies and measure how well they regulate the interaction force. Force2Act achieves an overall success rate of 93.8%, compared with 77.0% for the strongest baseline, and its reactive regulation reduces the failures on block pivoting from 30 to 10 of 150 trials. On a real robot, Force2Act reaches an average success rate of 94.2% over four tasks, against at most 27.5% for the baselines.

</details>


### [44] [InfoField3D: Differentiable Information Field for Embodied Task-Oriented View Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29230)
*Huan Lyu,Yawei Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: InfoField3D 将任务相关信息提升为可微解析场，用低阶球谐函数编码方向响应、用高斯占据函数的解析线积分建模遮挡，从而把任务驱动的相机选点转化为带约束的连续优化问题，实现高效的视角搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体执行任务时需要选择能提供充分任务相关信息的观测位置。现有方法要么用学习模型直接预测视角，难以引入可达性等新约束；要么对离散候选逐一打分搜索，代价高。

Method: 构建几何绑定的可微解析信息场 InfoField3D：其值表示该 3D 观测位置可获取的任务所需信息。用低阶球谐函数编码方向性信息响应，用高斯占据函数的解析线积分刻画视线方向上的遮挡。该形式化把相机放置问题转成成熟的约束连续优化问题，约束通过可行域灵活加入。

Result: 实验表明 InfoField3D 能高保真、紧凑地保留逐点信息景观，并以更低搜索成本给出比基线更高质量、更鲁棒的视角。在冻结 VLA 策略的受控评测中，推荐视角可在初始视角信息受限时改善下游任务表现。

Conclusion: 将信息几何化为可微解析场，可统一处理遮挡与约束，并以更低成本获得更优视角，且对下游策略有实际收益。

Abstract: Effective task execution requires embodied agents to select observation positions that provide sufficient task-relevant information. Existing approaches typically predict viewpoints with learned models or score discrete candidates, but they either struggle to incorporate new constraints such as reachability or require extensive candidate evaluation to search. We introduce InfoField3D, which lifts geometry-bound information into a differentiable analytic field, whose value at each 3D observation position represents the task-required information accessible there. It uses low-order spherical harmonics to encode directional information responses and analytic line integrals of Gaussian occupancy functions to model occlusion along lines of sight. This formulation casts task-driven camera placement as a well-studied constrained continuous optimization problem, enabling both flexible incorporation of constraints through the feasible domain and efficient viewpoint search even in the presence of occlusion. Experiments show that InfoField3D compactly preserves the pointwise information landscape with high fidelity and generally recommends higher-quality, more robust viewpoints at lower search cost than the evaluated baselines. Controlled evaluations with a frozen vision-language-action (VLA) policy further suggest that the recommended views can improve downstream task performance under information-limited initial viewpoints.

</details>


### [45] [Tacinf: A Unified Tactile Infrastructure for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29240)
*Qiwei Wu,Xiangyu Chen,Xin Xiang,Rui Zhang,Yihang Kang,Weiran Chen,Zikang Xie,Yangfan Li,Tao Wang,Xiangzheng Wang,Tianyi Bi,Zhiyan Zhang,Xianpeng Wang,Mingqian Zhou,Yan Yu,Chuanyu Li,Wenjie Zhang,Tao Li,Weihua Zhang,Renjing Xu,Junjie Lai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出Tacinf——一套低成本、模块化的力感知操作基础设施，打通仿真数据合成、触觉基准测试、视觉-触觉-语言-动作策略学习、手持数据采集与仿真到真机评估，可在仅用触觉仿真数据微调的情况下实现零样本真机迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 力感知操作要求机器人从任务语境和触觉反馈中推断合适的交互力，而现有工作缺乏低成本、模块化且覆盖"采集-训练-部署-评估"全链路的统一基础设施。

Method: 构建Tacinf，包含四个组件：仿真数据合成与触觉基准Tabero；VTLA（视觉-触觉-语言-动作）策略学习；与部署同款夹爪的手持采集设备Tacumi；任务对齐的仿真到真机评估Taco。任务指令可选地指定期望力水平，策略同时预测动作与指尖力。对各组件分别进行验证，并开源整套方案。

Result: 仅用触觉仿真数据微调的基础模型可零样本迁移至不同真机任务；8种策略变体在仿真与真机上的平均任务成功率高度相关（Pearson r=0.998，MMRV=0.010）；4个报告力测量的策略在三个任务、两个域中，"用力"指令下的平均抓握力均高于"轻柔"指令；在物体位置与实例同时随机化时，遥操作+仿真+Tacumi混合数据将真机平均成功率从20%（仅遥操作）提升至60%。

Conclusion: Tacinf验证了低成本模块化力感知基础设施的可行性：仿真数据与真机结果高度一致，语言力指令可有效调控抓握力，多源数据混合显著提升泛化成功率，全套件已开源以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Force-aware manipulation requires robots to infer appropriate interaction forces from task context and tactile feedback. We present Tacinf, a low-cost, modular infrastructure that enables policies to predict robot actions and fingertip forces from task language, vision, and touch. Task instructions can optionally specify the desired force level. Tacinf integrates simulation data synthesis and tactile benchmarking (Tabero), vision-tactile-language-action (VTLA) policy learning, handheld data collection using the same gripper as robot deployment (Tacumi), and task-aligned sim-to-real evaluation (Taco). Together, these components form an integrated workflow connecting data collection from multiple sources, foundation-model training, and hardware deployment. We validate each component, including the simulation data, benchmark, controller, and Tacumi, and are releasing the full stack as open source. A foundation model fine-tuned solely on tactile simulation data transfers zero-shot to different real-robot tasks. Across eight policy variants, mean task success rates are strongly correlated between simulation and the real robot (Pearson r=0.998, mean maximum rank violation MMRV=0.010). For the four policies with reported force measurements, mean grip force is higher under firm instructions than under gentle instructions on all three tasks in both domains. When both object positions and instances are randomized, a mixture of teleoperation, simulation, and Tacumi data raises mean real-robot task success from 20% with teleoperation alone to 60%.

</details>


### [46] [$ζ_0$: A Framework for World Action Models with Joint Scene and Body Foresight in Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29277)
*Yizhi Chen,Zhanxiang Cao,Yitong Wang,Junyu Liu,Hongchu Zeng,Xinyi Peng,Chuxuan Huang,Yicheng Su,Zihao Zhou,Shengcheng Fu,Haoyang Li,Liyun Yan,Tianyue Zhan,Jianming Ma,Yang Zhang,Keqi Zhu,Xiaxi Si,Jinming Yao,Qiao Sun,Shizeng Zhang,Jiangwei Zhong,Yue Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ζ0 框架，通过在同一潜空间中预测未来视觉特征与实际关节轨迹，把 VLA 策略升级为 World Action Model，在仿真与真机人形任务上显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 World Action Model 只做视觉结果预测来指导动作，但人形机器人的交互还依赖本体（身体）如何响应；纯视觉前瞻会忽略身体运动，而潜空间距离损失又没有直接监督动作响应本身。因此需要同时预测视觉未来与可测量的关节轨迹，并对动作响应进行直接监督。

Method: ζ0 在共享潜表示中同时预测未来视觉特征与实测关节轨迹，将 VLA 策略转化为 World Action Model。训练时引入一个以真实未来为条件的教师模型提供参考动作响应，并采用"双响应迁移"（dual-response transfer）让因果预测器分别经由教师和执行策略去匹配该参考响应；所有组件联合训练。部署阶段仅使用因果预测得到的未来，不依赖真实未来信息。

Result: 在 7 个 HumanoidArena 任务上，基于 Ψ0 与 GR00T N1.7 的 ζ0 平均成功率为 63.6% 和 79.5%，分别高出基线 18.8 与 9.0 个百分点；在 6 个真实世界任务上达到 78.3% 和 85.0%，分别提升 16.7 与 10.0 个百分点。在 GR00T 上，79.5% 的成绩超过了所有对比的七任务联合策略。

Conclusion: 将视觉前瞻与本体关节轨迹预测统一在共享潜空间，并辅以双响应迁移监督，是提升人形机器人交互性能的有效途径；该方法可即插即用地增强多种 VLA 主干，且在仿真与真机上均取得一致提升。

Abstract: World Action Models anticipate visual outcomes to guide robot actions, yet humanoid interaction also depends on how the robot's body responds. Visual foresight alone can miss body motion, while latent-distance losses do not directly supervise action responses. We present $ζ_0$, a framework that turns vision-language-action policies into World Action Models by foreseeing future visual features and measured joint trajectories in a shared latent representation. During training, a teacher conditioned on recorded futures provides a reference action response, and dual-response transfer trains the causal predictor to match it through the teacher and the executing policy. All components are trained jointly, and deployment uses only causally predicted futures. On seven HumanoidArena tasks, $ζ_0$ built on $Ψ_0$ and GR00T N1.7 reaches 63.6% and 79.5% mean success, 18.8 and 9.0 points above these bases. On six real-world tasks, it reaches 78.3% and 85.0%, gains of 16.7 and 10.0 points. On GR00T, its 79.5% tops all compared seven-task joint policies. Project page: https://chenyizhi123.github.io/zeta0-project/

</details>


### [47] [A2I-VLA: A Two-Stage Vision-Language-Action model with Semantic-Physical Alignment and Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29279)
*Kun Dai,Tianqi Wang,Haibo Lei,Chaoyang Li,Kai Zhang,Fuyuan Qiu,Lijun Zhao,Ruifeng Li,Chuqing Cao,Tao Xie,Wenming Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: A2I-VLA 是一个两阶段视觉-语言-动作（VLA）框架：阶段一学习语义对齐的力/触觉表征，阶段二通过门控交叉注意力桥接模块将其注入 VLA 模型，在三个接触密集型真实操作任务上把成功率从基线 PI05 的 70% 提升到 95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触密集型操作中，仅靠视觉难以可靠捕捉物理交互信息，力与触觉反馈不可或缺；但视觉-语言语义与物理测量之间存在表示鸿沟，使力/触觉信号难以直接融入 VLA 模型。论文因此提出先"语义对齐"再"集成"的两阶段方案。

Method: 两阶段设计。阶段一：引入跨模态对齐损失，拉近语义表示与物理表示的对应关系；用层次化多模态融合网络整合触觉与力两条特征流，并以未来力旋量预测损失（促使模型预判物理响应）与动作预测损失联合监督。阶段二：设计门控交叉注意力桥接模块，将阶段一学到的层次化物理特征自适应地注入 VLA 模型以指导动作生成。

Result: 在三个接触密集型真实机器人操作任务上显著优于基线 PI05，成功率由 70% 提升至 95%。

Conclusion: 通过"语义对齐的力-触觉表征学习 + 门控跨注意力特征注入"的分阶段策略，可有效弥合语义与物理之间的表示鸿沟，使力/触觉信息被 VLA 模型高效利用，显著提升接触密集型操作的成功率，为多模态物理反馈与视觉-语言-动作模型的结合提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Force and tactile feedback provide essential information about physical interactions in contact-rich manipulation that visual observations alone cannot reliably capture.However, integrating these signals into vision-language-action (VLA) models remains challenging owing to the representational gap between visual-language semantics and physical measurements.To address this challenge, we propose A2I-VLA, a two-stage VLA framework that first learns semantically Aligned force-tactile representations and then Integrates them into a VLA model.In Stage I, a cross-modal alignment loss is introduced to promote correspondence between semantic and physical representations. The tactile and force feature streams are then integrated through a hierarchical multimodal fusion network, and the resulting representations are jointly supervised by future wrench and action prediction losses. The former encourages the model to anticipate physical responses, while the latter associates force-tactile observations with task-relevant manipulation behaviors.In Stage II, a gated cross-attention bridge module adaptively integrates the hierarchical physical features learned in Stage I into the VLA model to guide action generation.Real-world experiments demonstrate that the proposed A2I-VLA significantly outperforms the baseline PI05 across three contact-rich manipulation tasks, improving the success rate from 70% to 95%.

</details>


### [48] [RSLaA: Self-Reconfiguring Roadside LiDARs as Agents for 3D Traffic Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29294)
*Yang Ma,Yichen Sun,Yubing Zheng,Zhongxiang Feng,Zeyang Cheng,Said M. Easa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 RSLaA 框架，把路侧激光雷达视为可自适应调整位姿的“智能体”，在决策时间窗内依据历史观测构建的概率占据栅格所计算的替代指标预测下一窗感知性能，并据此用 MPC 或 MAPPO 在线调整部署位姿，实验证明自适应配置优于静态部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路侧激光雷达协同感知可扩展自动驾驶车辆感知范围、缓解车端遮挡，但基础设施部署成本高。现有工作仅依据道路几何与聚合交通统计做一次性离线优化并长期固定，而交通流本质是动态的，平均意义上的最优布设在任一具体时刻可能远非最优。

Method: 提出 RSLaA 框架：决策时刻无真值标签，因此仅用历史观测的概率占据栅格计算替代指标，预测候选位姿在下一窗的感知表现。构建协同仿真基准，将五种候选替代指标与检测器无关的真值感知标签在每一决策窗配对比较，筛出多视角覆盖指标 J_mv 作为最可靠预测器；在此基础上实现两种自适应配置方法：滚动时域 MPC 优化器与轻量级 MAPPO 多智能体强化学习（集中训练、分散执行）。

Result: 在 10 个场景点位与 89 个六小时交通场景（共 890 次评估运行）上，自适应配置相较静态部署持续提升综合感知评分；J_mv 被识别为五种替代指标中最可靠的下一窗性能预测器。

Conclusion: 结果表明路侧激光雷达从“固定设施”转变为可自主调整的“智能体”是有益的；仅依赖历史占据栅格导出的校验替代指标即可支撑在线位姿决策，MPC 与 MAPPO 两类方法均能带来稳定增益。

Abstract: Cooperative perception with roadside LiDARs (RSLs) extends the sensing range of autonomous vehicles and resolves onboard-sensor occlusions, particularly at curves and intersections. Because infrastructure deployment is costly, existing work optimizes RSL configurations once, against road geometry and aggregated traffic statistics, and treats them as fixed thereafter. Traffic flow, however, is inherently dynamic, and a placement optimal on average can be far from optimal at any given moment. We introduce RSLaA (Roadside LiDARs as Agents), a framework in which each RSL adaptively adjusts its pose to the evolving traffic scene over decision-time windows. Since ground truth is unavailable at decision time, the framework centers on surrogate metrics computed solely from a probabilistic occupancy grid of historical observations, which predicts the next-window perception performance of candidate poses. We construct a co-simulation benchmark pairing five candidate surrogates with detector-agnostic ground-truth perception labels at every decision window, identifying the multi-view coverage metric J_mv as the most reliable predictor. Upon this validated surrogate we build two adaptive configuration methods, a receding-horizon (MPC) optimizer and a lightweight MAPPO-based multi-agent reinforcement learning scheme with centralized training and decentralized execution. Across 10 sites and 89 six-hour traffic scenarios (890 evaluation runs), adaptive configuration consistently improves the composite perception score over static deployment. Our results indicate that RSLs could be benefitted from being treated as agents.

</details>


### [49] [A Glimpse of Training VLMs as Foundation Models for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29338)
*Meng-Hao Guo,Yi Zhang,Zhe-Han Mo,Han-Zheng Wang,Jia-Jun Wang,Xin-Sheng Chen,Jiaxi Song,Yi-Xuan Deng,Jia-Ning Li,Xuanyu Chu,Hengrui Zhang,Huan-ang Gao,Yongming Rao,Shi-Min Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出nanoAstra，将机器人行为表示为含可复用参数化动作原语的语义步骤，并结合能力保持的监督微调（CP-SFT），在5000+小时机器人数据与通用VQA数据上训练VLM，实现可泛化的操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPT-6 Astra在机器人控制上展现出很强的泛化能力，但其训练细节披露有限，难以复现。作者希望给出一种可复现的、面向通用可操作任务的视觉语言模型训练方案。

Method: 1）语义动作建模：把机器人行为表达为由可复用、参数化动作原语组成的语义步骤，并以显式的进度描述进行引导；2）CP-SFT：用显式的机器人专用system prompt把操作输出约束到结构化动作空间，使适配集中在机器人相关语境，同时尽量不干扰预训练的通用能力；3）混合5000+小时机器人数据与通用VQA数据训练。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0随机化基准上，nanoAstra-9B取得17.8%的零样本成功率，显著优于先进VLA、WAM基线以及同规模VLM。在单张H20 GPU上推理速度快于对应运动执行，支持类似action chunk的流式执行，将机器人空闲时间从35.0%降至6.9%。

Conclusion: 语义动作建模与CP-SFT相结合，可在保持通用能力的同时获得较强的零样本操作泛化性，并借助快速推理实现推理与运动重叠的流式执行，显著提升机器人利用率。

Abstract: Recently, GPT-6 Astra demonstrates strong generalization in robot control, yet limited disclosure of its training leaves open how such capabilities can be reproduced. We introduce nanoAstra, an approach to training vision-language models (VLMs) for generalizable manipulation that combines semantic action modeling with capability-preserving supervised fine-tuning (CP-SFT). nanoAstra represents robot behavior as semantic steps composed of reusable, parameterized action primitives, guided by explicit progress descriptions. Meanwhile, CP-SFT uses an explicit robot-specific system prompt to constrain manipulation outputs to a structured action space, aiming to focus adaptation on robot-specific contexts and promote specialization while minimizing interference with broader pretrained capabilities. With this formulation, we train nanoAstra on more than 5,000 hours of robot data together with general VQA data. On RoboTwin 2.0's randomized benchmark, nanoAstra-9B achieves a 17.8% zero-shot success rate, substantially outperforming advanced VLA and WAM baselines as well as similarly sized VLMs. With inference faster than the corresponding motion execution on a single H20 GPU, our model supports action-chunk-like streaming execution that overlaps inference with motion, reducing the robot's idle time from 35.0% to 6.9%.

</details>


### [50] [More Than Picturing the Future: Motion-Oriented Latent Reasoning for Generalizable Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29339)
*Xin Zhang,Yushuo Hou,Anqi Zhao,Moyun Liu,Xingwei Zhao,Bo Tao,Jiawei Du,Joey Tianyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出运动导向潜在推理（Motion-Oriented Latent Reasoning），将VLA模型的视觉潜在从"预测整帧外观"转向"建模场景朝目标演化中的动作交互"，显著提升分布内与分布外泛化成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉前瞻式潜在推理沿用VLM范式，将每个潜变量对齐到孤立的未来帧（甚至叠加该帧的其他模态信息），服务于"理解"而非"行动"，导致策略被训练场景的外观绑定，场景一旦变化性能急剧下降。作者认为面向动作的视觉潜变量应刻画场景如何朝目标演化，而非未来帧长什么样。

Method: 先由语义潜变量沿具身思维链的阶段确定"做什么、怎么做"；再以其为条件，由粗到细预测各未来步骤的运动导向潜变量：从全局场景，到由操作对象及其目标位置构成的交互区域，再到机器人状态。

Result: 相比SOTA潜在VLA方法LaST₀：SimplerEnv-WidowX上平均成功率由37.5%提升至55.2%（分布内），由22.4%提升至30.2%（分布外偏移）；AgileX Piper双臂平台上由60.8%提升至90.8%（训练场景），由6.7%提升至37.5%（环境变化）。

Conclusion: 视觉潜变量应为动作建模场景演化而非帧外观重建；语义—运动分层、由粗到细的潜在推理能有效解耦外观偏置，在仿真与真实双臂平台上均大幅优于现有潜在VLA基线，尤其在外观与环境偏移下泛化更稳健。

Abstract: Latent reasoning lets Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models deliberate at a fraction of the cost of explicit chain-of-thought, increasingly through visual foresight, which we show to be indispensable for predicting actions. Yet this paradigm is inherited from Vision-Language Models, where it serves understanding rather than action: each latent is aligned with an isolated future frame, often enriched with further modalities of that frame. We find that this binds the policy to the appearance of its training scenes, so that it degrades sharply once the scene changes. We argue that visual latents for action should capture not what a future frame looks like, but how the scene evolves toward the goal. We therefore propose Motion-Oriented Latent Reasoning, which redirects visual latents from the whole frame to the interaction that the action must bring about. Semantic latents, following the stages of embodied chain-of-thought, first establish what to accomplish and how. Conditioned on them, motion-oriented latents anticipate each future step from coarse to fine: from the global scene, to the interaction region spanned by the manipulated object and its destination, to the robot state. Relative to the state-of-the-art latent VLA LaST$_0$, our method raises the mean success rate on SimplerEnv-WidowX from 37.5% to 55.2% in distribution and from 22.4% to 30.2% under out-of-distribution shifts, and on an AgileX Piper dual-arm platform from 60.8% to 90.8% in training scenes and from 6.7% to 37.5% under environmental changes.

</details>


### [51] [Specification-Aware Sparse Differentiation: Planning under Signal Temporal Logic with Sparsemax Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29346)
*Sai Sandeep Damera,Ryan Matheu,John S. Baras,Calin Belta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出直接从STL任务规范推导稀疏模式的方法，以利用可证明稀疏且可靠的SparseMax鲁棒性算子，使基于梯度的STL最优控制中Jacobian/Hessian稀疏化，在双臂协调任务上实现最多27倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于梯度的STL最优控制需求解非线性规划，其约束是轨迹光滑鲁棒函数；自动微分若不利用稀疏性，会完整计算求解器所需的Jacobian和Hessian，导致问题计算上不可行。CasADi等标准工具只能通过结构检测恢复稀疏模式，但无法识别SparseMax这类在数据相关阈值处导数恰为零的"轨迹相关稀疏性"。

Method: 1) 提出从任务规范直接规定稀疏模式的方法，依据STL语法、时序算子窗口及谓词读取的变量确定模式；2) 利用SparseMax这一可证明稀疏且可靠的光滑鲁棒算子，其在等于或低于数据相关阈值处导数精确为零；3) 推导暴露该轨迹相关稀疏性的模式以支持稀疏自动微分，并引入余量检验用于模式复用；4) 将Hessian限制在每条约束支撑的变量上；5) 采用精确Hessian内点法求解。

Result: 在双臂协调任务上验证：两臂共享划分为若干单元的手递区域，每次计划访问对应一条until约束，使另一臂在访问者离开前不得进入该单元。两个场景为5单元区域和64单元圆形区域，包含单次求解与刚体仿真上的闭环重规划。精确Hessian内点法求解比CasADi与IPOPT中使用可靠log-sum-exp鲁棒性的方法最多快27倍。

Conclusion: 直接从STL规范规定稀疏模式、并结合SparseMax的轨迹相关稀疏性，可显著加速基于梯度的STL最优控制；余量检验保证模式复用的可靠性，闭环重规划验证了方法的实用性。

Abstract: Gradient-based optimal control under a Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specification involves solving a nonlinear program whose constraints are smoothed robustness functions of the trajectory, and automatic differentiation without knowledge of sparsity computes the Jacobian and the Hessian that its solver needs in full. This often renders the problem computationally intractable. The dynamics and the specification prescribe a sparsity pattern through the STL syntax, the windows of the temporal operators and the variables that the predicates read. Standard tools such as CasADi recover this pattern by structural detection. In this work, we propose a method to prescribe such a pattern directly from the task specification. We show that our method allows us to exploit SparseMax, a recently proposed smooth robustness operator that is certifiably sparse and sound. Its derivative is exactly zero at every operand at or below a data-dependent threshold, which structural detection cannot see. We derive the patterns that expose this trajectory-dependent sparsity to sparse automatic differentiation, with a margin test for their reuse and Hessians confined to the variables of each constraint's support. We demonstrate the method on a dual-arm coordination task in which two arms share a hand-off zone divided into cells and one until constraint per scheduled visit keeps the other arm out of a cell until the visitor has left it. The two scenes are a zone of five cells and a circle of 64 cells, with single solves and closed-loop re-planning on a rigid-body simulation. Our exact-Hessian interior-point solves are up to 27 times faster than those with the sound log-sum-exp robustness in CasADi and IPOPT.

</details>


### [52] [Contact Harness: Enabling VLM Agents to Perform Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29377)
*Shilin Shan,Yuxuan Hu,Chuhao Zhou,Yimeng Dong,Bohan Hou,Jiaqi Bai,Boyu Ma,Jiahua Dong,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Contact Harness 框架：用可复用的优化技能引导 VLM 智能体，结合力/触觉的物理语义、任务阶段与视觉证据评估接触状态并选择动作，配合柔顺执行机制，无需微调、示教或仿真训练即可实现零样本真机接触丰富操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于预训练 VLM 的智能体已能零样本控制真实机器人，但仅靠视觉难以可靠判断接触丰富操作中的接触状态；力与触觉信号虽有互补性，却存在语义歧义，智能体难以直接利用。因此需要按物理意义、结合任务阶段与视觉证据来解释这些信号。

Method: Contact Harness：以可复用、经过优化的"技能"（prompt skill）引导 VLM 智能体从多模态反馈判断接触状态并选择后续动作；引入柔顺执行机制，利用实时力反馈调节接触中的运动。整个框架不需要模型微调、任务示教或仿真训练。

Result: 在 Rokae 与 Franka 机器人上用 GPT-6 Astra 开展零样本真机评测：单一 prompt skill 下 15 种连接器的完全插入率达 90.7%，并在真实 PC 上考察光照、背景和位置变化；在擦白板、书写、剥离、撬转等四项附加接触丰富任务上平均成功率 100%；六个仿真任务成功率 96.7%，而经任务示教微调的 VLA 基线最高仅 68.3%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，避免微调与示教的智能体式操作在接触丰富任务中具备很强的零样本泛化与真机部署潜力，为真实世界落地提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Agents built on pretrained VLMs have demonstrated zero-shot control of real robots, but vision alone is often insufficient for reliable contact-state assessment in contact-rich manipulation. Force and tactile feedback provide complementary information, but their semantic ambiguity makes them difficult for agents to use directly. These signals must be interpreted according to their physical meaning, together with task phase and visual evidence. We propose Contact Harness, which uses reusable, optimized skills to guide a VLM agent in assessing contact states from multimodal feedback and selecting subsequent actions. A compliant execution mechanism regulates in-contact motions using real-time force feedback. The framework requires no model fine-tuning, task demonstrations, or simulation training. We conduct zero-shot real-robot evaluations using GPT-6 Astra on Rokae and Franka robots. With a single prompt skill, Contact Harness achieves a full-insertion rate of 90.7% across 15 connector types, with further evaluations on a real PC under variations in lighting, background, and position. Across four additional contact-rich tasks, including whiteboard wiping, writing, peeling, and pivoting, Contact Harness achieves an average success rate of 100%. On six simulated tasks, Contact Harness reaches 96.7% success, compared with at most 68.3% for pretrained VLA baselines fine-tuned on task demonstrations, showing the potential of agent-based manipulation for contact-rich real-world deployment.

</details>


### [53] [Self-Refining VLA Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29383)
*Jeff Duan,Arjun Sharma,Jennifer Cruden,Vansh Tibrewal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将视频生成中的 Predict-and-Perturb 自精炼方法适配到 VLA 机器人策略的动作生成，无需大规模候选集成或额外校正器，即可提升 LIBERO 任务成功率并给出与失败强相关的确定性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于流的 VLA 模型从随机噪声迭代生成动作，不同噪声会导致不同轨迹与任务结果；现有测试时缩放方法依赖大规模候选集成、聚合或训练外部校正器，计算与训练成本高。因此作者希望用无需额外训练的自精炼手段在测试时提升动作质量。

Method: 把原本用于视频生成模式搜索的 Predict-and-Perturb（P&P）迁移到机器人领域，并针对机器人动作数据与视频数据在底层结构上的差异，对视频版精炼流程进行修改；用 SmolVLA 在 LIBERO 上做评测，并利用回合级 P&P 不确定性做失败预测检验。

Result: 在 LIBERO 的 400 个回合上，P&P 相比原始基线提升 5.3 个百分点；回合级 P&P 不确定性对任务失败的预测 AUC 为 0.851，显示出强相关性。

Conclusion: P&P 作为一种轻量的测试时自精炼策略可有效改善 VLA 动作生成，且其不确定性可作为可靠的失败预警信号；但结论目前仅基于单一模型与单一基准，泛化性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models typically use flow-based generative modeling to learn a rich, often multimodal, distribution of robot actions. Generating samples from flow-based methods involves iteratively updating random noise, and distinct noise samples from the same state can result in distinct trajectories and task outcomes. Existing test-time scaling methods improve action chunk generation via selection, aggregation, or correction, but commonly require the generation of large ensembles of candidates or the training of an external corrector. We adapt Predict-and-Perturb (P&P), a self-refinement procedure for flow-based models originally used for mode-seeking in video generation, to the robotics domain. We find that applying P&P to VLA models requires modifications to the refinement process used in video generation due to differences in the underlying structure of the data. Across 400 episodes of LIBERO evaluated using SmolVLA, P&P improves upon the stock baseline by 5.3 percentage points. Further, episode-level P\&P uncertainty correlates strongly with task failure (AUC 0.851).

</details>


### [54] [Phase-Conditioned Action Contracts for Auditable Microsurgical Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29426)
*Jiaxi You,Yiwei Shi,Yanlin Li,Rihui Song,Weibing Li,Kai Huang,Weiru Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"阶段条件可执行策略"，将学习式动作提议与确定性指令生成分离，以解决分阶段机器人手术中固定维度动作在不同阶段语义漂移的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分阶段机器人流程会复用固定维度的策略输出，但各阶段可允许的运动及其坐标的物理含义会发生变化，即"动作语义漂移"（action-semantic shift）。若不对其显式建模，输出的动作可能在该阶段不可行，影响安全性与可审计性。

Method: 方法将策略拆分为两部分：(1) 阶段特定的头网络生成动作提议；(2) 动作契约（action contract）剔除不可行分量、施加状态相关边界约束，并将剩余动作解码为阶段相关的任务空间指令。另引入面向失败的局部修复机制，以及只读的来源追溯（provenance trace），支持指令的精确重放与规则级审计。

Result: 在三个留出的视网膜注射评测块上，方法达到 94.27±0.64% 的流程完成率、35 μm 内 64.40±2.43% 的成功率，且无硬性违规。将契约与解码器应用于同一冻结的 Correction 网络后，完成率由 12.27% 提升至 90.47%。追溯记录可精确重构 96,350 条指令，全部 1,996 次绑定干预均符合其声明效果。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在可审计的分阶段显微手术控制中，显式建模可执行动作语义具有重要价值；仅对既有网络施加契约与解码即可带来显著性能提升。

Abstract: Staged robotic procedures may reuse a fixed-dimensional policy output even though the admissible motions and physical meanings of its coordinates change across phases. We call this action-semantic shift. To address it, we propose a phase-conditioned executable policy that separates learned action proposal from deterministic command formation. Phase-specific heads generate proposals, while an action contract removes inadmissible components, enforces state-dependent bounds, and decodes the remaining action into phase-dependent task-space commands. We further introduce failure-focused local repair and a read-only provenance trace for exact command replay and rule-level auditing. Across three held-out retinal-injection evaluation blocks, our method achieves $94.27\pm0.64\%$ procedural completion and $64.40\pm2.43\%$ success within $35\,μ\mathrm{m}$, with no hard violations. Applying the contract and decoder to the same frozen Correction network raises completion from $12.27\%$ to $90.47\%$. The trace exactly reconstructs 96,350 commands, and all 1,996 binding interventions follow their declared effects. These results show the importance of explicit executable action semantics for auditable staged microsurgical control.

</details>


### [55] [What Test-Time Training Corrects in Latent World Models? A Slow-Fast Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29453)
*Shuaicheng Niu,Guohao Chen,Kevin Z. Shen,Yunbei Zhang,Peilin Zhao,Yinan Zhang,Cyril Leung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文将世界模型在分布偏移下的预测误差分解为"跨动作共享的偏移"与"动作相关分量",发现AdaJEPA的规划增益主要来自对共享偏移的跟踪(占91%),且偏移的episode特异性部分不应跨回合保留,而重复出现的部分应长期积累,据此提出快慢双学习器的sft-JEPA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜空间世界模型通过预测候选动作结果来规划,但在分布偏移下预测误差会误导规划器。测试时训练虽有效,但"它纠正了什么"以及"更新是否应跨episode保留"尚不清楚。作者的动机是厘清这两点,从而设计更合理的测试时更新机制。

Method: 在PushObj推动物体任务上,从同一状态探查多个动作,将预测误差分解为跨动作共享偏移与动作相关分量;分别检验共享偏移对规划增益的贡献、偏移的episode特异性与跨episode重复性。基于分析结论,提出sft-JEPA:一个快速学习器逐episode跟踪偏移并在回合结束时重置,一个慢速LoRA学习器跨episode累积反复出现的知识。

Result: 仅共享偏移即可解释AdaJEPA约91%的规划增益;偏移中episode特异部分跨回合携带无益甚至有害,而剩余重复出现的偏移及动作相关误差需从多episode中学习保留。sft-JEPA在PushObj、PointMaze、RoboCasa零样本规划(配合DINO-WM)及OGBench(配合LeWAM)上平均指标全部领先,规划成功率较AdaJEPA最高提升22个点。

Conclusion: 测试时训练的有效性主要来自对共享预测偏移的校正,而校正的持久性应依据误差的时间尺度区分:快速、回合内重置的偏移跟踪与慢速、跨回合累积的知识学习相结合,能带来更稳健且更强的规划性能。sft-JEPA通过解析驱动的快慢双学习器设计验证了这一原则。

Abstract: Latent world models plan toward a goal by predicting the outcomes of candidate actions, but under the distribution shift, prediction errors can lead the planner astray. Test-time training using executed actions helps, yet what it corrects and whether its updates should persist across episodes remains unclear. We address both by analyzing the world model's prediction error. On PushObj, an object-pushing task, we probe multiple actions from the same state and decompose the error into an offset shared across actions and an action-dependent component. The offset effectively shifts the planner's goal, and AdaJEPA mainly tracks it: the common shift alone recovers 91% of its planning gain. Part of the offset is episode-specific, so carrying its correction into the next episode does not help and may even hurt. The remaining offset recurs across episodes and action-dependent error can be or needs to be learned and retained from many episodes. Our sft-JEPA therefore uses two learners on different timescales: a fast learner that tracks the offset and resets each episode, and a slow LoRA learner that accumulates recurring knowledge across episodes. Across PushObj, PointMaze, zero-shot planning on RoboCasa (with DINO-WM), and OGBench (with LeWAM), sft-JEPA leads all benchmark averages, improving planning success over AdaJEPA by up to 22 points.

</details>


### [56] [MobileNeTTT: a Lightweight DNN-based Time-To-Transit Estimator for Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29454)
*Chiara Boretti,Philippe Bich,Manuel Morteo,Roberto Tron,John Baillieul*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于"通过时间（Time-to-Transit, TTT）"这一视觉感知线索的单目仿生机器人导航方法，通过轻量级神经网络MobileNeTTT估计全视场TTT并驱动导航，以极小模型实现边缘硬件上的实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人导航依赖当前大型视觉深度基础模型，参数量与计算量巨大，难以在边缘硬件实时运行。TTT作为视觉皮层可计算、可作为深度代理的粗略感知线索，结构简单，更适合用于机器人导航。

Method: 1) 提出轻量级神经网络MobileNeTTT，利用完整视场提供丰富的视觉特征TTT估计；2) 使模型对相机旋转相对不敏感；3) 自动适应路径几何与障碍物分布；4) 导航模型仅1.95M参数，比主流大型视觉基础模型小2–3个数量级。

Result: 仿真实验与真实实验室实验均验证了方法在动态、空间复杂环境中的有效性，模型可实时运行于边缘硬件。代码、数据与视频均已开源。

Conclusion: 以TTT这一粗略但易用的视觉线索替代大模型深度估计，可在保证导航性能的同时大幅压缩模型规模与算力需求，为仿生、轻量、实时的单目机器人导航提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Time-to-Transit (TTT) is a perceptual cue, proxy for depth, that can be computed in the visual cortex, allowing animals to estimate the time remaining to move past features in the environment. We show how TTT can be estimated and used for bio-inspired navigation of a mobile robot equipped with a monocular camera. The paper describes a lightweight neural network estimation method, named MobileNeTTT, that (1) uses the full field of view and provides a rich array of visual feature TTT estimates; (2) is relatively insensitive to camera rotation; and (3) automatically adjusts to the geometry of the path and the presence of obstacles. Because TTT is a coarse visual cue, it is far simpler to use for robot navigation than current large visual depth foundation models. Our navigation model uses a 1.95M-parameter network that is two to three orders of magnitude smaller than well-known large vision foundation models, making it able to run in real time on edge hardware. Simulation and actual laboratory experiments validate the proposed approach in dynamic and spatially complex environments. Code, data, and video are available.

</details>


### [57] [Representation Alignment Is Only as Good as the Teacher's Behavioral Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29483)
*Zhengfeiyang Zhang,Haoyuan Chen,Hexin Wang,Hanyu Zhou,Zhenglin Wan,Wangbo Zhao,Pengfei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文质疑VLA表示对齐中"靠启发式选择视觉教师编码器"的做法，提出行为结构度量BSM（教师特征空间中相近的状态在同一任务中是否对应相似的专家动作），并据此设计BAVA对齐协议，在SmolVLA上以1/3对齐预算达到连续对齐效果，平均成功率提升4.6点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像生成中REPA的教师可用编码器内在指标评估，但VLA的教师选择主要依赖启发式（如全局语义或空间结构先验）。作者通过大规模实验表明这种实践不可靠：14个教师、16个操作任务、匹配训练预算下，没有现成视觉编码器能让π0.5的干净成功率提升超过1点，且所有教师都使扰动下成功率下降10–39点，说明需要新的教师选择依据。

Method: 1) 系统性评测：14个现成视觉编码器在16个操作任务上对π0.5做表示对齐，考察干净与扰动两种条件下的成功率；2) 提出BSM指标：度量教师特征空间中相邻状态在同一任务内是否被相似的专家动作跟随；3) 基于BSM提出BAVA通用对齐协议：优先选择BSM高于学生的视觉教师，并依据学生自身BSM在训练中提前终止对齐。

Result: BSM与扰动条件下的成功率强相关（r=0.87），而所有现成视觉编码器自身的BSM都低于π0.5；在SmolVLA上，BAVA以三分之一的微调对齐预算达到与连续对齐相当的性能，相较未对齐的微调平均成功率高4.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 表示对齐只有在教师编码了丰富的、与行为相关的结构时才对VLA有益；教师选择应基于行为结构而非全局语义或空间结构等启发式标准，BSM与BAVA为此提供了可操作的依据与协议。

Abstract: Recent works align the intermediate features of vision-language-action (VLA) models with frozen vision encoders during fine-tuning, hoping to inject additional priors such as 3D awareness or affordances. Unlike in image generation, where teachers for representation alignment (REPA) are assessed with encoder-intrinsic metrics, teachers for VLAs are still chosen largely by heuristics. We show that this practice is unreliable: across 14 teachers and 16 manipulation tasks, under matched training budgets, no off-the-shelf vision encoder improves the clean success rate of $π_{0.5}$ by more than 1 point, and all of them reduce its success rate under perturbations by 10 to 39 points. Neither global semantics nor spatial structure, the criteria used in image generation, predicts which teachers help. We therefore propose the behavioral structure metric (BSM), which measures whether states that are close in a teacher's feature space are followed by similar expert actions within the same task. BSM correlates strongly with success rate under perturbations ($r=0.87$), and every off-the-shelf vision encoder scores below $π_{0.5}$ itself. Based on it, we introduce Behavior-Aware VLA Alignment (BAVA), a general VLA alignment protocol that favors vision encoders whose BSM exceeds the student's and stops alignment early according to the student's BSM during training. On SmolVLA, BAVA matches continuous alignment with one third of the alignment budget, and improves the average success rate by 4.6 points over unaligned fine-tuning. Our results suggest that representation alignment benefits VLAs only when the teacher encodes rich behaviorally relevant structure.

</details>


### [58] [MAWAM: Joint Imagination for Collaborative Robot Manipulation with a Multi-Agent World Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29501)
*Rongze Tang,Chenyu Zhou,Jianjie Fang,Ziming Liu,Zhaolu Wang,Ziyou Wang,Xvyuan Liu,Wei Wu,Chen Gao,Yong Li,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 MAWAM（多智能体世界动作模型），将单智能体世界动作模型沿"智能体轴"扩展为共享权重的统一因果序列模型，联合预测多具身未来观测并解码各智能体动作，在真实异构机器人团队上实现实时闭环协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAM）在机器人操作上表现优异，但仅限单一具身智能体。真实操作场景常需多机器人跨不重叠工作空间协作，涉及错位的工作空间、耦合的时序以及异构具身，要求同时具备未来交互的时空对齐能力与对各自具身/角色身份的感知。现有方案（分散式单机策略、扁平化集中模型、多机器人 VLA）无法兼顾这两点。

Method: 1）提出 MAWAM，联合预测多具身的未来观测并从中解码各智能体动作；2）通过"智能体轴课程"（agent-axis curriculum）将单智能体 WAM 沿智能体维度扩展，以共享权重协调团队；3）在统一因果序列中使用跨全部智能体的联合自注意力实现想象未来的时空对齐，并用分层语言、身份编码与具身掩码将每条流绑定到其角色、身份和具身；4）形式化"多智能体必要性"的三个维度（why、when、who），构建 MA-Task Harness（相互约束的生成器与评估器共同演化的编码智能体流水线），据此构建含七个任务的 MA-Bench，同时报告联合成功率、角色分与协作分。

Result: 在 MA-Bench 上，MAWAM 取得 82.7% 的平均联合成功率，比最强基线高出 30.7 个百分点；并在真实异构机器人团队上实现实时闭环协作。

Conclusion: 将世界动作模型沿智能体维度扩展、在统一因果序列中结合联合自注意力与身份/具身绑定，是解决多机器人协作操作中时空对齐与具身感知双重需求的有效途径，并配套建立了面向多智能体必要性的基准与评估体系。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) excel at robot manipulation by imagining future observations and decoding actions from them, yet remain confined to a single embodied agent. In practice, manipulation often requires multiple robots to collaborate across disjoint workspaces, coupled timing, and heterogeneous embodiments. Such coordination demands both spatiotemporal alignment of future interactions and awareness of individual embodiment and role identities, whereas existing decentralized per-robot policies, flat centralized models, and multi-robot VLAs fail to unite both. To bridge this gap, we present MAWAM, a multi-agent world action model that jointly predicts the future observations of multiple embodiments and decodes each agent's actions from them for collaborative manipulation. Through an agent-axis curriculum, MAWAM expands a single-agent WAM along an agent dimension to coordinate teams under shared weights. Within one unified causal sequence, joint self-attention spans all agents to spatiotemporally align their imagined futures, while hierarchical language, identity encodings, and embodiment masks bind each stream to its role, identity, and embodiment. We further formalize multi-agent-necessary manipulation along three axes (why, when, and who) and develop MA-Task Harness, a coding-agent pipeline where mutually constraining generators and evaluators co-evolve customizable simulation-data and evaluation packages. Using this pipeline, we build MA-Bench across all axes with seven tasks, pairing joint success rate with role and collaboration scores for fine-grained analysis. On MA-Bench, MAWAM reaches 82.7% mean joint success, 30.7% above the strongest baseline, and achieves real-time closed-loop collaboration on a real heterogeneous robot team.

</details>


### [59] [A ROS2 based Human-In-The-Loop Framework for UAV Search and Rescue Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29507)
*Anubhav Pandey,Harshit Nagpal,Aditya Agarwal,Tejasvi Alladi,Fei Richard Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个基于ROS2的人在回路（HITL）框架，利用RGB-D感知与改进的YOLOv11将操作者的左/右手势转化为无人机导航指令，面向非结构化搜救（SAR）场景的鲁棒实时部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机自主框架缺乏对人体情境的感知，难以在非结构化搜救环境中实时自适应；同时ROS1的弃用迫使平台迁移到ROS2，需要兼顾实时通信、可扩展性与可维护性。作者强调目标不是在理想条件下追求峰值精度，而是在退化SAR环境下保证鲁棒性与实时部署。

Method: 构建基于ROS2的人在回路框架，采用RGB-D传感与YOLOv11进行手势识别，并通过深度加权位姿估计融合RGB与深度线索；使用ROS2 DDS实现实时通信；在Gazebo-PX4仿真及公开UAV-GESTURE数据集（清晰与Koschmieder雾况）上评估，含跨域不重训测试。

Result: 遮挡与多变环境下深度估计置信度达89%；雾况下RGB-D融合较纯RGB平均提升46.7%；遮挡处理提升15%，端到端时延低于50 ms；不重训即可对真人达80%识别准确率，10 m能见度下深度保持77%而RGB降至24%；改进YOLOv11在仿真达91%、真实数据训练后在真人留出集达99%。

Conclusion: 该开源ROS2实现便于SAR-UAV平台从ROS1迁移，使无人机可直接响应操作者手势而无需复杂路径规划，为退化环境下的鲁棒人机协作提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Existing Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) autonomy frameworks lack human contextual awareness, limiting real-time adaptability in unstructured Search and Rescue (SAR) environments. To address data availability, we propose a Robot Operating System (ROS2)-based human-in-the-loop (HITL) framework that translates human directional gestures (left/right) into UAV navigation commands using RGB-Depth (RGB-D) sensing and YOLOv11 (You Only Look Once). The deprecation of ROS1 necessitates migrating to ROS2, prioritising real-time communication, scalability, and long-term maintainability. Unlike approaches optimised for peak accuracy under ideal conditions, the proposed system prioritises robustness and real-time deployment in degraded SAR environments. Our system achieves 89% confidence in depth estimation under occlusion and varying environmental conditions in Gazebo-PX4 simulations. In foggy conditions, RGB-D fusion consistently provides more informative cues than RGB alone, with an average improvement of 46.7% across all scenarios and distances. The framework demonstrates superior occlusion handling (+15%), adaptive RGB-D fusion, temporal consistency modelling, and real-time deployment via a ROS2 data distribution service (DDS), with end-to-end latency below 50 ms. We evaluate the modified YOLOv11 and the depth-weighted pose estimation on a held-out simulated test set and on real people from the public UAV-GESTURE dataset, in clear air and in Koschmieder fog. Without retraining, the framework reads the gestures of real people with 80% accuracy, depth keeps 77% at a visibility of 10 m where RGB drops to 24%, and the modified YOLOv11 reaches 91% in simulation and 99% on held-out real people when trained on real data. The open-source ROS2 implementation eases the transition of SAR-UAV platforms from ROS1, enabling UAVs to act directly on operator gestures without complex path planning.

</details>


### [60] [Reset Dependence Is Embodied: Training Protocol Changes Which Morphologies Appear Optimal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29518)
*Kevin Qiu,Zhao Yang,Marek Cygan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 机器人协同设计基准中，训练回合的重置规则（躯干跌落高度阈值）会显著改变身体排名的结果；建议基准显式声明重置规则、检验排名对其的依赖，并报告跨协议稳健的身体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人协同设计基准通常在固定回合协议下训练控制器再对身体排名，但该协议本身可能影响排名，导致同一语料、同一任务与评估器下不同研究选出不同身体；本文聚焦协议中的重置规则这一环节。

Method: 对100个DERL演化得到的UNIMAL身体，在三种躯干跌落高度阈值设置下训练PPO控制器：阈值设为初始躯干高度的20%（Standard）、50%（Strict）、以及无阈值（Reset-free），并用同一评估器为所有控制器打分；辅以置换检验、种子噪声对照，并在24个身体上用SAC复现。

Result: Strict与Reset-free训练的排名相关性仅0.21，而若只有种子噪声则约0.89；去掉跌落重置对不同身体代价悬殊，平地演化的身体每步奖励相对Standard下降45%，可变地形演化的身体仅下降16%，置换检验支持该差异；在24个身体上，平地身体在PPO和SAC下均从Strict相对Reset-free中获益最多；按三种协议最差奖励选身体代价很小，样本内保留各协议最优身体至少91%的奖励，跨留出种子的均值超过80%。

Conclusion: 重置规则能左右协同设计基准的身体选择，基准应说明其重置规则、检验排名是否依赖该规则，并报告在多种协议下均表现稳健的身体。

Abstract: Robot co-design benchmarks rank bodies after training their controllers under a fixed episode protocol, but that protocol can change the ranking itself. Consequently, two studies can select different bodies from the same corpus even when they use the same task and evaluator. We study one part of that protocol, the reset rule, which ends a training episode when the robot's torso falls below a height threshold. We train proximal policy optimization (PPO) controllers for 100 DERL-evolved UNIMAL bodies with this threshold at 20% of each body's starting torso height (Standard), at 50% (Strict), or with no threshold (Reset-free), and score every controller with the same evaluator. The threshold changes which bodies look best: the rankings after Strict and Reset-free training correlate at only 0.21, against about 0.89 if seed noise were the only difference. Removing fall resets costs some bodies far more than others. Bodies evolved on flat ground lose 45% of their per-step reward relative to Standard training, and those evolved on variable terrain only 16%. Permutation tests over bodies support this difference, and on 24 bodies, flat-ground bodies gain the most from Strict over Reset-free training under both PPO and soft actor-critic (SAC). Choosing bodies by their worst reward across the three protocols costs little: in sample, they keep at least 91% of the reward of each protocol's best bodies, and over 80% averaged across held-out seeds. Co-design benchmarks should state their reset rule, test whether their rankings depend on it, and report bodies that stay strong across protocols. Project page: https://reset-dependence.github.io.

</details>


### [61] [Feedback Completion Certificates for Repeated Dwell Tasks with Time Windows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29525)
*Jiaxun Sun,Yuqi Yang,Senlan Yao,Hengyu Xue,Hongming Fu,Zhimu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文研究巡检机器人在多次到访同一站点、每次可开始驻留的时间窗不同且存在有界扰动时，反馈控制器需保留何种历史信息才能保证剩余截止时间全部满足；指出在去除冗余窗口后，保留一个边界和一个截断运行长度即可，并用该状态在经验证的分支运动基元库上恢复反馈策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巡检机器人对同一站点可能有多次测量义务，每个义务对应不同的驻留起始时间窗，控制器需在有限扰动下判断哪些历史信息是保证未来所有截止时间所必需的，从而避免状态爆炸并构造可复用的全定义域策略。

Method: 先对同一空间谓词与驻留长度去除冗余窗口，使剩余义务按两个端点排序；证明在无已过期义务的任意历史上，已服务窗口构成后缀，因此一个边界与一个截断运行长度即可保持原任务的未来可满足性。在此约简状态上，于经验证的分支运动基元库中恢复反馈策略：单一任务类型时每个运行长度只需一个标量阈值，固定驻留下每个时间层的工作量与所列分支数线性；多任务类型需联合递归。并以四旋翼巡检库阈值扫描与两站点MuJoCo巡逻实验评估。

Result: 四旋翼巡检库上阈值扫描降低了构造可复用全定义域策略的成本，而稀疏布尔基线在单次初始查询上更快；两站点MuJoCo巡逻中，三种任务感知过滤器在各自十二组配对运行中均满足每个时间窗；八组初始获证的运行中，完成过滤器在全部八组保留证书，仅考虑安全性的过滤为三组。

Conclusion: 约简后的历史摘要（边界加截断运行长度）足以在有界扰动下保证全部驻留窗口的满足，可用于高效恢复反馈策略；任务完成感知的过滤在保留形式化证书方面明显优于仅安全过滤，但全定义域策略构造与单查询速度之间存在权衡。

Abstract: An inspection robot may owe several measurements at the same station, each with a different window in which a dwell can begin. We ask what a feedback controller must retain about earlier visits to guarantee all remaining deadlines under bounded disturbances. For a common spatial predicate and dwell length, removing redundant windows orders the remaining obligations by both endpoints. On every history without an expired obligation, the served windows form a suffix. One boundary and one capped run length therefore preserve the future satisfaction of the original task. On a verified library of branching motion primitives we use this state to recover a feedback policy. For one task type a scalar threshold per run length suffices, with work linear in the number of listed branches per time layer for fixed dwell. Multiple types require a joint recursion. On a quadrotor inspection library the threshold sweep reduced the cost of constructing a reusable full-domain policy, while a sparse Boolean baseline answered a single initial query faster. In a two-station MuJoCo patrol, three task-aware filters all met every window in twelve paired runs each. Of eight initially certified runs, the completion filter retained the certificate in all eight, compared with three for safety-only filtering.

</details>


### [62] [Detecting Is Not Choosing: What a World Model Needs to Keep a VLA Policy Safe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29553)
*Thanish Bolla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究何种世界模型才能真正用于VLA策略的安全过滤。实验发现：基于精确仿真器前瞻的安全过滤器可将安全率提升11.5–25.7个百分点；但学习型评论家、学习动力学模型和视频模型只在"状态级"危险检测上有效（AUROC达0.864），在"同一状态下的候选动作排序"上接近随机（AUROC 0.551–0.560）。世界模型应以为决策内的排序能力而非检测能力来评价，且状态精度须在尾部与操作对象上可靠，而非仅看中位数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略虽能完成多数基准任务，却常违反安全约束。由于策略具有随机性，一种补救方式是采样多个候选动作块，再由世界模型预测并挑选安全的一个。于是关键问题变为：这样的世界模型究竟需要具备什么能力？现有工作缺少对"安全过滤器所用世界模型"能力要求的系统诊断，尤其未区分"检测危险状态"与"在同一状态下比较候选动作"这两种不同能力。

Method: 在仿真中构建以前瞻为基础的VLA安全过滤器，以仿真器作为精确世界模型作为上界；在LIBERO（三个策略）、LIBERO-Plus与RoboCasa（pi0.5）上评估安全率增益。随后用单一策略数据训练学习型评论家、学习动力学模型与视频模型，并分别测量两类指标：跨状态的危险状态检测能力（pooled AUROC）与同一决策内候选动作的排序能力（within-decision AUROC）。进一步对仿真器状态施加扰动（高斯误差、像素读取误差），考察检测与选择两种功能的退化顺序，以及增益保留情况。

Result: 精确前瞻过滤器将三个VLA策略在LIBERO上、pi0.5在LIBERO-Plus上的安全率提升11.5–25.7个百分点，并在RoboCasa三个任务上对pi0.5同样有效。学习型评论家能检测危险状态（pooled AUROC最高0.864），但在单状态内的候选排序接近随机（within-decision AUROC 0.551–0.560），所测的学习动力学与视频模型亦无改善。状态扰动实验中，选择性能远早于检测性能退化； LIBERO-Spatial上3.18 mm的中位位置误差即抹去70%的增益（部分因误差同时污染位移检查），LIBERO-10上损失较小且区间重叠。中位误差并不能预测代价：一个中位误差0.56 mm的像素读取器，其增益保留比同等中位误差的高斯误差少6.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 用于安全的世界模型应以"决策内（同一状态下）候选动作排序"能力来评价，而非仅以状态级危险检测来评价；并且其状态估计必须在误差尾部与所操作对象上保持准确，仅中位精度不足以支撑安全选择。安全过滤的实际收益高度依赖于世界模型在细粒度比较与尾部精度上的质量。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies complete most benchmark tasks but often violate safety constraints. Since they are stochastic, one remedy is to sample several candidate action chunks and execute one that a world model predicts is safe. We ask what such a model must do. In simulation, a lookahead safety filter with the simulator as an exact world model raises the safety rate of three VLA policies on LIBERO and of pi0.5 on LIBERO-Plus by 11.5 to 25.7 points. It also helps pi0.5 on three RoboCasa tasks. Learned critics trained on one policy's data detect dangerous states (pooled AUROC up to 0.864) but rank candidates at one state close to chance (within-decision AUROC 0.551-0.560), and the learned dynamics and video models we test do no better. When the simulator's state is perturbed, selection degrades long before detection. In the loop, a median position error of 3.18 mm removes 70% of the gain on LIBERO-Spatial, partly because the error also corrupts the displacement check. On LIBERO-10 the loss is smaller, and the intervals overlap. The median does not predict the cost: a pixel reader with a 0.56 mm median error keeps 6.5 points less of the gain than Gaussian error of a similar median. A world model for safety should thus be judged by within-state ranking, and its state must be accurate in the tail and on handled objects, not only in the median.

</details>


### [63] [Action Windows Are Inherently Temporal: A Benchmark for Time-Sensitive Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29558)
*Haoxiang Zhan,Haoqin Hong,Congcong Zhu,Jingrun Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 AWAIT，一个用于评测视觉语言模型（VLM）智能体在"动作窗口"内完成任务的仿真基准，同时把决策耗时与执行耗时纳入时间预算，揭示了现有 VLM 智能体在识别窗口开启时机与连续决策中的时间约束问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 智能体已在现有机器人操作基准上超越学习策略基线，但现有基准只部分覆盖具有时间敏感性的日常任务。这类任务中持续演变的物理过程只提供有限的成功干预窗口，且窗口本质上具有时间性，并对决策与物理执行施加共享的时间预算；要在该预算内协调干预，智能体必须判断窗口何时开启。现有评测缺乏对此的考察。

Method: 构建 AWAIT 仿真基准，在任务评测中要求智能体在动作窗口内完成任务，并把决策时间与执行时间一并计入统一的时间预算；据此对 VLM 智能体开展评测分析。

Result: 评测显示 VLM 智能体难以识别动作窗口的开启时刻；并表明即便单次决策耗时短于窗口时长，连续的决策与动作叠加也可能耗尽整个动作窗口。

Conclusion: 时间敏感任务中的动作窗口对 VLM 智能体构成实质性挑战，需同时考虑决策与执行的时间开销来评估和提升其干预时机把握能力；AWAIT 为此类研究提供了评测平台。

Abstract: Vision-language model (VLM) agents have surpassed learned-policy baselines on existing robotic manipulation benchmarks. However, these agents still struggle with time-sensitive everyday tasks that existing benchmarks for VLM agents cover only partially. In these tasks, evolving physical processes create finite windows for successful intervention. These action windows are inherently temporal and impose a shared time budget on decision making and physical execution. Coordinating intervention within this budget requires agents to determine when the window opens. We introduce AWAIT, a simulation benchmark that evaluates task completion within action windows while accounting for decision and execution time. Our evaluation reveals difficulties in recognizing window onset and shows that successive decisions and movements can exhaust an action window even when each decision takes less than the window duration. Project page: https://eyja42.github.io/await-site/.

</details>


### [64] [JEPA-Shield: Safe Diffusion Planning with Latent World Models via Conformal Shielding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29609)
*Taekyung Kim,Ege Yuceel,Steven Hong,Georgios Fainekos,Dimitra Panagou,Bardh Hoxha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 JEPA-Shield，将冻结的动作条件 JEPA 世界模型与任务空间安全约束相连，用分体共形预测把运动预测误差转成多时域不确定性管，并以备份式盾牌在扩散式采样 MPC 中筛选轨迹。在自建 VParkBench（几何泊车、卡车挂车泊车、可编辑高斯泼溅真实感环境）上取得 84.4%/82.3%/99.0% 成功率且未观察到约束违反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型能让机器人从图像进行规划，但其潜在预测本身无法直接判断机器人是否满足物理约束。作者希望把潜在预测落到任务空间的物理量上，并显式建模预测不确定性，从而在安全关键规划中提供概率性安全保证。

Method: 1) 冻结动作条件 JEPA 世界模型；2) 两个下游模型：从想象潜在轨迹预测相对运动，并从当前图像解码不安全几何；3) 用分体共形预测将端到端运动预测误差转化为多时域不确定性管；4) 备份式盾牌检验这些管与物理约束，在扩散启发的采样式模型预测控制中对候选轨迹和最终提案进行双重筛选。

Result: 在交换性与初始备份假设下，给出不重规划执行返回计划时在动作块边界处的概率性约束满足保证。闭环评估中三类任务成功率分别为 84.4%、82.3%、99.0%，无观测到的约束违反；消融显示盾牌与不确定性校准带来互补收益。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将潜在世界模型预测落地到任务空间量，并在安全关键规划中显式考虑预测不确定性，对可靠机器人规划十分重要。

Abstract: Latent world models enable planning from images, but their predictions do not directly establish whether a robot will satisfy physical constraints. We present JEPA-Shield, which connects a frozen action-conditioned Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) world model to task-space safety enforcement. Two downstream models predict relative motion from imagined latent rollouts and decode unsafe geometry from the current image. Split conformal prediction turns end-to-end motion-prediction errors into multi-horizon uncertainty tubes. A backup-based shield checks these tubes against physical constraints and screens both candidate trajectories and final proposals in diffusion-inspired sampling-based model predictive control. Under the stated exchangeability and initial-backup assumptions, we establish probabilistic constraint satisfaction at action-block boundaries for a returned plan executed without replanning. We also introduce VParkBench, with geometric car and tractor-trailer parking tasks and a editable photorealistic Gaussian-splatting environment. In closed-loop evaluation, JEPA-Shield achieves success rates of 84.4%, 82.3%, and 99.0%, respectively, with no observed constraint violations. Ablations show complementary benefits from shielding and uncertainty calibration. The results demonstrate the importance of grounding latent world-model predictions in task-space quantities and explicitly accounting for prediction uncertainty in safety-critical planning.

</details>


### [65] [Dr. Grasp-RL: Diverse and Robust Dexterous Grasping via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29610)
*Kunqi Xu,Xiaokang Sun,Xueyan Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种时间反转课程学习（time-reversed curriculum）配合协同重置机制，使简单的MLP策略（PPO训练）既能捕捉抓取位姿分布的多模态多样性，又保持闭环控制，从而同时兼顾灵巧性与鲁棒性，并在超过2万个未见物体上展现良好泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人灵巧抓取中，灵巧性需要多样的操作策略，鲁棒性需要闭环适应扰动。现有生成式方法能建模多模态分布产生多样抓取位姿，但开环执行削弱鲁棒性；强化学习方法能学得闭环抓取策略，但易收敛到狭窄的成功策略范围，行为多样性不足。两类能力此前被分开研究。

Method: 引入时间反转课程（time-reversed curriculum）与协同重置机制（coordinated reset mechanism），用于训练一个简单的MLP策略，采用PPO算法；通过该课程设计使策略覆盖抓取位姿分布的多个模态，同时保留闭环控制。

Result: 实验表明策略同时具备多样的抓取行为与鲁棒的闭环执行能力；仅用约400个物体训练，在超过20,000个未见物体上评估仍表现出强泛化性。

Conclusion: 通过时间反转课程与协同重置，单一简单闭环策略可将生成式方法的多样性与强化学习的闭环鲁棒性统一起来，且具备出色的样本效率与泛化能力。

Abstract: Human interaction with physical objects relies on a combination of dexterity and robustness. In robotic manipulation, dexterity requires not only flexible end effectors but also a diverse repertoire of manipulation strategies, while robustness requires closed-loop adaptation to disturbances and unexpected situations. Two prominent research directions in dexterous grasping have largely addressed these capabilities separately. Generative approaches model multimodal distributions to produce diverse grasp poses, but their reliance on open-loop execution can limit robustness. Reinforcement learning approaches learn closed-loop grasping policies, but can converge to a narrow range of successful grasping strategies, limiting behavioral diversity. We introduce a time-reversed curriculum with a coordinated reset mechanism that enables a simple MLP policy trained with PPO to capture multiple modes of the grasp-pose distribution while maintaining closed-loop control. Experiments demonstrate both diverse grasping behaviors and robust closed-loop execution. Trained on only approximately 400 objects, our policy further demonstrates strong generalization when evaluated on more than 20,000 unseen objects.

</details>


### [66] [TASC: Time-to-Collision Anticipation and Selective Crowd Attention for Non-Holonomic Service Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29637)
*Mohammadhossein Malmir,Mohammadali Rahmati,Mohammad Reza Kolani,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TASC导航系统，面向非完整约束（差速驱动）服务机器人，将注意力人群编码器、基于接近速度的碰撞时间（TTC）特征与连续控制策略（PPO）结合，在高保真仿真中实现了极高的存活率（各人群密度下超时率<0.5%），但以行人间碰撞率上升为代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务机器人需在与人共享的走廊和大厅中持续移动而不危及行人。现有反应式规划器在密集人群中可能无限停顿（冻结机器人问题）；而主流的基于注意力的学习型导航器多在标准人群基准上训练，假定全向运动或输出离散动作，不适用于差速驱动底盘。因此需要一种兼顾"持续运动"与"非完整运动学约束"的连续控制导航方案。

Method: 1）将原用于离散动作值学习的双选择注意力模块改造并接入PPO，用于连续速度控制；2）引入基于接近速度的碰撞时间（TTC）特征——一种保守的接触时间估计，既作为观测又作为奖励项；3）在NVIDIA Isaac Lab中用高保真3D机器人模型、10,000个并行环境、社会力模型行人和随机人群规模进行训练；4）进行消融实验（每个变体4个随机种子）及Gazebo零样本迁移与ROS 2实物硬件在环测试。

Result: 在30,000个完整运行的评估回合中，各测试人群密度下策略超时率均低于0.5%。代价是行人碰撞率随密度上升：5人时4.5%，10人时17.4%。去掉TTC特征的消融策略成功率下降4–7个百分点。策略无需重训练即可在Gazebo中运行，并通过ROS 2包部署于商用差速驱动服务机器人，推理延迟低于10 ms，已在含虚拟行人的硬件在环测试中验证。

Conclusion: TASC证明了将注意力人群编码、TTC安全特征与PPO连续控制相结合，可在非完整约束服务机器人上实现高存活率、低延迟且可实机部署的导航策略；但其安全性（碰撞率）随人群密度显著上升，说明"持续运动"与"安全"之间存在明显的权衡，是后续需要改进的方向。

Abstract: Service robots that share corridors and lobbies with people must keep moving without endangering them. Reactive planners can stop indefinitely in dense crowds (the freezing robot problem), whereas the attention-based learned navigators that dominate the standard crowd benchmark assume holonomic motion or emit discrete actions unsuited to a differential-drive base. This paper presents TASC, a navigation system for a non-holonomic service robot that integrates an attention-based crowd encoder, an anticipatory collision feature, and a continuous-control policy. We adapt the dual-selection attention module, originally developed for discrete-action value learning, to proximal policy optimization for continuous velocity control, and add a closing-speed time-to-collision feature, a conservative estimate of the time to contact that serves as both an observation and a reward term. The policy is trained on a high-fidelity 3D robot model in NVIDIA Isaac Lab with 10,000 parallel environments, social-force-model pedestrians, and randomized crowd sizes. In 30,000 evaluation episodes, each run to its end, the policy timed out in fewer than 0.5% at every tested crowd density. This liveness comes at a cost: pedestrian collisions rise from 4.5% with five pedestrians to 17.4% with ten. In an ablation with four seeds per variant, policies retrained without the time-to-collision feature succeed 4 to 7 percentage points less often. The policy runs in Gazebo without retraining and on a commercial differential-drive service robot through a ROS 2 package with an inference latency below 10 ms, verified in a hardware-in-the-loop test with virtual pedestrians.

</details>


### [67] [JKO-GRPO: Augmenting GRPO with Wasserstein Proximal Steps for Flow-Matching VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29646)
*Jiyong Kwon,Yikun Bai,Amirhossein Mollaali,Guang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 JKO-GRPO，在 Flow-GRPO 基础上引入 Wasserstein 近端步（JKO 步），把采样动作组视为经验分布，用软离散 c-transform 从组相对优势构造传输目标，并对确定性流采样器做共享噪声回归，同时扩展到序列 VLA 控制的随机时间分支机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线强化学习方法（如 Flow-GRPO、π_RL）只优化随机去噪转移的似然比，按优势对采样动作重新加权，却忽略了动作之间的距离（几何/传输结构），因此策略改进的利用效率有限。

Method: 1) 在给定状态下将一组采样动作视为经验分布，把策略改进形式化为 Jordan-Kinderlehrer-Otto (JKO) 步；2) 用软离散 c-transform 由组相对优势构造传输目标，将确定性流采样器的共享噪声回归对齐到这些目标，并与无 KL 正则的裁剪 Flow-GRPO 目标组合；3) 通过随机时间分支扩展到序列 VLA 控制：沿旧策略轨迹选取一个或多个动作块索引，并从相应状态出发用候选续接独立执行组级 JKO 更新。

Result: 摘要未给出实验数据；方法上以 Wasserstein 近端步补充了仅依赖似然比的加权方式，使动作间距离信息参与策略更新，并提供了序列 VLA 场景下的分支式更新框架。

Conclusion: JKO-GRPO 通过将 JKO/Wasserstein 近端步与 Flow-GRPO 结合，为流式 VLA 模型的在线强化学习提供了利用动作分布几何结构的新范式，并可经随机时间分支推广到多步序列控制。

Abstract: Flow-based vision-language-action (VLA) models such as $π_0,π_{0.5}$ generate continuous action chunks by flow matching, and online reinforcement learning (RL) can improve them beyond supervised fine-tuning. Existing methods, such as Flow-GRPO and $π_{\mathrm{RL}}$, optimize likelihood ratios of stochastic denoising transitions, which reweight the sampled actions by their advantages but do not use the distances between them. We propose JKO-GRPO, which augments Flow-GRPO with Wasserstein proximal steps, with two contributions: (1) At each state, we treat the group of sampled actions as an empirical distribution and formulate policy improvement as a Jordan-Kinderlehrer-Otto (JKO) step. We use a soft discrete $c$-transform to construct transport targets from group-relative advantages and combine shared-noise regression of the deterministic flow sampler onto these targets and combined with the clipped Flow-GRPO objective without Kullback-Leibler (KL) regularization. (2) We extend this approach to sequential VLA control through random-time branching: we select one or multiple action-chunk indices along old-policy trajectories and independently apply group-level JKO updates using candidate continuations from the corresponding states.

</details>


### [68] [Grounded in Contact: Reducing Floating and Interpenetration Artifacts with Multimodal Skill-Effect Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29662)
*Siyeon Kim,Mohanraj Devendran Shanthi,Joseph C. Liechty,Tucker Hermans*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文研究接触丰富的操作技能效果预测，提出用多模态概率模型表示物体位姿变化的多峰分布，并比较了混合密度网络、状态token化和流匹配三类模型（均结合隐动力学做下一步预测）。实验表明显式多模态模型比确定性/高斯基线更准确地预测结果，流匹配在规划中显著提升任务成功率，在真实机器人上抓取成功率约为确定性基线的两倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富操作（推、抓、放）中，状态与动作几乎相同时，接触条件或几何的微小变化会导致定性不同的物体运动（滑动、翻倒、旋转等）。确定性模型和高斯模型会把这些备选结果平均掉，或塞进单一宽分布，从而掩盖真实存在的多个模态，使下游推理与规划失效。因此需要一个能表达多峰结果分布的"技能效果模型"抽象。

Method: 将问题形式化为：在给定部分点云与动作的条件下，学习物体位姿变化的多模态概率分布。用三个代表性模型类实现这一抽象——混合密度网络（MDN）、状态token化（state tokenization）、流匹配（flow matching），每类都与一个隐动力学模型配对用于下一步预测。在多种物体与技能上与确定性基线、高斯基线对比，并用于规划（单步技能与多步序列）及真实机器人抓取实验。

Result: 在多样物体与技能上，显式多模态模型预测"实际发生哪种结果"的准确率高于确定性与高斯基线。规划中，流匹配在每一个技能上都显著优于确定性基线，并在每个多步序列上取得最高成功率。真实机器人上，概率模型的抓取成功率约为确定性基线的两倍。

Conclusion: 概率化的技能效果（多模态分布）是一种实用抽象，可用于不确定性下的推理与规划；显式建模多模态比确定性/单高斯假设更符合接触丰富操作的内在随机性，并在仿真规划与真实机器人上均带来实际收益。

Abstract: Learning to predict the effects of contact-rich manipulation skills requires models that represent distinct outcome modes rather than a single expected result. Even for basic skills such as pushing, picking, and placing, small variations in contact conditions or object geometry can produce qualitatively different object motions under nearly identical states and actions. Deterministic and Gaussian models tend to average over these alternatives or represent them as a single broad distribution, obscuring distinct modes such as sliding, toppling, and rotating. We study probabilistic skill-effect models that learn multimodal distributions over object pose changes given partial point clouds and actions. We instantiate this abstraction with three representative model classes: mixture density networks, state tokenization, and flow matching, each paired with latent dynamics for next-step prediction. Across diverse objects and skills, explicitly multimodal models predict which outcome occurs more accurately than deterministic and Gaussian baselines. In planning, flow matching significantly improves task success over the deterministic baseline on every skill and achieves the highest success on every multi-step sequence.
