On a real robot, probabilistic models succeed at pick about twice as often as the deterministic baseline, providing evidence that probabilistic skill effects offer a practical abstraction for reasoning and planning under uncertainty.

</details>


### [69] [SparQ4D: Learning to Act through Sparse 4D Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29682)
*Siyuan Xie,Jinxi Xiao,Ran Ji,Yikai Liu,Mingxi Li,Xuebing Li,Ye Shi,Yuxiang Gao,Jiayuan Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出轻量级查询式解码器 \method，将整体4D场景理解高效注入策略，训练提速至5倍且推理零额外开销，实现SOTA操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成功的操作任务需要策略理解物体的位置及其运动方式，但动作示范数据仅隐式包含这种时空结构，缺乏显式4D监督；而获取稠密4D预测代价高昂。

Method: 在预训练策略上附加基于查询的轻量解码器，将多种时空任务（深度、2D/3D点运动、多视角对应、手眼关系）统一到单一接口；训练时仅用少量采样查询即可学习丰富4D结构，推理时移除解码器实现零额外开销。

Result: 在多个骨干网络、仿真环境与真实场景中，\method 达到SOTA操作性能，优于对比方法，训练速度最高提升5倍；探针分析表明该4D结构可从策略中恢复。

Conclusion: 以低成本方式将显式4D表征注入策略是可行且高效的，兼顾性能、训练效率与推理开销。

Abstract: Successful manipulation requires a policy to understand where things are and how they will move, yet action demonstrations leave this spatiotemporal structure implicit. While explicit 4D supervision has proven effective, reducing the cost of dense 4D prediction remains challenging. This paper introduces \method, a lightweight yet expressive decoder that efficiently injects holistic 4D scene understanding into policies and improves success rates. At its core is a query-based decoder attached to a pretrained policy, which casts many spatialtemporal tasks into one unified interface (e.g. depth, 2D/3D point motion, multi-view correspondence, and hand-eye relations). This design enables efficient training and inference without sacrificing expressivity: the policy learns rich 4D structure from a small number of sampled queries during training, while the decoder can be removed at inference with zero additional overhead. Across multiple backbones, simulated environments, and real-world settings, \method achieves state-of-the-art manipulation performance, outperforming competing methods while training up to $5\times$ faster. Probing analyses further reveal that this 4D structure is recoverable from the policy.

</details>


### [70] [Performance Evaluation of Nonlinear Model Predictive and Sliding Mode Controllers on a 4-Cable Spatial CSPR in Simscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29748)
*Erfun Bayat Sarmadi,Ali Asadi,Moosa Ayati*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对四索驱动的空间索悬并联机器人（CSPR）分别采用非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）与滑模控制（SMC），在解析动力学模型与MATLAB Simscape Multibody物理仿真模型上进行全面对比研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要为高精度、受约束的索悬并联机器人选择合适控制策略，并验证解析模型与物理仿真的可靠性，因此对NMPC与SMC进行公平对比并交叉验证两种建模途径。

Method: 建立含绳索弹性与摩擦损耗的解析动力学模型，同时在MATLAB Simscape Multibody中搭建物理仿真模型；在相同参考轨迹、系统约束与整定策略下实现NMPC与SMC两种控制器，并分别在两种模型上测试。

Result: NMPC在跟踪精度与抗扰能力上优于SMC，但计算负担更高；两种控制方案下解析模型与Simscape模型结果高度一致，验证了解析建模的准确性及其与物理仿真的一致性。

Conclusion: 物理仿真可作为验证先进控制器的有效补充工具；对于所研究的高精度、受约束CSPR，NMPC性能更优。

Abstract: This paper offers a comprehensive comparative study between Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC) and Sliding Mode Control (SMC) applied to a spatial Cable-Suspended Parallel Robot (CSPR) actuated by four cables. The robot is modeled in two parallel ways: (i) via an analytical dynamic model with included cable elasticity and frictional losses, and (ii) in MATLAB Simscape Multibody, capturing realistic physical effects. Both controllers are implemented under identical reference trajectories, system constraints, and tuning strategies. Our results show that NMPC achieves superior tracking accuracy and stronger disturbance rejection compared to SMC, but at the cost of higher computational demand. Moreover, both controllers were tested on an analytical model of the robot and a Simscape model. The close agreement between the two, for both control schemes, confirms the accuracy of the analytical formulation and its consistency with the physics-based simulation. The study highlights the value of physics-based simulation as a complementary tool for validating advanced controllers under realistic conditions, and demonstrates that NMPC offers superior performance for the studied high-precision, constrained CSPR.

</details>


### [71] [CarlHOI: Counterfactual Predictive Imagination for Generalizable Multi-Humanoid Object Interaction under Constrained Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29749)
*Wenjie Lin,Jin Wei-Kocsis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CarlHOI——基于反事实预测想象框架与反事实锚定关系潜变量世界模型（CarlWM），使预训练的多类人形机器人协同搬运策略无需重训练即可适应未见环境约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理仿真人形控制虽已支持多机器人协同搬运共享物体，但将已学到的协同行为迁移到未见环境约束（如障碍需协同绕行、团队构型变化）仍很困难，通常需重新训练底层协同控制策略。

Method: 将共享物体、单个类人形个体与环境障碍显式表示为交互实体；用反事实仿真器轨迹离线训练 CarlWM；在不同共享路点指令下想象多种集体未来，预测人-物体耦合系统的响应；通过预测进度、失败与接触风险及长时域价值估计评估想象结果，从而选择自适应引导指令，同时保持预训练协同控制器不变。并构建三个障碍约束搬运任务的基准。

Result: 仅在四个类人形机器人上训练，却能零重训练泛化到2–10个机器人团队、未见障碍几何与布局、未见物体及新的环境约束组合；在多样配置下协同搬运成功率优于对比基线。

Conclusion: 反事实预测想象有潜力把预训练的多类人形协同技能扩展到原始训练条件之外，为不重训练的策略自适应提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in physics-based humanoid control enable teams of varying sizes to coordinate their movements and cooperatively transport shared objects. However, adapting learned cooperative behaviors to unseen environmental constraints remains challenging, particularly when obstacles require coordinated detours and changes in team configuration. We propose CarlHOI, a counterfactual predictive imagination framework built upon a counterfactual-anchored relational latent world model (CarlWM) for human-object interaction (HOI), enabling multi-humanoid teams to adapt to unseen environmental constraints without retraining their underlying cooperative control policies. CarlHOI explicitly represents the shared object, individual humanoids, and environmental obstacles as interacting entities. Trained offline using counterfactual simulator rollouts, the model imagines alternative collective futures under different shared waypoint commands, predicting how the coupled humanoid team and object would respond. By evaluating these imagined outcomes through predicted progress, failure, and contact risks, and long-horizon value estimates, CarlHOI selects adaptive guidance commands while preserving the pretrained cooperative controller. We introduce a benchmark comprising three challenging obstacle-constrained transport tasks to evaluate adaptation and generalization. Despite being trained exclusively with four humanoids, our model generalizes without retraining to teams of two to ten humanoids, unseen obstacle geometries and placements, unseen objects, and novel combinations of environmental constraints. Extensive experiments demonstrate improved cooperative transport success over evaluated baselines across diverse configurations. Results highlight the potential of counterfactual predictive imagination to extend pretrained multi-humanoid cooperative skills beyond their original training conditions.

</details>


### [72] [Into My Arms: Structured reinforcement learning for autonomous transport of objects and humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29771)
*Elle Miller,Reem Almheiri,Takuma Tsukakoshi,Ben Sanati,Tamon Miyake,Yushi Wang,Namiko Saito,Shigeki Sugano,Tetsuya Ogata,Rika Antonova,Oisin Mac Aodha,Sethu Vijayakumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SIE、IGT、HOP三种轻量结构化机制，使强化学习能够在单GPU一小时内训练出人形机器人整身搬运策略，并首次实现无人工干预的病床到轮椅的假人转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人整身操作面临三重困境：高维状态-动作空间、富接触环境、长任务时域，现有先进强化学习方法难以可靠解决。

Method: 以"刚够用的结构引导探索"为思路，引入三项机制：稀疏交互边（SIE）用于整身操作；交互门控搬运（IGT）支撑长时域持续搬运；提示物体路径（HOP）缓解碰撞与搜索复杂度瓶颈。三者均不限制策略自主发现操作策略。

Result: 方法可从刚体对象泛化到人体搬运；展示据称首个由人形机器人全程无人工干预完成的病床到轮椅医疗假人转移；在人体搬运场景中，单一策略可适应体重、体型显著变化及随机外力扰动，单GPU训练不足一小时。

Conclusion: 该工作为完全由强化学习驱动的人形机器人操作提供了一条可行路径，有望推动能够帮助人类的辅助机器人发展。

Abstract: Whole-body humanoid manipulation remains bottlenecked by high-dimensional state-action spaces, contact-rich environments, and long task horizons. This trilemma is a challenge that state-of-the-art reinforcement learning (RL) methods are yet to resolve reliably. We address it with three contributions that add just enough structure to guide exploration: Sparse Interaction Edges (SIE) for whole-body manipulation, Interaction Gated Transport (IGT) for sustained transport over long horizons, and a Hinted Object Path (HOP) that resolves collision and search complexity bottlenecks. Across all three, the policy remains free to discover its own manipulation strategy. The method generalises from rigid object to human transport. As a flagship demonstration, we show, to our knowledge, the first bed-to-wheelchair transfer of a medical mannequin by a humanoid robot completed with no human intervention during execution. In the domain of human transfer, we show that a single policy can adapt across substantial variations in body mass, size, and random force perturbations, all while training on a single GPU in under an hour. Our approach offers a pathway to fully RL-driven humanoid manipulation, enabling assistive robots that can help humans. Website: https://elle-miller.github.io/into_my_arms

</details>


### [73] [Generating Safety-Critical Driving Scenarios from Role-Typed Signal Temporal Logic Specifications Drafted by Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29800)
*Zifan Zeng,Qunli Zhang,Peng Wang,Shiming Liu,Feng Liu,Zheng Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于信号时序逻辑（STL）与类型系统驱动的引导扩散生成框架，将安全关键驾驶场景的"场景定义"与"有效性校验"统一为一份带角色的STL规格，由大模型起草、类型检查器审查、编译为扩散采样的最小编辑优化，在nuScenes与CommonRoad上以单一参数集取得比逐场景调权目标高0.14的有效率，并证明生成场景能有效暴露和改善规划器失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键场景在真实记录数据中极稀少，必须人工生成以测试与训练自动驾驶运动规划器。现有引导扩散方法存在两个痛点：一是每种场景类型都需要单独设计加权目标函数，且该目标与有效性校验彼此分离，调参成本高、难以泛化；二是被测车辆与攻击者被放在同一个优化中联合求解，被测车可以通过反应"抵消攻击"或把攻击"吸引到自己身上"，使生成的场景失效。

Method: 1）用带角色类型标注的子句（clause）书写STL规格，每个子句显式声明"可修改哪些智能体"和"可读取哪些智能体"，被测车仅在攻击开始时刻之后才可读取攻击者；2）由大语言模型根据自然语言描述起草规格，再由类型检查器判定其合法性；3）编译器将规格自动编译为优化问题：对扩散模型样本做满足规格的最小改动，每个子句只移动其"所有者"智能体，攻击者在被测车未反应之前先针对未反应版本求解，最后用同一规格的监控器（monitor）判定接受与否，从而把有效性校验与场景定义合并为一。

Result: 在nuScenes与CommonRoad上，在与规格无关的独立评判下，单一参数集生成有效场景的比率比"按场景类型逐类调权的加权目标"高0.14；在公开数据可表达的16类碰撞前（pre-crash）场景中，有11类仅凭自然语言描述即可生成至少一个有效场景。学习型规划器在生成场景上的失败率高于在其原始种子场景上的失败率；用这些场景微调可降低规划器在关键场景下的失败率，且增益幅度由标签中某一子句所要求的时间车距（time headway）决定，而非由生成器决定。

Conclusion: 将安全关键场景表述为角色类型化的STL规格，可把场景刻画、攻击者求解与有效性判定统一为单一可编译、可检查的形式，消除逐场景类型调权并避免被测车与攻击者的耦合优化问题；实验表明该方法生成有效性更高、覆盖面更广，生成的场景对规划器构成真实挑战并能带来可迁移的性能提升，且提升幅度取决于规格本身而非生成模型。

Abstract: Safety-critical scenarios are rare in recorded driving and must be generated to test and train motion planners of automated vehicles. In guided diffusion models, each scenario type needs its own weighted objective, separate from the validity check. Optimized together with the attacker, the vehicle under test can cancel the attack or draw it toward itself. We state each scenario as a signal temporal logic specification with clauses typed by role. Every clause names the agent it may change and the agents it may read, and the vehicle under test reads the attacker only from the attack onset. A large language model drafts the specification from a description, and a type checker accepts it. A compiler derives the optimization: it edits a diffusion model's samples by the smallest change that satisfies the specification, each clause moves only its owner, the attacker is solved against the unreacted vehicle under test before its response, and the monitor of the same specification decides acceptance. On nuScenes and CommonRoad, judged independently of the specification, one parameter set produced valid scenarios at a rate 0.14 above a weighted objective tuned per scenario type, and descriptions yielded at least one valid scenario for 11 of the 16 pre-crash types expressible in the public data. Learned planners fail more often on the generated scenarios than on their seeds. Fine-tuning on them lowers a planner's failure rate on critical scenarios, and the time headway one clause of the label requires, not the generator, sets the size of the gain.

</details>


### [74] [Superellipse Function: A Powerful Geometric Tool for the Design and Deformation Modeling of Tendon-Driven Continuum Manipulators With Flexural Patterns Experiencing Tension Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29801)
*Yang Liu,Farshid Alambeigi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出以超椭圆函数作为几何工具来设计与建模具有不连续截面几何的腱驱动连续体操纵器（TD-CM）的弯曲图案，并给出考虑分布/集中摩擦导致张力损失的建模框架、求解算法及设计空间/设计曲线方法，经仿真与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腱驱动连续体操纵器在部分约束腱的情况下会产生分布与集中摩擦力，导致张力损失并影响变形行为；而具有不连续截面几何的柔性图案（FP）缺乏直观、通用的几何参数化与设计手段。

Method: 采用超椭圆函数参数化柔性图案几何；建立四个关联几何参数、运动学与变形行为的函数映射；提出配套的数学模型与求解算法以刻画分布与集中摩擦力对张力损失和变形的影响；并引入设计空间与设计曲线的概念支持直观设计。

Result: 基于超椭圆函数设计并制造的TD-CM上开展了多项仿真与实验研究，采用不同误差指标对结果进行了分析与比较。

Conclusion: 超椭圆函数可作为通用且直观的几何工具用于TD-CM柔性图案设计与建模，所提框架能有效刻画摩擦引起的张力损失与变形行为，并借助设计空间/设计曲线实现直观设计。

Abstract: In this article, we feature the Superellipse function as a geometric and versatile tool for effective design and modeling of flexural patterns (FPs) in tendon-driven continuum manipulators (TD-CMs) with discontinuous cross section geometries. We also propose a unique complementary mathematical framework and solution algorithm for modeling the effects of distributed and concentrated friction forces- caused by partially-constrained tendons- on tension loss and deformation behavior of a TD-CM designed using a Superellipse function. Particularly, by employing this geometric function, we establish four intuitive functional mappings relating the manipulator's geometric parameters, kinematics, and deformation behavior. Thanks to the features of these intuitive mappings and Superellipse function, we then introduce the concepts of design space and design curve to facilitate intuitive design of a TD-CM with a generic FP geometry experiencing tendon tension loss. To thoroughly evaluate the performance of this novel framework and its aforementioned features, we performed various simulation and experimental studies on TD-CMs designed and fabricated based on a Superellipse function. Results were then analyzed and compared using different error metrics.

</details>


### [75] [How Should a Home Robot Update Its Memories When Human Routines Change?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29818)
*Oliver Wang,Mani Srivastava*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文探究家庭机器人在"会反复变化的日常规律"下如何记忆物体位置，发现保留完整观察历史的简单规则优于仅存语言模型摘要的记忆，而摘要几乎无额外增益，并呼吁建立评估"规律回归"能力的长期基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭机器人在做家务时需要知道物体在哪，但住户会因生病等原因改变物品摆放习惯，之后又恢复原状。机器人记忆既要适应新规律，又不能删除那些日后可能再次有用的经验。现有关于"语言模型写记忆摘要"的方法都在静态世界中被评估，无法检验规律变化与回归这一关键场景。

Method: 构建居民"生病—康复—再次生病"的模拟家庭环境。对比三类方法：(1) 规则法，保留物体曾出现过的所有房间；(2) 仅保存文字摘要的语言模型记忆；(3) 既能读摘要又能读原始观察历史(sighting history)的语言模型记忆。此外做消融：在有历史之上再加摘要；以及删除第一次生病期间的记忆，观察性能变化。

Result: 规则法优于所有仅摘要型记忆；让语言模型访问原始观察历史可以弥补差距；在历史基础上额外生成摘要没有可测量的提升。当疾病复发时，保留历史的方法搜索表现优于第一次生病期间，而仅摘要方法没有一致改善。删除第一次生病记忆会消除规则法的全部收益以及摘要法的一部分收益。摘要确实记录了生病期间物体去向，但很少把"病期模式"与"正常作息"区分开。

Conclusion: 主流"用语言模型摘要机器人记忆"的做法难以记住可能回归的规律事实，对长期部署是隐患。建议未来基准显式评估"临时规律改变后又恢复"这一记忆能力，并提示保留原始观察历史比纯摘要更可靠。

Abstract: A home robot that helps with chores needs to know where objects are, but people move objects according to routines that change and later change back. The robot's memory must adapt to the new routine without erasing experience that may become useful again. One approach is to have a language model maintain written summaries of the home from the robot's own observations, but existing evaluations of such memories use static worlds. We explore simulated households where a resident gets sick, recovers, and later gets sick again. We compare a rule that keeps every room an object has been seen in, language-model memories that store only written summaries, and language-model memories that can also read the raw history of sightings. The rule outperforms every summary-only memory, and giving language models the sighting history closes the gap. Surprisingly, writing summaries on top of the history adds nothing we can measure. When the illness returns, methods that keep the history search better than they did the first time, while summary-only methods show no consistent improvement. Deleting the first illness from memory removes all of the rule's gain and part of theirs. The summaries recorded where objects went during the illness but rarely distinguished sick-day patterns from normal routines. Our findings suggest that common approaches to summarizing robot memories struggle to remember facts about a routine that might return, highlighting the need for future benchmarks to evaluate this aspect of long-term robot deployment.

</details>


### [76] [Arcadian: Open-Source Humanoid Ecosystem for Accessible and Customizable Research and Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29868)
*Zelin Shen,Gabriel Ramos Salinas,Qiyu Zhang,Yue Qin,Chen Qian,Yanran Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 Arcadian——一个开源的人形机器人生态（约1米高、成本约6800美元），采用嵌入式金属3D打印实现高强度成本比，配套CAD、BOM、装配文档与训练部署代码，并提供社区支持，以降低科研与教学团队的准入门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 米级人形机器人的复现与改造对制造资源和工程支持有限的科研与教学团队而言仍然困难，现有方案往往在成本、结构耐久性与可定制性之间难以兼顾，进入门槛高。

Method: 提出开源人形机器人生态 Arcadian：硬件上采用金属嵌入3D打印结构以兼顾强度与成本，无需CNC加工且保留设计灵活性；软件上提供在仿真中训练的强化学习运动策略与VR遥操作接口；并通过 Tnkr.ai、MakerWorld、Discord 服务器与项目论坛构建社区反馈驱动的迭代机制，公开全部CAD模型、物料清单、装配文档及训练部署代码。

Result: 大量仿真与硬件测试验证了设计：仿真训练的强化学习运动策略成功迁移到实物样机，VR遥操作界面可驱动双臂完成简单操作任务；机器人高约1米，整机成本约6800美元。

Conclusion: Arcadian 在成本效益、结构耐久性与完全可定制性之间取得平衡，显著降低了人形机器人研究与教育的进入门槛，并通过开源资源与社区支持形成可持续迭代的生态。

Abstract: Reproducing and modifying a meter-scale humanoid remains challenging for research and teaching groups with limited fabrication resources and engineering support. This paper presents Arcadian, an open-source humanoid ecosystem that offers accessible and customizable humanoid hardware and software with community support. Arcadian aims to strike a balance among cost-effectiveness, structural durability, and full customizability, lowering the barrier to entry for research and education. Specifically, this meter-scale humanoid adopts metal-embedded 3D printing to achieve a high strength-to-cost ratio, eliminating the need for computer numerical control (CNC) machining while retaining design flexibility. Extensive simulation and hardware tests validate the design: a reinforcement-learning (RL) locomotion policy trained in simulation transfers to the hardware, and a virtual reality (VR) teleoperation interface drives both arms for simple manipulation tasks. The robot is around one meter tall and costs approximately $6,800 to build. All computer-aided design (CAD) models, the bill of materials, assembly documentation, and training and deployment code are publicly released, distributed through Tnkr.ai and MakerWorld and supported by a Discord server and a project forum through which community feedback informs design updates. The ecosystem resources are available at https://anonymous-humanoid.gitbook.io/docs.

</details>


### [77] [ProMix: Efficient Data Mixture Optimization for Robotic Imitation via Proxy-Reference DRO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29873)
*Qie Sima,Wei Xue,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ProMix 用一个 1500 万参数的小代理模型学习各领域采样权重，并将其迁移到参数量达 70 亿的 VLA 主干，从而以与主干无关的 11 GPU 小时成本逼近 Re-Mix 的数据混合优化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型在开放世界操作中表现优异，但当训练数据从异构机器人、任务与环境简单混合（uniform pooling）时性能容易饱和。Re-Mix 这类分布鲁棒的数据混合优化能挽回大部分性能，但其权重搜索需要在全量语料上训练两遍全尺寸策略，成本过高，多数实验室难以承担。

Method: 提出 ProMix，即"代理—参考"式 DRO（proxy-reference DRO）配方在机器人模仿学习上的计算高效实现：使用 1500 万参数的代理模型，以最小化相对冻结参考模型的最坏情况超额损失为目标学习各领域的采样权重；学到的权重无需重新调参即可迁移到最大 7B 参数的 VLA 主干。同时给出对所提方法中参考模型作用的首个受控分析，并引入熵正则。

Result: 在 Open-X Embodiment 数据集的 9500 条真实世界轨迹上评估：均匀混合平均成功率 68.4%，ProMix 提升至 76.2%，在评估噪声范围内与 Re-Mix 相当；混合优化成本降至与主干无关的 11 GPU 小时，而 50M 对比规模下的全尺度搜索需 16 GPU 小时，7B 规模需 192 GPU 小时；最差领域绝对提升 +17.1%。消融显示移除参考模型会带来过度悲观（平均 -3.8%，最差领域 -14.3% 成功率），熵正则可防止权重崩塌。

Conclusion: ProMix 使分布鲁棒数据混合优化在普通实验室算力下变得可行，且权重可跨主干规模迁移；参考模型与熵正则对该配方不可或缺。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models excel in open-world manipulation, yet their performance often saturates when training data are naively pooled across heterogeneous robots, tasks, and environments. Distributionally robust data mixture optimization, exemplified by Re-Mix, can recover much of this performance, but its weight search requires training full-scale policies twice on the entire corpus, placing it out of reach for most laboratories. We present ProMix, a compute-efficient instantiation of the proxy-reference DRO recipe for robotic imitation learning: a 15M-parameter proxy learns per-domain sampling weights by minimizing worst-case excess loss against a frozen reference model, and the learned weights transfer, without re-tuning, to VLA backbones up to 7B parameters. We further provide, to our knowledge, the first controlled analysis of the reference model's role in robotic DRO, showing that removing it induces over-pessimism toward noisy, low-quality domains (-3.8% average and -14.3% worst-domain success rate), and that entropy regularization is needed to prevent weight collapse. Evaluated on 9500 real-world episodes from the Open-X Embodiment dataset, ProMix improves the average success rate over uniform mixing from 68.4% to 76.2%, matching Re-Mix within evaluation noise while reducing mixture-optimization cost to a backbone-independent 11 GPU-hours - versus 16 GPU-hours at the 50M comparison scale and 192 GPU-hours at 7B for full-scale searches - with a +17.1% absolute gain on the worst-case domain.

</details>


### [78] [Motion Mamba 2.0: Autoregressive Diffusion Mamba for Long-Horizon Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29897)
*Yue Huang,Tianshan Zhang,Zeyu Zhang,Jayeon Kang,Akide Liu,Sungjoon Choi,Dacheng Tao,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Motion Mamba 2.0，一种基于 Mamba 的根-身体扩散架构，结合局部注意力、可恢复循环状态与持久内容记忆（PCM），实现长时程、实时可控的人体运动生成，并通过 CUDA Graph、算子融合等执行优化获得显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互动画与人形机器人需要长时程、实时可控的运动生成，须响应变化的指令与空间目标。现有自回归扩散方法逐步生成运动，但有限的注意力缓存难以访问久远历史，且重复去噪带来大量推理开销。

Method: Motion Mamba 2.0：以 Mamba 构建根-身体扩散架构，融合局部注意力与可恢复的循环状态；持久内容记忆（PCM）将 Mamba 循环状态中的压缩运动历史、状态变化及文本条件特征转化为动态更新的内容槽，供局部注意力访问。推理加速方面设计了专用执行方案，结合 CUDA Graph 重放、算子融合与执行流简化，同时保持规范的历史更新。

Result: 在重定向 HiPHI 上实现最长 39.52 秒 rollout 的准确终端控制；在 BONES Seed 与重定向 HumanML3D 的短时程对比中保持较强的空间控制与文本-运动匹配。RTX 4090 上完整窗口延迟中位数由 1685.35 ms 降至 276.14 ms，配对加速中位数 6.138 倍，且输出与提交状态完全一致。Unitree G1 上的真实世界实验展示了顺序文本引导动作组合、富有表现力的运动及键鼠交互式移动控制。

Conclusion: 通过 Mamba 循环状态记忆与内容记忆机制，配合执行层优化，Motion Mamba 2.0 在长时程可控性、实时性与真实机器人部署上均取得良好效果。

Abstract: Long-horizon, real-time controllable motion generation is essential for interactive animation and humanoid robotics, where an agent must respond to changing instructions and spatial goals while moving. Existing autoregressive diffusion methods generate motion incrementally, but bounded attention caches limit access to distant history, while repeated denoising introduces substantial inference overhead. We present Motion Mamba 2.0, a Mamba-based root-body diffusion architecture that integrates local attention, resumable recurrent states, and persistent content memory (PCM) for long-horizon controllable motion generation. PCM transforms compressed motion history from Mamba recurrent states, together with state changes and text-conditioned features, into dynamically updated content slots accessible to local attention. To enable efficient inference, we further develop an execution scheme tailored to this architecture, combining CUDA Graph replay, fused kernels, and execution-flow simplification while preserving the canonical history update. Evaluations on retargeted HiPHI demonstrate accurate terminal control over rollouts of up to 39.52 s, while short-horizon comparisons on BONES Seed and retargeted HumanML3D show that the model also retains strong spatial control and text-motion matching. On an RTX 4090, the optimized execution reduces median complete-window latency from 1685.35 to 276.14 ms, achieving a median paired speedup of 6.138$\times$ while preserving identical outputs and committed states in the tested rollouts. Moreover, we conduct extensive real-world evaluation on the Unitree G1, demonstrating sequential text-guided action composition, expressive motion, and interactive locomotion through keyboard and mouse control.

</details>


### [79] [UnderGrasp: Envelopment-Oriented Grasp Refinement for an Underactuated Gripper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29907)
*Zhiwen Song,Ruidi Liu,Guanglu Jia,Zuowei Chen,Hao Wen,Qing Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出UnderGrasp，一个面向欠驱动夹爪的事后抓取精化与评估框架，复用冻结生成器的候选与质量先验，联合建模目标、环境、支撑面与接近路径，预测插入深度、预抓取宽度和目标闭合宽度，并用包覆质量与交互质量排序，提升成功率与包覆率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欠驱动夹爪依靠被动柔顺实现包覆抓取，但通用抓取生成器只预测末端位姿与单一宽度，无法刻画"接近—闭合"过程；传统碰撞过滤也难以区分有害干涉与可接受接触，导致候选被过度拒绝或执行不可靠。

Method: 以冻结的抓取生成器为上游，复用其候选与质量先验；模型联合编码目标、局部环境、支撑面与接近路径几何，为每个候选提出多种插入深度、预抓取宽度与目标闭合宽度的组合；将分别预测的包覆质量与交互质量与上游先验融合排序，权衡可行性、包覆性与场景扰动；后端仅在夹爪专用仿真生成的配置与交互标签上训练，无需真机抓取标签。

Result: 真机实验（欠驱动夹爪）中，AnyGrasp 的总体成功率/包覆率由0.55/0.12提升至0.82/0.55，GraspNet 由0.42/0.08提升至0.65/0.36；将固定插入深度增至40 mm虽使包覆率升至0.35，但成功率降至0.52，说明自适应精化的价值；接近过程中涉及目标接触与环境接触的试验，条件成功率分别从0.36升至0.89、从0.25升至0.86。

Conclusion: 通过任务级参数（插入深度、预抓取宽度、闭合宽度）的自适应精化与双维度质量评估，可在不更换生成器、不使用真机抓取标签的条件下显著提升欠驱动夹爪的成功率与包覆质量，并在存在可接受接触时显著提高执行可靠性。

Abstract: Underactuated grippers can form enveloping grasps through passive compliance, while the terminal pose and single width predicted by generic grasp generators do not fully capture the approach-closure process. Conventional collision filtering also struggles to distinguish harmful interference from acceptable contact. We present UnderGrasp, a post-hoc grasp refinement and evaluation framework for an underactuated gripper that reuses grasp candidates and quality priors from a frozen generator. The model jointly encodes the target, local environment, support surface, and approach-path geometry to propose diverse combinations of insertion depth, pre-grasp width, and target closing width for each candidate. These actions are ranked by combining separately predicted envelopment and interaction quality with upstream priors, balancing grasp feasibility, envelopment, and scene disturbance rather than rejecting all environmental contact. The back end is trained solely on configuration and interaction labels generated by gripper-specific simulations, without real-robot grasp labels. Real-robot experiments with an underactuated gripper show that UnderGrasp improves the overall success rate (SR) and the envelopment rate (ER) among successful grasps from 0.55/0.12 to 0.82/0.55 with AnyGrasp, and from 0.42/0.08 to 0.65/0.36 with GraspNet. In contrast, increasing the fixed insertion depth to 40 mm improves ER to 0.35 but reduces SR to 0.52, supporting the value of adaptive refinement. For trials involving target or environmental contact during approach, the conditional success rates increase from 0.36 to 0.89 and from 0.25 to 0.86, respectively, demonstrating more reliable execution under contact. Project page: https://dishenliu2004-hub.github.io/undergrasp-site/

</details>


### [80] [SuperNav-1.5: Structured Spatial Memory For Agentic Navigation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29912)
*Jiarui Guo,Jinkai Zhang,Yuanhong Yu,Yongchen Zai,Futang Zhu,Ruizhen Hu,Sida Peng,Xiaowei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SuperNav-1.5，一种由智能体自建结构化空间记忆的场景级导航系统，通过"区域分割占据地图 + 地图锚定记忆节点"实现跨会话、连续多目标的高效导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有场景记忆方案存在明显缺陷：稠密场景图给多模态大模型带来沉重推理与上下文开销；图像—文本记录又无法直接转换为可执行的几何路径。因此需要一种既轻量又可执行的空间记忆表示，以支持跨会话与连续物体目标的高效导航。

Method: 构建由智能体自身维护的结构化空间记忆：以区域分割的占据地图为骨架，并在地图上标注智能体维护的、与地图对齐的记忆节点；该地图既引导智能体探索未访问区域，也提供通往已知位置的可执行路径。针对多会话场景，提出智能体引导的重定位方法：由当前观测与存储记忆推断区域先验，用以约束几何匹配，从而在存储地图中完成机器人定位。

Result: 在单目标、多目标与需求驱动导航基准上均优于基线方法，路径效率提升尤为显著；多会话评测显示所构建记忆可被复用；并在真实四足机器人上完成部署验证。

Conclusion: 结构化、地图锚定的空间记忆是兼顾效率与可执行性的有效场景级记忆设计，可支撑跨会话记忆复用与真实机器人部署。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge of scene-level memory design for efficient navigation across sessions and successive object goals. Recent methods commonly represent a scene using scene graphs or image--text records. However, dense scene-graph representations impose substantial reasoning and context overhead on multimodal large language models, while image--text records cannot be directly converted into executable geometric routes. To address these limitations, we propose SuperNav-1.5, an agentic navigation system equipped with a structured spatial memory constructed by the agent. The memory consists of an area-segmented occupancy map annotated with agent-maintained, map-grounded memory nodes. This map guides the agent toward unvisited areas and provides executable routes to known locations. For multi-session navigation, we introduce agent-guided relocalization, whereby the agent infers an area prior from current observations and stored memory records to constrain geometric matching and localize the robot within the stored map. Together, these designs enable efficient navigation across session and successive object goals. Experiments show that our method outperforms baselines on single-object, multi-object, and demand-driven navigation benchmarks, with particularly strong gains in path efficiency. Multi-session evaluation demonstrates that the constructed memory can be reused, while deployment on a real quadruped robot further shows the system's applicability.

</details>


### [81] [MoMa-NMP: Scene-Scale Neural Motion Planning for Mobile Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29913)
*Adam Kan,Jason Jingzhou Liu,Jiahui Yang,Kenneth Shaw,Deepak Pathak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 MoMa-DemoSmith 演示生成流水线与 MoMa-NMP 神经全身运动规划器，解决移动机器人在房间级复杂环境中难以规模化生成高质量训练轨迹的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭等复杂环境中的移动机械臂需长时间尺度协调基座与机械臂，完成绕障、窄道通行与受限工作空间穿越。神经运动规划器能从部分观测实现快速闭环规划，但扩展到房间级场景困难，因为生成高质量训练轨迹本身就是难题。

Method: MoMa-DemoSmith 将问题拆分为两阶段：面向大型复杂场景的全局路径发现，以及面向避障的局部运动精修；该分离使得在程序化生成场景中批量产生数百万条具有挑战性的全身轨迹成为可能。随后用行为克隆训练 MoMa-NMP，再做特权在线策略蒸馏。

Result: 在自建仿真基准上达到 87.0% 成功率；在未见过的真实场景 80 次试验中成功率为 77.5%。

Conclusion: 全局—局部解耦的演示生成方式可有效扩展神经移动操作规划的训练数据规模，并使策略在仿真与真实场景中均获得较强的闭环规划性能。

Abstract: Mobile manipulators operating in homes and other complex environments must coordinate their base and arm over long horizons, navigating around obstacles, through narrow passages, and between constrained workspaces. Neural motion planners can provide fast closed-loop planning from partial observations, but scaling them to room-sized environments is difficult because generating high-quality training trajectories is itself a challenging motion-planning problem. To address this, we introduce MoMa-DemoSmith, a demonstration-generation pipeline that separates the problem into global path discovery for large complex scenes and local motion refinement for collision avoidance. This key separation enables the generation of millions of challenging whole-body trajectories across our procedurally generated scenes. We train MoMa-NMP on these demonstrations through behavior cloning (BC) followed by privileged on-policy distillation, achieving 87.0% success on our simulation benchmark and 77.5% across 80 trials in unseen real-world scenes. See video results at https://adamhkan.com/moma-nmp/.

</details>


### [82] [TidyFlow: Geometry-Aware Tabletop Arrangement Generation with Human-Aligned Tidiness Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29918)
*Jiahui Qian,Tiandong Wang,Shubao Fan,Chenyu Niu,Erqi Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TidyFlow：结合条件Flow Matching与拓扑感知几何细化的桌面物品整齐摆放生成框架，三迭代细化后几何有效率从23.21%/10.58%提升至88.91%/57.91%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 桌面物品摆放需同时兼顾物体间的功能关联与有限桌面空间内的几何约束（不碰撞、不越界），而现有生成方法难以兼顾这两方面，容易产生几何错误。

Method: 构建GTL与WTL两个桌面布局数据集，并提出六维整齐度评分函数；用条件Flow Matching以物体属性和桌面几何为条件学习多模态布局分布；再基于碰撞与越界模式构造拓扑感知细化数据，用成对感知注意力网络迭代修正物体位姿。

Result: 三次细化迭代后，GTL几何有效率由23.21%升至88.91%，WTL由10.58%升至57.91%；用户偏好研究显示所提整齐度评分与人类排序正相关，Spearman系数0.5543。

Conclusion: 生成+几何细化两阶段范式能有效提升桌面布局的几何有效性，且所提可量化整齐度指标与人类偏好具有一定一致性，可作为布局质量评估依据。

Abstract: Generating tidy tabletop arrangements that align with human organizational preferences requires considering both functional relationships among objects and geometric constraints within limited tabletop space. We present TidyFlow, a tabletop arrangement generation framework that integrates conditional Flow Matching with topology-aware geometric refinement. We construct two tabletop arrangement datasets based on functional grouping and geometric constraints, namely General Tabletop Layouts (GTL) and Workstation Tabletop Layouts (WTL), and introduce a six-dimensional tidiness scoring function. Conditional Flow Matching is employed to learn a multimodal distribution of target arrangements conditioned on object attributes and tabletop geometry. To address geometric errors in generated layouts, we construct topology-aware refinement data based on collision and boundary-violation patterns, and employ a pair-aware attention network to iteratively refine object poses. Experiments demonstrate that, after three refinement iterations, TidyFlow improves geometric validity from 23.21% to 88.91% on GTL and from 10.58% to 57.91% on WTL. Furthermore, a human preference study demonstrates a positive correlation between the proposed tidiness scores and human rankings, achieving a Spearman correlation coefficient of 0.5543.

</details>


### [83] [SAGE-Drive: Benefit-Aware Adaptive Reasoning for Autonomous Driving Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29947)
*Donglei Rong,Wenbin Yao,Congcong Bai,Xi Gao,Qiaoqiao Ren,Sheng Jin,Cheng Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE-Drive框架，通过预测"推理收益"（有无显式推理时规划误差的降低量）并在给定推理预算下仅对高收益场景调用显式推理，从而以少量计算开销获得接近全量推理的规划精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式推理能帮助自动驾驶车辆理解复杂交通交互并支持轨迹规划，但带来额外计算开销；且推理收益随场景变化，实验中仅53.5%场景受益、46.5%反而变差，平均收益接近零，因此需要判断"何时"进行显式推理。

Method: 先对有/无显式推理的成对规划结果进行分析，以规划误差的降低量作为"推理收益"的监督标签；再用无推理初始轨迹的几何特征与多模态场景信息学习预测该收益；最后在预设推理预算下，仅对预测收益最高的场景调用显式推理。

Result: 在1,497个成对驾驶场景上，显式推理在53.5%情况下提升精度、46.5%情况下降低精度。测试集上，20%预算时ADE为1.5191 m（随机选择为1.5252 m）；35%预算时ADE为1.5148 m，接近全场景推理的1.5141 m。

Conclusion: 预测的推理收益可作为有效的推理选择准则，使显式推理仅在能提升规划精度的场景中触发，以一小部分计算成本取得接近全量推理的效果。

Abstract: Explicit reasoning enables autonomous vehicles (AVs) to interpret complex traffic interactions and support trajectory planning, but introduces additional computational demands. Determining when explicit reasoning can improve planning accuracy remains a key challenge, as its benefits vary across driving scenarios. To address this challenge, this study proposes Scene-Adaptive Gating of Explicit Reasoning for Driving (SAGE-Drive), a benefit-aware adaptive reasoning framework for autonomous driving planning. First, paired planning outcomes with and without explicit reasoning are analyzed to measure the reasoning benefit, i.e., the reduction in planning error achieved by explicit reasoning, which serves as the supervision label. SAGE-Drive then learns to predict these benefits using geometric features of the initial trajectory planned without reasoning and multimodal scene information, and invokes explicit reasoning only for the scenarios with the highest predicted benefits under a predefined reasoning budget. Experiments on 1,497 paired driving scenarios reveal that explicit reasoning improves planning accuracy in 53.5% of cases while degrading it in 46.5%, yielding a near-zero average benefit. On the test set, with a 20% reasoning budget, SAGE-Drive achieves an average displacement error (ADE) of 1.5191 m, compared with 1.5252 m for random selection. At a 35% budget, it achieves an ADE of 1.5148 m, close to the 1.5141 m obtained when reasoning is applied to every scenario. These findings establish the predicted reasoning benefit as an effective criterion for reasoning selection, allowing explicit reasoning to be invoked where it improves planning accuracy at a fraction of its computational cost.

</details>


### [84] [TeleMultiStream: An Integrated Robot Teleoperation System with Low-Latency Multi-Stream Video Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29980)
*Chenchen Lv,Lingchen Hu,Yaosai Zhang,Chunlan Li,Gengkui Wei,Wei Xin,Li Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 TeleMultiStream，一个将多流 WebRTC 视频回传与 UDP 位姿下行相结合的机器人遥操作系统，通过缓冲区复用、有界队列和原生 XR 渲染降低延迟；在有线局域网 ZED Mini 测试中，Glass-to-Glass 延迟中位数为 60 ms（软件编码）和 66 ms（高性能硬件编码），较 82 ms 的 RobotVision-PC 参考分别低 26.8% 和 19.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练具身智能模型需要大规模高质量示范数据，机器人遥操作是重要采集手段；低延迟遥操作还能让人在 DAgger 类交互学习中及时接管并提供纠正性示范。因此需要一套低延迟、支持异构视频源的遥操作系统。

Method: 系统由多流 WebRTC 视频反馈与 UDP 位姿下行组成，支持异构视频源，并采用缓冲区复用、有界队列和原生 XR 渲染以减少处理与等待时间；在有线局域网环境下用 ZED Mini 对软件编码与高性能硬件编码两种模式进行对比评测，涵盖单流与三流、低分辨率与高分辨率等配置。

Result: 单流 ZED Mini 测试中，软件与硬件编码的 Glass-to-Glass 延迟中位数分别为 60 ms 和 66 ms，比 82 ms 的 RobotVision-PC 基线低 26.8% 和 19.5%；低分辨率下两种编码视觉延迟相当，三流配置 1 的中位数分别为 72 ms 和 66 ms；高分辨率配置 2 中硬件编码将平均编码时间从 11.85 ms 降至 8.80 ms、G2G 中位数从 94 ms 降至 88 ms，CPU 占用约 67%（软件编码为 265%）；三流接收时 100 Hz UDP 平均校正单向延迟为 1.576 和 1.800 ms，平均估计丢包率均为 0.0033%。

Conclusion: TeleMultiStream 在多流、异构视频源条件下实现了低延迟、低丢包的遥操作反馈，尤其在高分辨率场景中硬件编码在延迟与 CPU 开销上均优于软件编码，可支撑用于机器人示范数据采集的响应式遥操作。

Abstract: Training embodied-intelligence models requires large-scale, high-quality demonstration data. Robot teleoperation provides an important means of collecting these data. Low-latency teleoperation also helps human operators take control promptly and provide corrective demonstrations in DAgger-based interactive learning. This paper presents TeleMultiStream, a robot teleoperation system that combines multi-stream WebRTC video feedback with UDP pose downlink. The system supports heterogeneous video sources and uses buffer reuse, bounded queues, and native XR rendering to reduce processing and waiting. In wired-LAN ZED Mini tests, TeleMultiStream's software and high-performance hardware encoding modes achieved dominant-interval Glass-to-Glass latency medians of 60 and 66~ms, respectively. These medians were 26.8\% and 19.5\% lower than the 82-ms RobotVision-PC reference, respectively. At the lower tested resolutions, the two TeleMultiStream encoding modes provided comparable visual latency. Their respective medians in three-stream Configuration~1 were 72 and 66~ms. In the higher-resolution Configuration~2, high-performance hardware encoding outperformed software encoding in latency and CPU utilization. It reduced mean encoding time from 11.85 to 8.80~ms and the dominant-interval Glass-to-Glass median from 94 to 88~ms. Its CPU utilization was approximately 67\%, compared with 265\% for software encoding. During three-stream reception with high-performance hardware encoding, the mean corrected one-way UDP latencies at 100~Hz were 1.576 and 1.800~ms in Configurations~1 and~2, respectively. Mean estimated packet loss was 0.0033\% in both configurations. These results support responsive teleoperation for robot demonstration collection.

</details>


### [85] [One Latent World for Heterogeneous LiDARs: A Scan Pattern Agnostic 4D World Model via Direct Ray Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30007)
*Minwoo Jung,Dongjae Lee,DongGuw Lee,Yeong Sang Park,Maurice Fallon,Ayoung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种扫描模式无关的4D LiDAR世界模型，将场景与传感器观测解耦，一个空间隐变量可由同一安装位置的异构LiDAR直接观测，无需重新生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR世界模型通常按训练传感器的扫描模式合成扫描，或从中间3D表面/场渲染，前者限制向其他扫描模式的迁移，后者会导致错误深度与虚假回波。

Method: 解码器直接从隐变量预测每条射线的命中概率与深度，结合全局场景上下文与在初步深度处检索的局部特征；每个射线独立解码，共享射线的LiDAR获得相同回波；再用扩散Transformer在该隐空间中生成场景，并依据自车与物体运动对观测隐变量做warp以预测4D序列。

Result: 在KITTI-360和nuScenes上生成逼真场景，预测帧间配准误差较最佳对比世界模型降低约70%；解码器深度误差比其它世界模型所用渲染低19%–28%；生成扫描对多个下游任务有益。

Conclusion: 无需中间表面或场的隐空间解码实现了模式无关、跨传感器一致且更准确的LiDAR世界建模，并支持4D预测与下游应用。

Abstract: LiDAR world models generate and predict metric 3D observations in the form of LiDAR scans. They typically synthesize scans following the scan pattern of their training sensor or render them from an intermediate 3D surface or field. However, following one scan pattern limits transfer to LiDARs with other patterns, and rendering from intermediate geometry can cause incorrect depths and false returns. In this paper, we propose a scan pattern agnostic 4D LiDAR world model that decouples the scene from sensor observation, so one spatial latent can be observed by heterogeneous LiDARs at the same mounting position without regeneration. Our decoder predicts the hit probability and depth of each ray directly from the latent, combining global scene context with local features retrieved at a preliminary depth. Without an intermediate surface or field, it reduces incorrect depths and false returns, and as each ray is decoded independently from one latent, LiDARs that share a ray receive the same return and observe one consistent scene. Finally, a diffusion transformer generates scenes in this latent and predicts 4D sequences from observed latents warped by the given ego and object motion. On KITTI-360 and nuScenes, our model generates realistic scenes and reduces the registration error between predicted frames by about 70% relative to the best compared world model. Its decoder yields 19 to 28% lower depth error than the rendering used by other world models, and generated scans benefit several downstream tasks. Our code will be open-sourced. More details are available at https://minwoo0611.github.io/OneLatentWorld.

</details>


### [86] [FlexWAM: Learning Flexible Visual Routing for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30024)
*Mingyu Mei,Haojie Xu,Zibo Dai,Hongjie Fang,Shirun Tang,Zhengrui Lv,Yang Liu,Chen Guang,Xingyue Zhao,Huiliang Shen,Zaixing He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlexWAM 通过视内/跨视交替注意力、基于观测条件路由器的视角路由动作注意力，以及基于反事实视角信用训练的 critic，实现按当前观测动态调整对各相机视角的依赖，在五个真实操作任务上平均成功率比最强基线提升 16.4/23.0 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在不同操作阶段会动态调整对各感官模态的依赖，而现有 World Action Models 虽融合多相机观测，却无法根据当前观测自适应地调整对各单独视角的依赖。核心问题是如何在整合互补视觉证据的同时，优先利用与当前动作最相关的视角。

Method: 三个模块：(1) 交替使用视内注意力与跨视注意力，分别捕捉视角特有动态与互补线索；(2) 在动作预测中采用 View-Routed Action Attention，用观测条件路由器为相关视角分配优先级；(3) 为校准路由，利用反事实视角信用训练 critic——通过抑制某视角对动作注意力的贡献后动作损失的敏感度来估计该视角的贡献。

Result: 在两个机器人平台、五个具有挑战性的真实操作任务上，以两种视频骨干网络评估；两个变体相比最强基线的平均任务成功率分别提升 16.4 和 23.0 个百分点。

Conclusion: 按观测自适应路由多视角信息可显著提升世界动作模型在真实操作任务中的表现，反事实视角信用为路由提供了有效监督；方法对视频骨干具有可迁移性。

Abstract: Humans dynamically adjust their reliance on sensory modalities across manipulation stages. Although existing World Action Models (WAMs) fuse multi-camera observations, they struggle to adapt their reliance on individual views based on the current observations. This raises a central question: how can the action expert integrate complementary visual evidence while prioritizing the views most relevant to the current action? FlexWAM addresses this challenge through three modules. First, we alternate within-view and cross-view attention to capture view-specific dynamics and complementary cues. For action prediction, View-Routed Action Attention uses an observation-conditioned router to prioritize relevant views. To calibrate routing, we train a critic using counterfactual view credit, estimated from action loss sensitivity upon suppressing each view's contribution to action attention. We evaluate FlexWAM with two video backbones on five challenging real-world manipulation tasks across two robot platforms. The two variants improve mean task success by 16.4 and 23.0 percentage points over the strongest baseline. Project website: https://flexwam.github.io/.

</details>


### [87] [Design of a Compact Cable-driven 4-DoF Salamander Robotic Limb with Large Range of Motion and High Load Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30026)
*Zhuowen Li,Qiyuan Fu,Auke Ijspeert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种缆索驱动的4自由度蝾螈式机器人肢体，采用空间布线将执行器移至近端，在保持大运动范围与负载能力的同时实现紧凑轻量设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蝾螈的肢体兼具大运动范围、多自由度和高承载能力，是其游泳与陆行转换的关键；但用直接驱动架构在紧凑肢体内复现这些能力困难，因为多个关节执行器需足够间距或置于远端以避免自碰撞，导致尺寸和转动惯量增大。

Method: 设计缆索驱动4自由度肢体（髋俯仰、偏航、横滚各1自由度，膝1自由度），采用空间缆索布线把执行器布置在近端；通过多段缆索路径的机械补偿、基于模型的前馈解耦以及输出侧关节角反馈来抑制传动耦合。

Result: 与直接驱动肢体相比，质量减少20.4%，投影包络面积减少20.5%，估算有效偏航轴惯量减少31.9%；实验验证各关节在45°、118°、118°、135°范围内可独立解耦控制，抬升测试中可支撑至少5.4倍自重的等效垂直载荷。

Conclusion: 该肢体为后续蝾螈运动与神经控制研究提供了紧凑且具生物代表性的平台。

Abstract: A compact yet agile and powerful limb is essential for amphibious legged robots that transition between spine-based swimming and leg-based terrestrial walking, as salamanders do. Salamander limbs combine large ranges of motion (ROM), multiple degrees of freedom (DoFs), and high load capacity. These features enable swinging and body-weight support during walking, as well as folding alongside the body during swimming. Their proximal kinematics can be represented by three rotational DoFs at the hip and one at the knee. Replicating these capabilities within a compact robotic limb is challenging with direct-drive architectures, because multiple joint-mounted actuators must be sufficiently spaced or placed distally to avoid self-collision, increasing limb size and rotational inertia. Here we present a cable-driven 4-DoF salamander robotic limb with a spatial cable-routing architecture that relocates actuators proximally while preserving large ROM and load capacity. Transmission-induced coupling is mitigated by mechanically compensating multi-segment cable paths, model-based feedforward decoupling, and output-side joint angle feedback. Compared with a direct-drive limb, the proposed design reduces mass by 20.4%, projected envelope area by 20.5%, and estimated effective yaw-axis inertia by 31.9%. Experiments verify independent, decoupled control over joint-angle ranges of 45 deg, 118 deg, 118 deg, and 135 deg for hip pitch, yaw, roll, and knee flexion/extension, respectively. During lifting tests, the limb supported an equivalent vertical load of at least 5.4 times its own weight. The proposed limb provides a compact, biologically representative platform for future studies of salamander locomotion and neural control.

</details>


### [88] [Spiking World-Action Models for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30041)
*Mingquan Liu,Peng Zhou,Shanming Yao,Shuai Wang,Simo Wu,Malu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SpikingWAM——首个脉冲神经网络世界动作模型，用稀疏任务骨架作为世界状态，在仿真与真机上以极低能耗达到竞争性成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAM）需多次运行大网络、难以部署于机器人；脉冲神经网络（SNN）低功耗，但现有WAM以稠密视频为世界状态，脉冲版会把大部分算力浪费在背景上。

Method: 采用任务骨架状态（TSS），仅保留机械臂与任务目标物体的关键点，并用小型脉冲前端从图像估计物体关键点；提出体坐标脉冲编码（BFSC）与网格重合注意力（GCA）保持毫米级精度；提出持久单步读出（PSR），使每个规划仅需运行网络一次。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin 2.0仿真基准上，以远低于ANN方法的能耗取得竞争性成功率，并在LIBERO-Plus上超过所有对比方法；真实机器人实验也验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 任务骨架使脉冲网络能以极低能耗实现WAM，为低功耗具身智能提供新思路。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) predict how the world responds to actions and offer a promising path toward general embodied intelligence, but they are large and run the network many times per plan, so they are hard to deploy on robots. Spiking neural networks (SNNs) compute with sparse binary spikes and use additions instead of multiplications, which saves power. However, existing WAMs use dense videos as the world state, so a spiking version would spend most of its computation on the background. We therefore introduce SpikingWAM, the first spiking WAM, which uses a sparse task skeleton as the world state. Inspired by biological motor control, which attends only to the key positions of a task, the Task Skeleton State (TSS) keeps the keypoints of the arm and the task objects, and a small spiking front end estimates the object keypoints from images. Body Frame Spike Coding (BFSC) and Grid Coincidence Attention (GCA) keep millimetre precision, and the Persistent Single Step Readout (PSR) runs the network once per plan. On the simulated benchmarks LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus and RoboTwin 2.0, SpikingWAM achieves competitive success rates with far less energy than ANN methods and surpasses all compared methods on LIBERO-Plus. Real robot experiments further confirm its effectiveness. These results show that a task skeleton lets spiking networks implement WAMs at very low energy, offering a new solution for low power embodied intelligence. Project page: https://wxqnl.github.io/spikingwam/

</details>


### [89] [RoboLiveMem: Is Robot Memory Solved When the World Does Not Wait?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30047)
*Yunbei Zhang,Yuyou Zhang,Janet Wang,Ming Liu,Yijiang Li,Yikai Wang,Weirui Ye,Ding Zhao,Jihun Hamm*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RoboLiveMem基准，在同一记忆任务上分别在"思考时世界暂停"与"思考时世界持续运行"两种条件下评测机器人代理，揭示现有基准因暂停仿真器而系统性高估了模型的记忆能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人记忆基准在模型推理时暂停仿真器，导致决策期间世界不会变化，模型记住的信息在行动时仍然有效，因而无法反映真实家庭环境中物体被持续移动的情形，也无法区分"遗忘"与"行动过晚"。

Method: 构建包含29个任务族、三个难度层级的基准，覆盖16个BEHAVIOR-1K真实场景（住宅、车间、餐厅）与受控的ManiSkill任务；每个任务混合真实变化与无害的相似干扰物，并设有完全无变化的对照；多数任务配有保持变化在视野内的孪生版本。对16个模型开展34,176个episode的评测。

Result: （1）暂停条件掩盖问题：真实场景中记忆目标发生变化时，8个Claude与GPT模型在暂停条件下平均成功率97%，世界运行时仅50%，且排名改变，如暂停下从不失败的Claude Opus 5.5仅44%。（2）提速无效：更慢的模型表现不更差，仅给2秒决策时间的模型仍比其暂停成功率低至少36个百分点。（3）模型看到了变化，却依据变化结束前的相机帧行动；行动前重新观察可弥合大部分真实场景差距，但同一物体被多次替换时，9个被测模型仍因记忆而失分。

Conclusion: 暂停式基准会显著高估机器人记忆能力，速度并非瓶颈，核心问题是感知—行动之间的时序错配；行动前重新观测是有效的缓解手段，但在反复变化场景下记忆机制仍存在局限。

Abstract: Robot agents built on frontier vision-language models now solve most robot memory benchmarks. Does this mean they can remember what they need in a home, where people and machines keep moving things? Existing memory benchmarks cannot answer this question, because they pause the simulator while the model thinks: nothing can change during a decision, so whatever the robot remembers is still true when it acts. We introduce RoboLiveMem, a benchmark that runs the same memory tasks twice, once with the world paused while the model thinks and once with the world running. It contains 29 task families in three tiers, from 16 realistic BEHAVIOR-1K scenes of homes, workshops and a restaurant to controlled ManiSkill tasks. Every task mixes real changes with harmless look-alikes and runs without any change, so that no fixed strategy succeeds, and most tasks have a twin that keeps the change in view, which separates forgetting from acting too late. Testing 16 models on 34,176 episodes, we find three surprises. (1) Paused results hide the problem: when a remembered object changes in a realistic scene, the 8 Claude and GPT models succeed in 97% of episodes on average with the world paused, but in only 50% with the world running, and their ranking changes: Claude Opus 5.5, which never fails when paused, succeeds in 44%. (2) Speed is not the fix: slower models do no worse, and models given only 2 s per decision still fall at least 36 points below their paused success. (3) Models see the change but act on camera frames from before it ended. Looking again just before acting closes most of the realistic gap, yet when identical objects are swapped several times, all nine models we test still lose points to memory.

</details>


### [90] [HERO: Hierarchical Region-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30072)
*Xulin Xiao,Shengyi Liang,Mingjie Zhang,Chengkai Wu,Chen Chen,Zhigang Li,Jinni Zhou,Jun Ma,Boyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HERO: 分层自主探索框架，全局区域级规划给出长时域引导，局部用双分支图策略在可达候选内做目标选择，兼顾路径效率与低在线计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境规模增大时，路径效率与在线计算量难以兼顾：分层规划器依赖手工局部视点选择规则；图强化学习推理成本随探索图增长，且全局方向仅隐式表达。

Method: 分层框架分离全局规划与学习式局部决策；将学习式目标选择限制在当前区域内的可达候选，以降低图策略推理成本；区域级规划提供长时域引导；提出双分支图策略，平衡前沿效用与朝下一区域的引导。

Result: 在未见过的2D地图上，所评估方法中平均路径长度与目标决策成本最低；MARSIM仿真及三个真实场地部署验证了框架在LiDAR建图与底盘动力学条件下的适用性。

Conclusion: 按层级职责分工、约束学习式候选范围并显式引入区域级引导，可在保持低在线计算的同时提升自主探索的路径效率，并具备真实平台可行性。

Abstract: Autonomous exploration is widely used to map unknown environments, yet maintaining both path efficiency and low online computation remains difficult as environment scale increases. Hierarchical planners reduce global search through coarse regional representations but often rely on handcrafted local viewpoint-selection rules. Graph-based reinforcement learning can improve local decisions, but its inference cost grows with the exploration graph and global direction is usually represented only implicitly. To address these limitations, we present HERO, a hierarchical framework that assigns distinct responsibilities to global planning and learned local decision-making, enabling each level to focus on the function it is best suited to perform. To reduce the growing inference cost of graph-based policies, HERO restricts learned target selection to reachable candidates within the current region while using region-level planning to provide long-horizon guidance. To avoid informative targets causing excessive deviation from this global direction, the proposed Dual-Branch Graph Policy balances Frontier Utility with guidance toward the next region, improving both local exploration and global progress. Experiments on unseen 2D maps show the lowest overall mean path length and target-decision cost among the evaluated methods. MARSIM simulations and deployments at three real-world sites further demonstrate the framework's applicability with LiDAR-based mapping and chassis dynamics.

</details>


### [91] [PushMimic: Learning Adaptive Humanoid Box Pushing via Dynamic Motion Augmentation and Reference Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30077)
*Yulun Zhuang,Hanyu Zhang,Jingyu Liu,Yanran Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PushMimic 框架仅用一条平地运动学参考动作，学习出能适应未知箱体质量与斜坡角度的自适应人行人形机器人推箱策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人形机器人推箱任务中，箱体质量、斜坡倾角等物理属性在推动开始前未知，传统方法需要为每种质量与倾角组合分别提供参考动作，成本高且难以泛化。

Method: 提出 PushMimic：以单条平地运动学参考动作为输入，利用基于采样的轨迹优化，将该参考动作扩展为覆盖多种质量与倾角组合的动力学可行运动数据集；再通过单阶段训练将数据集蒸馏为一个运动跟踪策略，使策略在不观测这些物理属性的情况下自适应调整推动姿态。

Result: 仿真中，在 5–15 kg 箱体质量、最大 15° 斜坡下，成功率由基线（跟踪原始参考动作）的 57.8% 提升至 88.8%，手臂推动代价降低 22%。Unitree G1 硬件实验表明，策略可在连续推动倾斜跑步机时自适应调整躯干俯仰，支持箱体质量达 19 kg、坡度达 12.6°。

Conclusion: PushMimic 只需一条参考动作即可替代“每种质量与倾角各一条参考动作”的做法，实现物理属性未知条件下的自适应人形推箱，并在仿真与真机上验证了有效性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Humanoid box pushing is challenging when the physical properties of the task, such as the box mass and the ramp inclination, are unknown before the push begins. This paper presents PushMimic, a framework that learns adaptive humanoid box pushing from a single level-ground kinematic reference motion. The learned policy adapts its pushing posture to these properties without observing them. PushMimic augments the reference motion into a dynamically feasible motion dataset over these properties with sampling-based trajectory optimization, and distills the dataset into one motion tracking policy in one stage of training. Simulation results over box masses from 5 to 15 kg and ramp inclinations up to 15 degrees show that PushMimic raises the success rate from 57.8% to 88.8% compared with a baseline that tracks the original reference motion. It also reduces the cost of push of the arms by 22%. Hardware experiments on a Unitree G1 humanoid show that the policy adapts its torso pitch during continuous pushing on an inclined treadmill, with box masses up to 19 kg and ramp inclinations up to 12.6 degrees. PushMimic therefore needs only one reference motion, instead of one for every box mass and ramp inclination.

</details>


### [92] [MotionServe: Efficient Multi-Robot Serving by Exploiting Robot Execution Slack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30083)
*Zikang Wen,Yuning Zhang,Dong Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MotionServe是一个共享GPU的VLA策略服务系统，通过将机器人观测时机与服务器端批次形成协同调度，在保证任务成功率的同时显著减少机器人因等待推理而造成的停顿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多台机器人需并发运行众多任务专用的VLA策略，为每台机器人独占GPU成本过高，因此需要共享GPU推理；LoRA虽可让兼容策略共享冻结骨干并实现跨策略批处理，但机器人推理必须跟得上物理执行节奏。动作分块使机器人在等待新预测时仍有缓冲动作可执行，这段剩余缓冲时间构成"执行余量"；余量耗尽机器人就会停顿。提前观测可保留更多余量，却可能导致破坏性的重规划、降低成功率；延后调度以形成更大批次能提高GPU效率，但会消耗余量、增加停顿风险，二者存在权衡。

Method: 提出MotionServe共享GPU服务系统，协调机器人观测时机与服务器端批次形成。其中MotionExec依据预测动作块中的运动结构选择观测触发点，使推理与剩余缓冲执行重叠；SlackBatch将各机器人执行预算与估计的未完成GPU工作量结合，决定何时等待、对哪些请求成批以及何时派发。

Result: 在NVIDIA H200单卡、最多96台仿真机器人共享的场景下，评估了LIBERO与SimplerEnv WidowX任务上的X-VLA以及LIBERO上的OpenVLA-OFT。在96台机器人规模下，相比各模型的最低停顿基线，MotionServe将每回合平均停顿分别降低43.4%和32.6%，任务成功率分别提升8.98和2.30个百分点；骨干共享还使96个策略的权重内存分别减少97.6%和91.1%。

Conclusion: 将机器人侧观测触发与服务器侧批次调度联合设计，能够有效缓解共享GPU推理中"及时性"与"吞吐效率"的矛盾，在近乎不损失成功率的前提下大幅减少物理执行停顿，并借助LoRA骨干共享显著降低多策略部署的内存开销。

Abstract: As robotic arms undertake diverse manipulation tasks, a deployment may need to serve many task-specific vision-language-action (VLA) policies concurrently. Dedicating a GPU to each robot is costly, motivating shared-GPU inference. Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) allows compatible policies to share a frozen backbone with lightweight policy-specific adapters, reducing model-weight memory and enabling cross-policy batching. However, robot inference must keep pace with physical execution. With action chunking, robots continue executing buffered actions while awaiting new predictions, and the remaining buffered time provides each robot's execution slack for serving its next request. Once this slack is exhausted, the robot stalls until new actions arrive, interrupting task progress. Observing earlier preserves more slack but can lead to disruptive replanning and lower task success, whereas delaying dispatch to form larger batches can improve GPU efficiency but consumes slack and risks stalls. To balance these competing demands, we present MotionServe, a shared-GPU serving system that coordinates robot observation timing with server-side batch formation. MotionExec selects observation triggers based on motion structure in predicted action chunks, allowing inference to overlap with remaining buffered execution. SlackBatch combines the resulting per-robot execution budgets with estimated outstanding GPU work to determine when to wait, which requests to batch, and when to dispatch them. We evaluate X-VLA on LIBERO and SimplerEnv's WidowX tasks and OpenVLA-OFT on LIBERO, with up to 96 simulated robots sharing one NVIDIA H200 GPU. At 96 robots, MotionServe reduces mean stall per episode by 43.4% and 32.6% and improves task success by 8.98 and 2.30 percentage points, respectively, over each model's lowest-stall baseline. Backbone sharing also reduces weight memory for 96 policies by 97.6% and 91.1%.

</details>


### [93] [Policy-intrinsic Demonstration Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30099)
*Sunjae Jeong,Geunhyeok Yu,Dowoon Lee,Hyoseok Hwang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"策略内在筛选"(policy-intrinsic curation)新范式，并实例化为PICKER方法：利用预训练策略的前向推理挑选与目标任务错误模式一致的候选演示，效果媲美性能感知筛选而耗时接近检索式筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向新任务微调机器人策略时目标演示稀缺，需要从其他任务的候选演示中补足。现有两类筛选方法各有缺陷：检索式筛选（基于相似度）忽略策略本身，会把"看起来相似"但对策略无益的候选评为高分；性能感知筛选虽能估计候选对策略的实际影响，但需要在候选上训练，耗时过长。因此亟需一种既能考虑策略、又足够高效的筛选方式。

Method: 提出第三条路线——策略内在筛选：直接借助预训练策略估计每个候选对策略的影响，仅通过前向推理完成，无需训练。具体实例为PICKER（Policy-Intrinsic Curation from Known Errors），其准则为：优先选取策略在其上犯的错误与在目标演示上所犯错误相一致的候选演示。

Result: PICKER的成功率达到与性能感知筛选相当的水平，而耗时与检索式筛选大致相当；且其选择结果在不同随机种子下保持稳定。

Conclusion: 策略内在筛选填补了效率与策略相关性之间的空白：以极低计算成本获得接近性能感知筛选的微调收益，并具备良好的选择稳定性，为少样本机器人策略微调的演示筛选提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a robot policy for a new target task requires target demonstrations, but only a few are available. Demonstrations of other tasks can fill this gap, and demonstration curation selects the useful ones among these candidates. Retrieval-based curation selects the candidates that are most similar to the target demonstrations. Since it does not use the policy, any candidate that looks similar receives a high score. Performance-aware curation therefore estimates the effect of each candidate on the policy. However, this requires training on the candidates and takes a long time. We propose a third branch, policy-intrinsic curation, which uses the policy and still takes little time. It estimates this effect with forward passes of the pretrained policy and needs no training. We instantiate this branch with PICKER (Policy-Intrinsic Curation from Known Errors). It selects the candidates on which the policy makes errors that agree with those on the target demonstrations. PICKER reaches success rates comparable to those of performance-aware curation and takes about as long as retrieval-based curation. Its selection is also stable across seeds.

</details>


### [94] [Agent on Guard: Self-Improving Runtime Intervention for Robust Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30101)
*Gengming Cui,Mingfei Han,Jiawei Zhang,Mozheng Liao,Yunkai Guo,Minghao Jin,Tingwen Du,Salman Khan,Xiaojun Chang,Zhihui Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AoG是一个分层框架，通过物理经验让冻结的机器人基础策略与编程智能体实现时序对齐的协作，在RoboDojo-Sim上成功率50.57%、任务分57.26，并大幅降低延迟与token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础策略提供已学得的执行能力，编程智能体能够推理失败并构造恢复行为，但两者如何有效结合的关键问题是：何时干预有益、何时策略可可靠恢复。该问题是时序性的——推理期间物理世界仍在演化，有效的恢复方案可能在决策生效前已不可行。现有方法缺乏对干预时机、恢复窗口与推理耗时的统筹考虑。

Method: 提出Agent on Guard（AoG）分层框架：演化式外层规划器随物理状态分配控制权，将决策与物理时钟对齐，考虑观测新鲜度、剩余恢复窗口以及推理、恢复与后续执行所需时间；耦合的内层循环联合优化干预时机、恢复技能与交接条件；并以任务进展、失败复现、最终结果与资源消耗等下游信号而非局部修正来评估恢复。

Result: 在RoboDojo-Sim上成功率达50.57%、任务分57.26，分别超过最强基线11.22个百分点与10.68分；端到端回合延迟降低80.4%，在线token消耗降低85.1%（相对GPT-6 Astra智能体）。

Conclusion: 将智能体的临时解决方案转化为可复用的协作能力，与冻结策略形成互补；通过时序感知的控制权分配与下游反馈驱动的优化，在提升任务表现的同时显著降低时间与计算开销。

Abstract: Robot foundation policies provide learned execution capabilities, while programming agents can reason about failures and construct recovery behaviors. Combining them effectively requires learning when intervention helps and when the policy can reliably resume. This challenge is inherently temporal: the physical world continues to evolve during reasoning, and a useful recovery may become infeasible before a decision takes effect. We introduce Agent on Guard (AoG), a hierarchical framework that improves collaboration between a frozen base policy and a programming agent through physical experience. An evolving outer planner allocates control as the physical state evolves, aligning decisions with the physical clock with attention to observation freshness, remaining recovery windows, and the time required for reasoning, recovery, and subsequent execution. Coupled inner loops jointly refine intervention timing, recovery skills, and handoff conditions. Rather than judging recovery by local correction alone, AoG uses downstream task progress, failure recurrence, final outcomes, and resource consumption to improve both recovery behaviors and scheduling. This turns temporary agent solutions into reusable collaboration capabilities that complement the frozen policy. On RoboDojo-Sim, AoG achieves a success rate of 50.57% and a task score of 57.26, exceeding the strongest evaluated baseline by 11.22 percentage points and 10.68 points, respectively. It also reduces end-to-end episode latency by 80.4% and online token consumption by 85.1% relative to the GPT-6 Astra agent.

</details>


### [95] [RoboThink: Let the Agent Think, Let the Policy Act](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30111)
*Yunbei Zhang,Zijian Jin,Janet Wang,Xingchen Zhao,Zhenyu Zhang,Weirui Ye,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboThink 让智能体负责"思考"、策略负责"执行"：通过指令通道与反馈通道把智能体的推理注入机器人策略，从而在漂移、误听、任务变更等场景下大幅提升成功率并降低推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略实时执行但缺乏"为什么失败"的推理能力，而旁观机器人运行的推理智能体常能诊断问题。现有方案要么让智能体直接接管控制（每回合耗时数分钟），要么仅向策略发送指令；但策略只在全新开始时才遵循新指令，半途状态超出训练分布，且指令无法修正运动方式（如持续向左漂移时，"稍微向右推"并非策略学过的任务）。

Method: 提出 RoboThink，智能体思考、策略学习按智能体引导行动。设两条通道：(1) 指令通道，告诉策略做什么——用户中途说"是牛奶不是黄油"时，智能体重写指令，机器人无需训练即可回撤自身动作；(2) 反馈通道，告诉策略怎么动——仅从智能体监督中学习，采用 agent-labelled DAgger、角色 token 与反事实反馈训练，且只被策略的动作模块读取。

Result: LIBERO 上（Claude Opus 5.5）：未知恒定漂移下成功率 0.09→0.87，随机漂移下 0.42→0.84；误听与变更请求完成率 0.91–1.00。效率上，独立行动的 GPT-6 Astra 每成功需 2.1 分钟、每回合超百万 token，而 RoboThink 为 55 秒、8k token。配方可跨策略（VLA 与世界动作模型）、跨机器人（单臂、双臂、移动操作）迁移。RoboTwin 2.0 全部 50 个双臂任务上，恒定漂移 0.12→0.74，随机漂移 0.31→0.56。智能体可插拔：由一个智能体训练的策略可与其它（含开源权重）智能体配合，且无需重训即可随更强智能体变强。

Conclusion: 将推理与执行解耦——智能体做高层次推理与纠错，策略通过指令与反馈两条通道吸收其引导——可在不牺牲实时性、不重训的前提下显著提升鲁棒性与交互纠错能力，并具备跨策略、跨本体、跨智能体的通用性。

Abstract: Robot policies act in real time but fail without knowing why, whereas a reasoning agent that watches the robot can often tell what went wrong. Existing systems either hand the agent control, which takes minutes per episode, or let it send instructions. Yet a policy follows a new instruction only from a fresh start, since a half-finished task leaves it in states outside its training data. Nor can an instruction fix how the robot moves. If a drift keeps pulling the hand to the left, "push right, slightly" is not a task the policy has learned. We propose RoboThink, in which the agent thinks and the policy learns to act on the agent's guidance. An instruction channel tells the policy what to do. When the user says "the milk, not the butter" after the robot has started, the agent rewrites the instruction and the robot undoes its own motion, with no training. A feedback channel tells it how to move. It is taught from the agent alone with agent-labelled DAgger, role tokens and counterfactual feedback, and is read only by the policy's action module. On LIBERO, RoboThink with Claude Opus 5.5 raises success from 0.09 to 0.87 under an unknown constant drift and from 0.42 to 0.84 under a random one. It also completes misheard and changed requests in 0.91 to 1.00 of the episodes. Acting alone, GPT-6 Astra needs 2.1 min per success and over a million tokens per episode, whereas RoboThink needs 55 s and 8k tokens. The same recipe works across policies, for both vision-language-action models and world action models, and across robots, from one arm to two arms and a mobile manipulator. On all 50 RoboTwin 2.0 tasks with two arms, success rises from 0.12 to 0.74 under the constant drift and from 0.31 to 0.56 under the random one. Finally, the agent is a plug-in. A policy taught by one agent works with other agents, including open-weight ones, and grows stronger with a stronger agent, without retraining.

</details>


### [96] [Value function based Reward shaping for safety in quadrupeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30133)
*Janani S K,Manan Tayal,Shishir N. Y Kolathaya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种受障碍函数启发的安全导向奖励塑形框架，形式简单、易于实现，可通用于不同环境与任务；在 IsaacGym 中的 Unitree Go1 四足机器人仿真实验表明，策略优于现有基线，安全违规从 27% 降至 0.1%，且性能几乎无损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习已从简单控制任务扩展到状态空间庞大的复杂现实任务，但训练时间仍是瓶颈。奖励塑形是常用手段，然而现有方法多依赖价值函数，面临可扩展性问题；因此需要一个不依赖价值函数、简单易实现且具有安全导向的奖励塑形方案。

Method: 受障碍函数（barrier function）思想启发，设计一种新的安全导向奖励塑形框架，通过改造奖励公式嵌入安全约束，强调形式简单、易实现，可适配多种环境和任务；在 IsaacGym 平台上对 Unitree Go1 四足机器人开展仿真实验，并与现有基线对比。

Result: 所提奖励公式使策略表现优于现有基线；安全违规率从 27% 降至 0.1%，同时性能几乎没有下降。

Conclusion: 障碍函数式的奖励塑形是一种低实现成本、可扩展且有效的安全强化学习手段，能在几乎不牺牲任务性能的前提下大幅提升安全性；后续可进一步在更多环境与真实机器人上验证其通用性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has progressed from simple control tasks to complex real-world challenges with large state spaces. While RL excels in these tasks, training time remains a limitation. Reward shaping is a popular solution, but existing methods often rely on value functions, which face scalability issues. This paper presents a novel safety-oriented reward-shaping framework inspired by barrier functions, offering simplicity and ease of implementation across various environments and tasks. To evaluate the effectiveness of the proposed reward formulations, we conduct simulation experiments on the Unitree Go1 quadruped robot in Isaacgym. The policy was better than the existing baselines and reduced safety violations from 27% to 0.1% without almost any compromise on the performance.

</details>


### [97] [LeadVLA: Learning Follower-Aware Route Execution for Robot Leading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30149)
*Tianle Zeng,Hanjing Ye,Bingyi Xia,Yuchen Zhou,Zibo Zhang,Yufei Huang,Yonggen Ling,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LeadVLA 提出一种结构化多模态策略，把机器人带路任务拆解为显式路径参考、行进节奏和残差自适应三部分，并配套构建闭环数据生成基础设施 LeadInfra 与标准基准 LeadBench，在闭环评测中取得最高任务成功率并在无需真机微调下迁移到真实环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人带路需要一边执行路线一边持续适应指定跟随者。现有系统往往通过显式状态、规划器或模块化接口来中介"跟随者理解"与"物理路线执行"；而直接用端到端轨迹预测学习这一耦合，容易把路线执行与跟随者响应纠缠在一起，难以训练与评估。同时该任务缺乏可闭环、可复现的数据与评测基准。

Method: 方法上提出 LeadVLA 结构化多模态策略：保留路线作为显式的 Path 参考，并学习用于推进路线进度的 Pace（节奏）以及用于局部、以跟随者与场景为条件的自适应 Residual（残差）。数据与评测方面构建 LeadInfra——基于 Unreal Engine 的可配置闭环数据生成基础设施，实例化约 400 万样本训练语料；并构建 LeadBench，包含 108 个完全留出的闭环回合。

Result: 在 LeadBench 上，LeadVLA 在受评测的机器人策略中取得最高任务成功率 39.6%，同时保持较强的路线保真度与跟随者响应性，并可迁移到未见过的真实世界环境，且无需在真实世界对策略做微调。

Conclusion: 将带路任务显式分解为路径参考、节奏与残差的结构化策略，比端到端纠缠式学习更有效；配套的闭环数据基础设施与标准化基准为该问题提供了可训练、可测量的基础，仿真训练的策略具备零真机微调的迁移能力。

Abstract: Robot leading requires executing a route while continuously adapting to a designated follower. Existing robot-leading systems often mediate follower understanding and physical route execution through explicit states, planners, or modular interfaces; directly learning this coupling through end-to-end trajectory prediction can instead entangle route execution with follower response. We introduce LeadVLA, a structured multimodal policy that preserves the route as an explicit Path reference while learning a Pace for route progress and a Residual for local follower- and scene-conditioned adaptation. To make this problem trainable and measurable, we develop LeadInfra, a configurable Unreal Engine-based closed-loop data-generation infrastructure from which we instantiate an approximately 4M-sample training corpus, and LeadBench, a standardized benchmark with 108 fully held-out closed-loop episodes. LeadVLA achieves the highest task success (39.6%) among evaluated robot policies on LeadBench while maintaining strong route fidelity and follower responsiveness, and transfers to unseen real-world environments without real-world policy fine-tuning. Project Page: https://louiszengCN.github.io/LeadVLA_PAGE/

</details>


### [98] [Toward Open Room-Scale Scanning with Consumer LiDAR via Depth-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30166)
*Minsuk Jang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文检验能否在手机上用完全可检查的开源流程替代 ARKit 黑箱做房间扫描：Mobile-LIO 仅用手机深度与惯性数据，在 7 段 ScanNet++ iPhone 录像上均完成扫描，与 ARKit 参考偏差 0.2–2.0 m，而按专用扫描仪配置运行的 6 个开源 LiDAR-惯性估计器有 41/42 次运行偏差≥4.46 m。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前消费级 LiDAR 房间扫描依赖 ARKit，其跟踪与地图更新是黑箱：应用只能拿到深度与位姿，无法查看生成过程，扫描出错时无法溯源。作者希望验证一个每个阶段都可检查的开源替代方案是否在设备端可行，使误差能够被诊断。

Method: 提出 Mobile-LIO，一个仅使用手机深度与惯性数据、不使用 ARKit 位姿的深度-惯性里程计流程。在 7 段 ScanNet++ iPhone 录音上与 ARKit 派生参考对比，并与 6 个开源 LiDAR-惯性估计器（按其专用扫描仪配置）比较；用激光扫描网格作为精度基准，另在 iPhone 15 Pro Max 上做 3 段 7–13 s 录像的 15 次回放测试。比较本身并非受控实验。

Result: Mobile-LIO 在全部 7 个场景完成，偏差 0.2–2.0 m；6 个基线在 42 次运行中有 41 次偏差≥4.46 m，其中 2 个还未能通过无噪声适配检查，且 Mobile-LIO 另设速度上限而基线没有。修正手机端代码后，15 次回放中 14 次能跟上传感器。精度方面：3 m 内记录深度与网格的差异在 3 个留出场景上（中位差 1.8–2.3 cm 与 17 cm 的数据）；融合 ARKit 位姿的地图仅达 USIBD 最粗精度等级 LOA10；设备端精度未测。误差溯源显示：在误差最大的场景中，无噪声渲染使误差从 1.97 m 降到 0.04 m，说明记录深度（5–7 m 处误差 20–54 cm）与估计器共同贡献了误差。

Conclusion: 开源深度-惯性流程可在手机上用手机深度完成跟踪，并在短时手机回放中跟上传感器，但由于其开放性，误差可被逐段拆解归因（估计器与深度数据各自贡献）。不过它尚不如基于 ARKit 位姿的地图精确，仅达到最粗的 USIBD 精度等级，且未在真实设备上测量精度。因此“可检查的开源替代”在可行性上成立，在精度上仍待改进。

Abstract: Room scanning with consumer LiDAR today depends on ARKit, whose tracking and map update are a black box: an application receives depth and poses but cannot inspect how they were produced, so an error in a scan cannot be traced to its cause. This paper examines whether an open alternative, one whose every stage can be inspected, is feasible on the device. Mobile-LIO is a depth-inertial odometry pipeline that uses the phone's depth and inertial data and no ARKit poses. On seven ScanNet++ iPhone recordings it completed every scene with 0.2-2.0 m deviation from the ARKit-derived reference, where six open-source LiDAR-inertial estimators run as configured for dedicated scanners had at least 4.46 m in 41 of 42 runs (two of the six also failed a noise-free adapter check, and Mobile-LIO has a speed cap they lack; the comparison is not controlled and does not rone 15 Pro Max, a buildwith corrected phone code kept up with the sensor in 14 of 15 replay runs of three 7-13 s recordings; e estimator before thosecorrections. Laser-scanned meshes give the accuracy. Within 3 m, recorded depth differed from the m on three held-out scenes,maps fused with ARKit poses that the dataset scales and aligns to the mesh la a median 1.8-2.3 cm from 17 cm. Because thepipeline is open, the gap could be examined: in the scene with the largest error, noise-free renderefrom 1.97 m to 0.04 m, sorecorded depth, whose error reached 20-54 cm at 5-7 m, contributed alongside the estimator. An open de tracked on phone depth and kept up with the sensor in short phone replays, but it is not yet as accurate as ARKit-pose maps, whicheach only the coarsestUSIBD accuracy level (LOA10); its accuracy on the device was not measured.

</details>


### [99] [Confidently Lost: Silent Failure and Edge Deployment of Dense Visual SLAM under Photon Starvation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30173)
*Shrikrishna Bhagirath Rajule,Srinath Neelamagam,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 低光下基于学习双视图几何的稠密视觉SLAM会"静默失效"：网络在无结构输入时伪造出高自洽几何，系统误信该统计量，导致95%以上接受对应关系违背真值极线几何；该统计量在关键帧对基线下可无监督区分崩溃与正常，但现有补救（低光权重、阈值重调）只能提高"被跟踪"比例而无法获得准确轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密视觉SLAM（如MASt3R-SLAM）通常在良好光照下评测，低光失效问题被忽视。作者希望揭示：低光失效并非"置信度下降"式的可检测失败，而是网络在缺乏场景结构时"制造置信度"，且同一被高估的统计量被两个子系统朝相反方向使用，造成关键帧饥饿与重定位过度接受的矛盾行为。

Method: 以9级量化噪声帧对与真实暗帧对作为探针，测量网络的跨视图几何自洽度；在暗序列上统计帧消耗、关键帧数、因子图边拒绝情况、帧率与对应关系相对真值极线几何的违规率；分析关键帧与重定位两个模块对同一统计量的相反阈值解读；提出在关键帧对基线下读取该统计量作为无真值、无额外计算（样本内）的崩溃检测信号；对比低光适配权重与两个阈值重调两种补救；并在Jetson AGX Orin + 足式机器人平台上对四条路线回放验证。

Result: 纯噪声帧对得到0.936的自洽度，真实暗帧对为0.935，几乎无差别；暗序列消耗1605帧、仅产出7个关键帧、不拒绝任何因子图边并报告满帧率，而超过95%被接受的对应关系违背真值极线几何；该统计量在关键帧对基线下可分开ETH3D暗序列中的崩溃与正常运行；低光权重与阈值重调均将跟踪轨迹从1/6提升至5/6，但都不准确；在足式机器人平台上，四条路线中最暗的两条复现了该机制特征，且其表现形式随路线几何与传感器编码变化。

Conclusion: 低光下学习式双视图几何SLAM的失败是"自信的错误"而非"可见的不确定"：网络并非失去置信度，而是凭空生成置信度，系统因信任自洽统计量而静默崩溃。仅靠适配权重或调阈值只能改善跟踪存活率而非精度，说明需要针对该机制的检测（如关键帧对基线下的统计量读取）与结构性修复，并注意其在真实平台上的表现依赖路线几何与传感器编码。

Abstract: Dense visual SLAM built on learned two-view geometry is evaluated in good light; in low light it fails silently. Two frames of 9-level quantized noise, no scene at all, give MASt3R-SLAM's network a cross-view geometric self-consistency of 0.936; a real dark frame pair gives 0.935. Starved of structure, the network invents the same geometry for both views and the system trusts the agreement: on a dark sequence it consumes 1,605 frames, emits 7 keyframes, rejects no factor-graph edges and reports full frame rate, while over 95% of the correspondences it accepts violate the ground-truth epipolar geometry. One inflated statistic is read by two subsystems against thresholds in opposite directions, so keyframing starves while relocalization accepts almost every candidate. The same statistic, read at keyframe-pair baselines, separates the confident collapse from working runs on the ETH3D dark sequences without ground truth or extra computation (in-sample). Low-light-adapted weights take tracked trajectories from 1/6 to 5/6, as does retuning two thresholds; neither yields accurate ones, because the adaptation withdraws confidence from wrong geometry rather than finding right geometry. Replayed on a Jetson AGX Orin carried by a legged robot, the mechanism's signature reproduces on the two darkest of four routes, and which of its faces appears varies with route geometry and sensor encoding. The network does not lose confidence in the dark; it manufactures it.

</details>


### [100] [Quantizing in the Loop: Drift-Aware Ultra-Low-Bit Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30178)
*Yanlong Chen,Thongthornpatch Chamadol,Yichen Guo,Yunfei Gao,Mohan Liu,Addison Lin Wang,Yawei Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LoopQ，一种on-policy超低比特量化框架，通过使用量化策略自身rollout的状态进行校准，并加入只优化查找表与缩放因子的readout对齐微调，在2比特下使GR00T N1.7于LIBERO-Plus达到60.4%成功率（全精度61.8%），1比特下π0.5仍完成80.0%任务，并已在Jetson AGX Thor与真实机械臂上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大VLA模型部署到资源受限机器人需要极端压缩，但现有PTQ方法在≤2比特时几乎崩溃。作者归因于两处失配：一是在静态离线示范上校准导致分布偏移，量化扰动会把策略推入未见状态并使未校准误差累积；二是最小化逐层权重重构误差是错误代理，因为动作生成只依赖动作头接收的表征。

Method: 1）on-policy校准：用量化策略自身rollout产生的状态做校准，闭合分布偏移的环；2）readout对齐微调：冻结trellis码，仅优化轻量查找表与缩放因子，使骨干readout对齐全精度教师；3）全部线性层采用2比特（极端下探至1比特权重），配合8比特激活以在整数内核上运行。

Result: LIBERO-Plus上GR00T N1.7全线性层2比特达60.4%成功率，接近全精度61.8%；每权重1比特时π0.5仍完成80.0%的LIBERO任务；8比特激活下2比特π0.5可在嵌入式NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor的整数内核上运行，权重缩小4.1倍、内存减少44%；真实AgileX Piper机械臂上三个任务均在距全精度一个成功率以内。

Conclusion: 超低比特VLA量化的关键在于对齐策略实际访问的状态分布与动作头所依赖的readout表征，而非层权重重构误差；LoopQ以on-policy校准加readout对齐微调在1–2比特下保持了接近全精度的操作性能，并具备边缘设备与真机可行性。

Abstract: Deploying large vision-language-action (VLA) models on resource-constrained robots demands extreme compression, yet existing post-training quantization (PTQ) methods frequently collapse at ultra-low bit-widths ($\le$ 2 bits). We trace this failure to two mismatches in standard pipelines. First, calibrating on static offline demonstrations induces distribution shift: minor quantization perturbations drive the policy into unseen states where uncalibrated errors compound. Second, minimizing the layer-wise weight reconstruction error is a flawed proxy, as action generation depends only on the representations consumed by the action head. We present LoopQ, an on-policy ultra-low-bit quantization framework. LoopQ closes the loop by calibrating on states from the quantized policy's own rollouts. It then adds readout-aligned fine-tuning, which freezes the trellis codes and optimizes only lightweight lookup tables and scaling factors to align the backbone readout with the full-precision teacher. With all linear layers at 2 bits, LoopQ achieves a 60.4% success rate on LIBERO-Plus with GR00T N1.7, close to the 61.8% of full precision. Even at 1 bit per weight, $π_{0.5}$ still completes 80.0% of LIBERO tasks. With 8-bit activations, the 2-bit $π_{0.5}$ runs on integer kernels on an embedded NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor, with 4.1$\times$ smaller weights and 44% less memory. On a real AgileX Piper arm, it stays within one success of full precision on each of three tasks. Code and checkpoints are available at https://github.com/ForeverBlue816/LoopQ

</details>


### [101] [Trustworthy agentic lunar robotics: safety-constrained contingent planning with a foundation-model planner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30179)
*Bingqi Huang,Bingchuan Wei,Yingkai Cai,Zhaokui Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出非对称知识级重规划（AKR），把规划器视为不可信黑箱：可逆动作按规划器假设执行，不可逆动作须先由最小代价感知覆盖验证全部前置条件，从而在不可预测的月面运维中兼顾自主性与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月面基地长期运行需要机器人在部署前未预见的情形下修复设备、运送货物。已飞行的空间自主软件只执行预先验证过的流程，超出范围即进入安全保持；而基础模型虽能现场生成计划，却无法在执行前验证。因此需要一个既能现场自主规划、又能在不可逆动作上保证安全的形式化框架。

Method: 将运维任务形式化为带安全约束的偶然规划问题，包含不可逆动作、错误记录、三值物理证据以及代价高且有截止时间的感知。提出AKR：把规划器当作不可信黑箱，可逆动作依据规划器的假设直接执行，不可逆动作只有在最小代价感知覆盖验证了全部前置条件之后才执行。

Result: 在测量可靠的前提下：(1) 对任意规划器与记录，不会有不可逆动作在虚假前置条件下执行；(2) 任何可靠的基于知识的策略，其不可逆动作前的感知代价存在下界；(3) 配完整规划器时执行终将到达已验证目标或经认证的保持状态。实验中AKR在21,000次蒙特卡洛试验与58次全栈运行中均未执行危险动作，而FF-Replan行为规则在7%的试验中执行了危险动作；AKR运行中位耗时为同任务最快运行的1.10倍，K-replanner为1.50倍；AKR在21次应急与序列故障运行中完成20次且任务模型不变，原PLEXIL与EUROPA→PLEXIL流程则一次也未完成。

Conclusion: AKR通过"可逆放开、不可逆严验"的非对称设计，在无需信任规划器的前提下同时获得可证明的安全保证与接近最优的执行效率，并在应急与序列故障场景中显著优于传统流程化自主方案，为月面基地的自主运维提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Sustained lunar-base operations require robots that restore equipment and deliver cargo under conditions unforeseen before deployment. Flown space autonomy executes pre-validated procedures and enters a safe hold outside their scope, whereas foundation models synthesize plans on site that cannot be verified before execution. We formulate these operations as safety-constrained contingent planning with irreversible actions, erroneous records, three-valued physical evidence, and costly, deadline-constrained sensing. We propose asymmetric knowledge-level replanning (AKR), which treats the planner as an untrusted black box: reversible actions run on the planner's assumptions, and irreversible actions run only after a minimum-cost sensing cover verifies every precondition. Under sound measurements, no irreversible action executes on a false precondition for any planner or record, the sensing cost before an irreversible action is bounded below for every sound knowledge-based policy, and execution with a complete planner ends in a verified goal or a certified hold. AKR executed no hazardous action in 21,000 Monte Carlo trials with sound observations or in 58 full-stack runs; the FF-Replan acting rule executed one in 7% of those trials. The median AKR run took 1.10 times the fastest run on the same mission, against 1.50 under the K-replanner acting rule. AKR completed 20 of 21 contingency and sequential-fault runs with an unchanged task model; the original PLEXIL and EUROPA$\rightarrow$PLEXIL procedures completed none.

</details>
