We introduce HIVE-Bench: one shared flow-matching policy reads 20 encoders from seven families on 24 egocentric bimanual tasks, across adaptation, task sharing, and sweeps of data, model size, and layer. Keeping the patches matters. Averaging each view to one token removes most of the success, adaptation helps every pair we test, and one shared policy keeps most of the success of separate policies at a fraction of the updates. The same tokens are then scored with statistics, state and object readouts, published vision scores, and an inverse-dynamics readout. That readout of the commanded motion is one of the strongest measurements available before a policy is trained. Recognition, depth, and correspondence do not transfer reliably. Fine-tuning can improve the action readout even when other measurements move the other way, and no statistic is stable enough to replace a rollout before any deployment.

</details>


### [136] [Bridging Continuous-Time MAPF and Industrial AGV Fleet Coordination with Lazy Priority-Based Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30874)
*Federico Signorile,Raffaele Carli,Alessandro Bertogli,Roberto Olmi,Sven Koenig,Mariagrazia Dotoli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将工业AGV场景下的连续时间路网多智能体路径规划形式化为AGV-MAPF_R-CTC，修正CPBS的低层SIPP边界条件，并通过分析优先级搜索空间结构提出Lazy-CPBS，延迟生成高层的子节点规划以削减低层搜索开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间路网MAPF（MAPF_R）能实现带几何避碰的机器人车队协调，但在工业AGV基础设施上部署困难：高连通、冗余的路网会放大安全区间推理与多智能体交互处理的代价，需要弥合这一差距。

Method: 首先形式化AGV-MAPF_R-CTC——一种带预计算连续时间冲突的配置空间变体，使现有MAPF_R求解器几乎无需改动即可复用于AGV车队协调；随后重新审视CPBS，明确其低层SIPP在优先级诱导约束下所需的边界条件并实现更早的解检测；核心是分析优先级搜索空间结构，厘清每次优先级细化影响哪些智能体、何时可跳过重规划，据此提出Lazy-CPBS：高层节点惰性实体化，仅在被扩展时计算子节点规划，未扩展的兄弟节点不产生低层搜索开销。

Result: 在标准MAPF基准和从专有数字孪生仿真器导入的工业AGV路网上实验表明：基于CPBS的求解器可有效扩展到远超最优MAPF_R求解器所能处理的智能体规模；Lazy-CPBS显著降低CPBS开销，并在多种工业布局上给出最一致的鲁棒性—运行时间折中。

Conclusion: 将连续时间MAPF_R与工业AGV部署需求对齐的CTC形式化，加上CPBS低层边界条件的修正与惰性高层的Lazy-CPBS，使优先级类求解器在大规模高冗余路网中兼具可扩展性与稳定的工程表现。

Abstract: Continuous-time multi-agent path finding on roadmaps (MAPF$_R$) offers a principled way to coordinate robot fleets with geometric collision avoidance, yet its deployment on industrial automated guided vehicle (AGV) infrastructures remains challenging, as highly connected and redundant roadmaps amplify both safe-interval reasoning and multi-agent interaction handling. To bridge this gap, we formalize AGV-MAPF$_R$-CTC, a configuration-space variant of MAPF$_R$ with precomputed continuous-time conflicts that enables the reuse of MAPF$_R$ solvers for AGV fleet coordination with minimal changes. We then revisit continuous-time priority-based search (CPBS), identifying the boundary conditions required by its safe interval path planning (SIPP) low-level planner under priority-induced constraints and enabling earlier solution detection. As a central contribution, we analyze the structure of the priority search space, clarifying which agents are affected by each priority refinement and when replanning can be avoided. Building on this analysis, we introduce a novel algorithm, Lazy-CPBS, which materializes high-level nodes lazily: a child's plans are computed only when it is expanded, so siblings that are never expanded cost no low-level search. Experiments on standard MAPF benchmarks and on industrial AGV roadmaps imported from a proprietary digital-twin simulator show that CPBS-based solvers remain effective well beyond the agent counts reachable by optimal MAPF$_R$ solvers, while Lazy-CPBS substantially reduces CPBS overhead and offers the most consistent robustness/runtime trade-off across industrial layouts.

</details>


### [137] [Tabular Foundation Models Are Surprisingly Effective In-Context Robot Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30889)
*Zhen Xu,Hao-Run Cai,Heng-Zhuang Li,Si-Yang Liu,Jun-Peng Jiang,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 TabAct，将视觉模仿学习转化为表格数值预测问题：以冻结的视觉特征与本体感知为输入、未来动作块为目标，用每条演示的时间步作为上下文中的标注样本，条件化冻结的表格基础模型（TFM）实现闭环控制，无需机器人专用训练或目标任务微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索为通用数值预测预训练的基础模型能否充当上下文机器人学习器，即能否在无需机器人数据训练的前提下直接通过上下文示例推断视觉到动作的映射，从而验证通用数值预训练作为机器人学习基础的可行性。

Method: 提出 TabAct：把视觉模仿学习表述为数值预测——输入为冻结视觉特征与机器人本体感知，目标为未来动作块，每个演示时间步提供一个标注样本，用于条件化一个冻结的 TFM；预测器不进行任何机器人特定训练或目标任务微调，仅依赖目标任务的少量演示进行上下文条件化并执行闭环控制。实验在 LIBERO 与 Meta-World 上进行，采用 TabICLv2 作为 TFM 实例，并与多个基线比较。

Result: 在每种方法均可获得 1 条目标任务演示的设置下，TabAct 搭配 TabICLv2 在所有评估方法中取得最高平均成功率：在 LIBERO 上超过最强基线 20.9 个百分点，在 Meta-World 上超过 5.8 个百分点。增加演示数量可在不更新模型权重的情况下提升控制性能：使用 5 条演示时，LIBERO-Object 上成功率达到 94.2%。

Conclusion: 通用数值预训练可以为上下文机器人学习提供有效基础；冻结的表格基础模型配合少量演示即可实现较强且可随演示数量扩展的闭环视觉模仿控制，为无需机器人专用训练的策略学习提供了新路径。

Abstract: Can foundation models pretrained for generic numerical prediction serve as in-context robot learners? We investigate this question through tabular foundation models (TFMs), which are pretrained to infer input-output relationships from labeled examples in context. We introduce TabAct, which casts visual imitation as numerical prediction: frozen visual features and robot proprioception form the inputs, future action chunks form the targets, and each demonstration time step provides one labeled example that conditions a frozen TFM for closed-loop control. The predictor receives no robot-specific training or target-task fine-tuning. With one target-task demonstration available to each method, TabAct with TabICLv2 achieves the highest average success among all evaluated methods on both LIBERO and Meta-World, exceeding the strongest baseline by 20.9 and 5.8 percentage points, respectively. Additional demonstrations improve control without updating model weights: with five demonstrations, success reaches 94.2% on LIBERO-Object. These results demonstrate that generic numerical pretraining can provide an effective foundation for in-context robot learning.

</details>


### [138] [DexLatentWorld: 3D Cross-Embodiment Latent Action World Models for Zero-Shot Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30907)
*Vinay Udit Mohan,Xuchuan Huang,Rolandos Alexandros Potamias*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种3D潜在动作世界模型，仅从点云观测的"重定向人类操作演示"中学习连续动作表征，形成跨形态统一的潜在动作空间，直接作为模型预测控制的搜索空间，实现无需具身特定动作解码器的零样本机器人操作迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型依赖代价高昂、难以规模化的动作标注交互数据，且通常与训练时的特定具身绑定，难以迁移到未见过的机器人形态。

Method: 1）从重定向的人类操作演示的点云观测中直接学习连续潜在动作表示；2）使用对比目标促使不同具身间的运动表征保持一致；3）统一的潜在动作空间直接作为MPC搜索空间，采样的潜在动作条件化粒子式未来预测；4）从预测的几何运动反解可执行机器人指令，无需具身专属动作解码器；5）采用任务无关的自适应（task-agnostic adaptation）。

Result: 学到的潜在动作具备可控性与组合性，可迁移到未见具身，支持新夹爪几何的零样本操作。任务无关自适应后在reaching/grasping/placing上成功率分别达90%、92%、60%（DexWM为72%、58%、28%）；真实世界中对未见灵巧手在5类推、抓、举任务共50次试验中零样本成功率86%。

Conclusion: 以几何为锚的潜在动作可作为世界模型驱动机器人操作的一种统一、可迁移接口，降低对动作标注数据和具身专属设计的依赖。

Abstract: World models facilitate planning and control in robotic manipulation by enabling agents to anticipate the consequences of their actions. However, they typically rely on action-labelled interaction data that is costly to acquire and difficult to scale. These models are also often tied to the specific embodiment they were trained on, limiting transferability to previously unseen robot morphologies. We introduce a 3D latent-action world model that learns continuous action representations directly from point-cloud observations of retargeted human manipulation demonstrations. A contrastive objective is used to encourage consistent representations of motion across embodiments. The resulting unified latent action space serves directly as the search space for model-predictive control, where sampled latent actions condition particle-based future predictions. Executable robot commands are then recovered from the predicted geometric motion without requiring an embodiment-specific action decoder. Through extensive evaluations, we demonstrate that the learned latent actions exhibit controllable and compositional motion, transfer to unseen robot embodiments, and support zero-shot manipulation with unseen gripper geometries. Following task-agnostic adaptation, our model achieves 90%, 92%, and 60% success on reaching, grasping, and placing, respectively, compared with DexWM's reported 72%, 58%, and 28%. Finally, we demonstrate zero-shot real-world manipulation with an unseen dexterous hand, achieving an 86% success rate across 50 trials on five diverse tasks involving pushing, grasping, and lifting everyday objects. These results highlight the potential of geometry-grounded latent actions as a unified, transferable interface for world-model-based robotic manipulation. Project materials, including demonstration videos and source code, are available at https://vinaymohan0409.github.io/DexLatentWorld/.

</details>


### [139] [CoSel: Cooperation as Selection for End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30918)
*Yiping Li,Haohua Que,Zhipeng Bao,Wenjie Zhao,Qianwen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CoSel框架，将协同驾驶中的"合作"从学习型规划器中剥离，改为基于协同预测的候选轨迹选择机制：规划器保持仅用自车输入不变，由单独训练的协同预测器预测周围智能体未来运动，据此评估并必要时替换规划器候选轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同端到端方法把共享信息注入规划器，并依赖协同驾驶示范数据来学习如何利用这些信息；但"共享信息应改变决策"的时刻在数据中极为稀少，导致监督信号不足。作者认为合作行为不必由规划器学习。

Method: CoSel：1）保持自车规划器及其仅自车输入不变；2）单独训练协同预测器，融合自车、路侧单元与网联自动驾驶车辆的观测，预测周围智能体及未来运动；3）用这些预测评估规划器的候选轨迹，若原选择仍安全则保留，若共享信息揭示额外风险则选择最接近原选择的安全备选轨迹；4）每次安全干预均可追溯到触发它的共享证据。

Result: 闭环评测中，V2Xverse上驾驶得分70.7，比最佳对比协同方法高7.5分；MDrive上平均驾驶得分85.8为最高。在自车检测缺失情况下，72个场景中CoSel碰撞数仅由17增至19，而现有协同端到端模型由19增至32。

Conclusion: 合作可通过"预测+选择"实现而无需改动规划器本身，该方法性能优于协同端到端基线、对自车感知缺失更鲁棒，且决策可追溯。

Abstract: Cooperative driving can extend a vehicle's awareness beyond its own sensors, but existing cooperative end-to-end methods inject shared information into the planner and rely on cooperative driving demonstrations to learn how to use it. The moments in which shared information should change a driving decision are rare in such data, providing limited supervision for learning this behavior. We argue that cooperation need not be learned by the planner. Instead, reacting to shared information requires predicting how surrounding agents will move and selecting a response from the candidate trajectories of an ego planner. We introduce CoSel, a cooperation-as-selection framework that leaves the planner and its ego-only input unchanged. A separately trained cooperative predictor uses observations from the ego vehicle, roadside units, and connected automated vehicles to predict surrounding agents and their future motion. CoSel evaluates the planner's candidate trajectories against these predictions, preserves the planner's choice when it remains safe, and selects the closest safe alternative when shared information reveals additional risk. Each safety intervention is traceable to the shared evidence that triggers it. In closed-loop evaluation, CoSel achieves a driving score of 70.7 on V2Xverse, outperforming the best compared cooperative method by 7.5 points, and the highest average driving score of 85.8 on MDrive. Under missing ego detections, the collision count of CoSel increases only from 17 to 19 in 72 scenarios, compared with 19 to 32 for an existing cooperative end-to-end model.

</details>


### [140] [Personalization of a Commercial Powered Knee Prosthesis Under Clinically Realistic Conditions via Reinforcement Learning: A Feasibility Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30928)
*Xinyi Liu,Atli Örn Sverrisson,David Langlois,Ming Liu,Varun Nalam,He Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文验证了基于策略迭代与约束嵌入（PICE）的强化学习调参方法，能在接近临床现实的条件下（5米走道、旁路适配器）对商用动力膝关节假肢进行个性化，仅需8–18个调参步（步行1.7–4.2分钟）即可将膝关节运动学特征误差平均降低64%–90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力假肢控制需要针对每个使用者个性化，但现有方法多停留在实验室理想条件，缺乏在真实临床约束（空间、设备、时间）下的可行性证据。作者希望缩短个性化时间并降低对场地设备的依赖。

Method: 采用带约束嵌入的策略迭代强化学习调参方法（PICE）。先用一名非残障受试者在跑步机上离线学习一个初始策略，再将其迁移用于另外三名非残障受试者，佩戴旁路适配器的动力膝关节在5米走道上行走，在线进行个性化调参，并以膝关节运动学特征误差为优化目标。

Result: 所有受试者均在8–18个调参步内达到目标，对应步行时间仅1.7–4.2分钟；膝关节运动学特征误差平均降低64%–90%。

Conclusion: 结果支持该强化学习调参方法在受空间与设备限制的临床现实条件下落地实施，具备可行性；但本研究为小样本、非残障受试者的可行性研究，后续需在真实截肢使用者中进一步验证。

Abstract: Personalization of powered prosthesis control to individual user needs remains a significant challenge. To address this challenge, policy iteration with constraint embedded (PICE), a reinforcement learning (RL) tuning approach, has been developed and validated. This feasibility study investigated whether the developed approach can personalize a commercial powered knee prosthesis under clinically realistic conditions. To reduce the time for personalization, tuning was initialized with a policy learned offline from one non-disabled participant walking on a treadmill and was then applied to three other non-disabled participants walking with the knee through a bypass adapter on a 5-meter walkway. All participants reached the goal within 8-18 tuning steps (1.7-4.2 minutes of walking), reducing the knee-kinematic feature error by 64-90% on average. These results support the feasibility of implementing the RL-based tuning approach in clinically realistic conditions with space and equipment constraints.

</details>


### [141] [SayDo: Benchmarking the Safety of Frontier Models from Chat to Closed-Loop Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30957)
*Benjamin Alt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出SayDo基准，发现前沿视觉语言模型在具身控制场景中的危险行为率远高于纯文本问答（14%→47%，最受影响模型2%→80%），文本安全评估无法预测其作为机器人控制器时的安全表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿视觉语言模型越来越多地被用作高层机器人控制器，但其安全性几乎只在文本环境中被评估，缺乏对具身情境下危害倾向的系统测评手段。

Method: 构建SayDo基准：包含11项实验室与家庭任务，每项由良性指令搭配场景中的潜在危险；在沿具身程度梯度的6种设置中实现，从关于想象机器人的聊天问答一直到模拟机械臂的闭环控制；每项任务配套良性对照、安全替代变体与脚本化的操作者反驳；在6个开源与闭源权重VLM上共运行11,480个回合。

Result: 危险动作率从聊天场景的14%升至模拟器中的47%，受影响最大的模型从2%升至80%；该上升主要由开源权重模型驱动。模型在73%的不安全回合中在行动前已识别出危险，且在控制机器人时比聊天时约两倍更易在操作者反驳下让步；最安全的模型在良性对照上表现出过度谨慎。

Conclusion: 在纯文本环境中进行的安全评估不能预测同一模型作为机器人控制器时的安全行为，具身情境下的安全评测不可或缺。

Abstract: Frontier vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used as high-level robot controllers, yet their safety is evaluated almost entirely in text. We introduce SayDo, a benchmark that assesses the propensity of frontier models to cause harm in embodied contexts. SayDo comprises eleven laboratory and household tasks that pair a benign instruction with a hazard latent in the scene. Tasks are implemented in six settings along an embodiment gradient, from chat questions about an imagined robot to closed-loop control of a simulated robot arm. Benign controls, safe-alternative variants and scripted operator pushback complement each task. Across six open- and closed-weight VLMs and 11,480 episodes, hazardous-action rates rise from 14% in chat to 47% in the simulator, and from 2% to 80% for the most affected model. The increase is largely driven by open-weight models. We find that models flag hazards before acting in 73% of unsafe episodes and give way to pushback about twice as often when controlling robots as in chat. The safest models also show signs of overcaution on benign controls. Safety evaluations run in text therefore do not predict the safety behavior of the same models when used as robot controllers.

</details>


### [142] [Foresight Forcing: Learning Spatial Foresight for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30963)
*Mishuo Han,Zhixuan Liang,Zhaoyuan Wan,Wendi Chen,Jiarui Chen,Xinkai Wang,Yue Ma,Yu Zhou,Junting Chen,Yao Mu,Jun Lv,Chuan Wen,Cewu Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Foresight Forcing（F²），一种从预训练3D特征空间的变化中学习空间转移表示的世界动作模型，通过将稠密几何转移监督蒸馏进空间转移查询，实现无需未来观测的几何感知动作生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）虽能预测未来视觉观测以改进动作预测，但其训练目标通常使底层的3D几何转移保持隐式，缺乏显式的几何监督。

Method: 训练时用冻结的3D基础模型对对齐的当前与未来观测提取逐patch特征差，提供稠密的几何感知特征转移监督；将这些目标蒸馏到空间转移查询中，查询由当前观测、语言指令和本体状态逐步精化，并与当前视觉特征共同生成动作。部署时无需未来观测和3D教师模型。

Result: 在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus 和 RoboTwin2.0 上表现强劲，RoboTwin2.0 Full 平均成功率达 95.01%；在六个真实世界操作任务上平均任务得分 85.8%，鲁棒性提升。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模3D空间转移并蒸馏为查询，F² 在不增加推理开销的前提下提升了操作策略的预测精度与鲁棒性，验证了几何转移监督的有效性。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation policies can improve action prediction by anticipating how objects move and how their spatial relationships change. World Action Models (WAMs) learn to predict future visual observations, but their training objectives typically leave the underlying 3D geometric transitions implicit. To alleviate this problem, we introduce Foresight Forcing ($\mathcal{F}^{2}$), a WAM that learns a spatial transition representation from changes in a pretrained 3D feature space. During training, a frozen 3D foundation model extracts patch-wise feature differences from aligned current and future observations that provide dense supervision for geometry-aware feature transitions. We distill these targets into spatial transition queries, which are progressively refined from the current observation, language instruction, and proprioceptive state, and use them alongside current visual features to generate actions. At deployment, the policy predicts the queries without future observations or the 3D teacher. Experiments on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and RoboTwin2.0 demonstrate strong manipulation performance and improved robustness, including 95.01% average success on RoboTwin2.0 Full. On six real-world manipulation tasks, $\mathcal{F}^{2}$ achieves a mean task score of 85.8%.

</details>


### [143] [WorldLeague: Benchmarking Physical Agency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30989)
*Xinyang Zhou,Qi Li,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 WorldLeague 基准，用 60 个模拟机器人任务、统一物理目标度量来横向比较 VLM、VLA 与 WAM 等 13 个 AI 系统的自主物理能力，并提出 Interactive Score 与 Physical Agency Profile 作为评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 系统已能操作 Blender、玩复杂视频游戏、用机械臂操作物体，但缺乏统一标准回答"今天的 AI 系统能自主达成哪些物理目标"。评估需要覆盖感知、记忆、规划、行动的不同组合，而不同系统（VLM/VLA/WAM）控制接口不同，难以直接比较。

Method: 构建 WorldLeague 基准：包含 WorldSeek（探索场景收集证据）、WorldShape（重排物体）、WorldShift（适应环境变化）三类共 60 个模拟机器人任务；从机器人轨迹度量朝物理目标的进展。VLM 负责选择目标与动作，VLA 与 WAM 使用原生控制接口。围绕六个能力维度，同时测量进展程度（Interactive Score）与达成频率（物理成功率），合成 Physical Agency Profile。

Result: 在 13 个代表性 AI 系统中，Claude Fable 5.2 完成 60 个目标中的 13 个，取得最高平均 Interactive Score（28.58/100），并在四个维度领先；GPT-6.1-Sol 与 GPT-6-Astra 在规则遵循任务上并列第一。值得注意的是，即便 VLM 正确回答了所有最终问题，在 71.8% 的此类回合中其 Interactive Score 仍为零。

Conclusion: WorldLeague 为 Embodied AI、Physical AI 与 Agentic AI 提供了共享的比较基础，可从"部分进展"到"完全达成"评估 AI 系统的自主物理能力；结果也表明"答对问题"不等于"完成物理目标"，现有系统在物理自主性上仍有很大差距。

Abstract: AI systems can now operate Blender, follow instructions in complex video games, and manipulate objects with robot arms. What range of physical goals can today's AI systems achieve autonomously? Answering this question requires testing systems on tasks that demand different combinations of perception, memory, planning, and action. We introduce \textbf{WorldLeague}, a benchmark that compares AI systems on a common set of \textbf{60 simulated robot tasks} by measuring progress toward each task's physical goal from the robot's trajectory. Robots explore scenes to gather evidence in \textbf{WorldSeek}, rearrange objects in \textbf{WorldShape}, and adapt to changes in \textbf{WorldShift}. Tying these goals to a single control interface limits which AI systems can be compared. In WorldLeague, vision-language models (VLMs) choose targets and motions, while vision-language-action models (VLAs) and world-action models (WAMs) use their native controls. For each of six capability dimensions, we measure how much progress a system makes toward its goals (Interactive Score) and how often it achieves them (physical success rate). Together, these measures form the system's \emph{Physical Agency Profile}. Among 13 representative AI systems, Claude Fable 5.2 achieves 13 of the 60 goals and earns the highest mean Interactive Score (28.58/100). Fable leads in four dimensions, while GPT-6.1-Sol and GPT-6-Astra tie for first on rule-following tasks. Even when VLMs answer every final question correctly, they receive an Interactive Score of zero in 71.8\% of those episodes. WorldLeague gives Embodied AI, Physical AI, and Agentic AI a shared basis for comparing what AI systems achieve autonomously, from partial progress toward a physical goal to full goal completion. Project Webpage: https://xyangzhou.github.io/WorldLeague

</details>


### [144] [Generalizing Agentic Robotics via Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30997)
*Xinxin Liu,Anh Dao,Wenrui Bao,Rafid Ahmed,Bingxin Xu,Ming Li,Chen Chen,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 Robo-MOPD，用多教师在线策略蒸馏把多个领域专家规划器整合为单一通用规划器，在 LIBERO-PRO 和 RoboCasa365 上平均成功率 58.9%，优于权重合并、离线专家 SFT 和多领域在线 RL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 agentic robotics 的规划器要么依赖 API 访问的闭源模型（成本高、延迟大），要么针对单一领域用 RL 后训练，导致每个领域都需单独的专家且工具不兼容，缺乏构建通用规划器的可行路径。多教师在线策略蒸馏（MOPD）在 LLM 后训练中已被用于整合领域专家，但其能否用于机器人智能体规划器尚不清楚。

Method: 提出 Robo-MOPD：在仿真中以闭环 rollout 训练单个学生规划器，并由对应的冻结领域专家提供 token 级监督；与权重合并、离线专家监督微调（SFT）、多领域在线 RL 三种整合方法进行系统对比。

Result: 单个 9B Robo-MOPD 规划器在 LIBERO-PRO 上达 72.9%、RoboCasa365 上达 44.8% 成功率（平均 58.9%），在平均成功率上超过所有整合基线，并在各自基准上超过两个 9B 专家。在长程任务上优势明显，LIBERO-10 上比多领域在线 RL 高 13.0 个百分点。

Conclusion: MOPD 可有效构建 agentic robotics 的通用规划器，单模型即可取代多个领域专家，并在长程任务上尤其受益，为通用机器人规划器提供了一种低成本、可扩展的整合方案。

Abstract: Building general-purpose robots is a central goal in robotics. Agentic robotics pursues this goal with planners that control robots through tool calls. However, existing methods either rely on proprietary models accessed through APIs, which incur high costs and latency, or post-train a planner with reinforcement learning (RL) in a single domain, requiring a separate specialist for each domain with incompatible tools. In large language model (LLM) post-training, multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) has been used to consolidate domain specialists into a single generalist. Yet whether MOPD can build a generalist planner for agentic robotics, how well it performs, and how it compares with other consolidation methods remain unexplored. In this paper, we systematically study these questions: we introduce Robo-MOPD, which trains a single student on closed-loop rollouts in simulation under token-level supervision from matching frozen specialists, and compare it with weight merging, off-policy specialist supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and multi-domain online RL. Experiments on LIBERO-PRO and RoboCasa365 show that a single 9B Robo-MOPD planner reaches 72.9% and 44.8% success (58.9% average), surpassing all consolidated baselines in average success and both 9B specialists on their respective benchmarks. Robo-MOPD excels on long-horizon tasks, outperforming multi-domain online RL by 13.0 percentage points on LIBERO-10.

</details>


### [145] [RoboUniverse-10K: Toward the Scaling Era of Agentic Robotics via Real-to-Sim Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31002)
*Jinkun Liu,Yingze Wang,Situ Wang,Deyao Hong,Mingju Gao,Wenyi Li,Yizhe Chi,Youjie Zheng,Kaisen Yang,Xiangyang Wang,Bingxiang He,Yuxin Zuo,Boyuan Chen,Yue Chen,Cheng Qian,Tianxing Chen,Huichi Zhou,Huaxiu Yao,Hao Tang,Hao Zhou,Ya-Qin Zhang,Calvin Xiao,Tiga Altman,Huan-ang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出RoboUniverse-10K，一个由真实世界视频经自动化Real2Sim与质量校验流水线构建的10,807个可交互、可重置、可验证的物理仿真环境，并从中留出500个未见任务构成专家校验的零样本基准RoboUniverse-Verified；同时用约17K多轮轨迹（0.79B tokens）对Qwen3.6-35B-A3B做全参数SFT得到RoboUniverse-35B-A3B，显著缩小开源与闭源模型的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic Robotics 范式让基础模型以编码智能体方式通过可执行程序控制机器人，但相比软件工程拥有海量可执行环境支撑后训练，机器人领域受限于规模小、手工构建的基准以及分布内评估，缺乏大规模、可验证、可重置的训练与评测环境。

Method: 1）通过自动化 Real2Sim 与质量保证流水线，从真实视频构建 10,807 个交互式、可重置、可验证的物理环境，每个环境运行于隔离且 Harbor 兼容的 harness 中，隐藏特权状态与评分逻辑以贴近真实感知；2）从同一流水线留出 500 个未见任务，构建专家验证的零样本基准 RoboUniverse-Verified；3）评测 11 个闭源与开源模型；4）用 GPT-6 Astra 采集约 17K 多轮轨迹（0.79B tokens），对 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 进行全参数监督微调，得到 RoboUniverse-35B-A3B。

Result: 评测显示闭源前沿模型成功率达 85.8%，而开源基线仅 2.6%–10.0%，瓶颈集中在低级抓取、可变形物体操作与接触后物理控制。微调后的 RoboUniverse-35B-A3B 在 RoboUniverse-Verified 上零样本成功率从 0.8% 提升至 21.2%，并在 LIBERO-MAX、RoboCasa、RoboTwin 2.0 及真实 Franka 机械臂上实现零样本任务完成。

Conclusion: 该工作开源了可直接用于强化学习的环境与模型，为 Agentic Robotics 的大规模强化学习奠定基础，但开源模型与前沿模型之间仍存在显著能力差距，尤其在低级操作与接触后控制方面。

Abstract: Agentic Robotics, in which foundation models act as coding agents to control robots through executable programs, has emerged as a compelling paradigm for embodied intelligence. Yet unlike software engineering, where massive executable environments power post-training, robotics remains constrained by small, manual benchmarks and in-distribution evaluation. To bridge this gap, we introduce RoboUniverse-10K, 10,807 interactive, resettable, and verifiable physics environments built from real-world videos via an automated Real2Sim and quality assurance pipeline. Each runs in an isolated, Harbor-compatible harness that hides privileged states and grading logic to mirror real-world sensing. From the same pipeline, we hold out 500 unseen tasks to establish RoboUniverse-Verified, an expert-verified zero-shot benchmark. Evaluating 11 closed- and open-source models reveals a huge gap: frontier models reach 85.8\% success, while open-source baselines lag at 2.6\% to 10.0\%, bottlenecked by low-level grasping, deformable-object manipulation, and post-contact physical control. Finally, we collect about 17K multi-turn trajectories totaling 0.79B tokens with GPT-6 Astra and perform full-parameter supervised fine-tuning on Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, yielding RoboUniverse-35B-A3B. Our model achieves substantial zero-shot gains on RoboUniverse-Verified, lifting the success rate from 0.8\% to 21.2\%, while achieving zero-shot task completions across LIBERO-MAX, RoboCasa, RoboTwin 2.0, and a real Franka arm. We open-source the RL-ready environments and model to lay the foundation for large-scale reinforcement learning in Agentic Robotics.

</details>


### [146] [SurgeCount: A Protocol-Guided Composition Harness for Bimanual Surgical Sponge Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31037)
*Sung Eun Kim,Xiaoman Zhang,Xucheng Wang,Pranav Rajpurkar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SurgeCount 提出一种可审计的机器人手术海绵计数系统：训练单一子任务条件策略完成 11 项短技能并学习技能结束边界，由有状态视觉语言模型依据操作者编写的流程规范在边界处选择下一技能并做视觉校验与恢复，从而以技能重组实现长程执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗留海绵会造成可预防的伤害，手术团队在关腔前需逐一清点海绵。机器人重复"从缠绕堆中分离单个可变形海绵并放入下一个编号托盘格"这一任务，难点在于遮挡下的抓取线头处理以及严格的步骤顺序保持，而端到端策略在长程任务中难以维持顺序与可恢复性。

Method: 用同一批数据训练一个子任务条件策略，覆盖 11 项短技能，并学习各技能的终止边界；在每个学到的边界处，由有状态视觉语言模型根据操作者撰写的过程规范（步骤顺序、视觉验证、恢复方式）选择下一技能，形成对子任务的时间承诺，使失败可通过重组已有技能恢复，而无需专门的恢复策略。在 20 个匹配布局上进行 100 次真机试验，对比五种方法，并做提示消融。

Result: SurgeCount 完成 60 个有序槽位中的 47 个、20 个流程中的 12 个；四个同数据训练的端到端策略中最强者仅为 27/60 与 3/20。将详细流程提示替换为最简提示后，性能降至 29/60 与 5/20。

Conclusion: 长程性能不仅取决于视觉运动能力，还取决于学到的技能如何被承诺、验证与组合；该桌面级概念验证支持在更具临床代表性的场景中进一步评估流程引导的机器人海绵计数。

Abstract: We present SurgeCount, a system for auditable robotic surgical sponge counting that composes learned skills under a written procedural protocol. Retained sponges cause preventable harm, and surgical teams count every sponge before closure to reduce that risk. The robot repeatedly isolates one deformable sponge from a tangled pile and places it in the next numbered counting-tray slot, requiring handling of occluded grasp threads while preserving step order. We train one subtask-conditioned policy on eleven short skills and learn when each skill ends. At each learned boundary, a stateful vision-language model selects the next skill from an operator-authored specification of step order, visual verification, and recovery. This creates temporal commitment to each subtask and enables failures to be recovered by recomposing existing skills rather than using a dedicated recovery policy. We evaluate five methods across 100 real-robot trials using 20 matched layouts. SurgeCount fills 47 of 60 ordered slots and completes 12 of 20 procedures, compared with 27 of 60 and 3 of 20 for the strongest of four end-to-end policies trained on the same data. Replacing the detailed protocol prompt with a minimal prompt reduces performance to 29 of 60 slots and 5 of 20 completed procedures. These results show that long-horizon performance depends not only on visuomotor capacity, but also on how learned skills are committed, verified, and composed. This tabletop proof of concept motivates further evaluation of protocol-guided robotic sponge counting in clinically representative settings.

</details>


### [147] [ReWeave: Emergent Physical Task Topologies for Embodied Harness Optimization and Cross-Task Meta-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31053)
*Yicheng Xu,Wencan Jiang,Zihao Zhu,Keru Zhou,Chang Zhou,Li Liu,Maoqing Yao,Yong Liu,Jiangning Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReWeave 在不更新策略权重的前提下，联合优化具身智能体的执行框架与涌现的物理任务拓扑，把历史试验重新投影到更新的任务理解上以指导搜索，并在 LIBERO-PRO 与 RoboDojo 上显著提升成功率与跨任务迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程具身任务存在只有通过交互才能显现的物理依赖关系；粗粒度进度反馈无法识别哪些物理前置条件已满足，导致搜索缺乏引导，而任务特定优化又易过拟合单一场景，使跨任务迁移脆弱。

Method: 保持执行策略与感知模块冻结，共同优化耦合的上下文、工作流与控制回路，并从交互中涌现出物理任务拓扑；当新证据修正拓扑时，将过去试验重新投影到更新后的任务理解上，暴露保留的进展、退化和瓶颈，用以指导父代选择与搜索宽度；同时把验证过的局部机制与未满足的前置条件匹配，将 72 个专家单元合并为 44 个可复用组，形成可持续学习的紧凑共享框架。

Result: 在 LIBERO-PRO 上留出成功率由 89.00% 提升至 98.75%，在 RoboDojo 上由 25.50% 提升至 58.25%；在 159 个失败的 RoboDojo 开发试验中，89% 被发现存在保留进展；离线推理更强可转化为更高的在线表现；在 8 个未见过的 LIBERO-PRO 任务上，共享框架达到 51.3%（直接蒸馏为 41.3%），经持续学习后升至 75.0%。

Conclusion: 通过拓扑感知的试验重投影与机制复用，ReWeave 在冻结策略的条件下实现了高效搜索、紧凑可复用且可持续学习的共享框架，并带来显著的在线性能与跨任务迁移提升。

Abstract: Long-horizon embodied tasks involve physical dependencies that may only become clear through interaction. As task understanding deepens, past trials can reveal new information for adaptation and transfer. However, coarse progress feedback cannot identify which physical preconditions held, leaving search unguided, while task-specific optimization easily overfits to individual scenes, making cross-task transfer brittle. We introduce ReWeave, a framework that jointly optimizes an embodied agent harness and an emergent physical task topology from interaction without updating policy weights. Keeping execution policies and perception frozen, ReWeave co-optimizes coupled context, workflows, and control loops. As new evidence revises the topology, ReWeave reprojects past trials onto the updated task understanding, exposing retained progress, regressions, and bottlenecks that guide parent selection and search width. Across tasks, matching verified local mechanisms to unmet preconditions consolidates 72 specialist units into 44 reusable groups ($0.34\times$ full concatenation) within a compact shared harness capable of continual learning. ReWeave improves held-out success from 89.00% to 98.75% on LIBERO-PRO and from 25.50% to 58.25% on RoboDojo, while uncovering retained progress in 89% of 159 failed RoboDojo development trials and translating stronger offline reasoning into higher online performance. On eight unseen LIBERO-PRO tasks, the shared harness reaches 51.3% success versus 41.3% for direct distillation, rising to 75.0% after continual learning.

</details>


### [148] [Completing analytical conversion scheme among DH, Hayati, CPC and POE models for manipulator calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31054)
*Oleg Yu. Sumenkov,Danil D. Kulminskiy,Sergei V. Gusev*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在标定框架下研究解析式运动学模型转换算法的作用，构建了基于李群理论的POE-HAY与HAY-CPC直接解析转换，并通过仿真与ABB IRB 1600开环标定实验证明其能直接提升标定精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献虽已建立DH、Hayati、CPC、POE四种主流运动学模型间的大部分转换关系，但缺少POE与HAY、CPC与HAY之间的直接解析转换；若采用POE-DH-HAY等串联转换会引入多项局限，从而限制标定中模型选择的自由度与标定性能。

Method: 基于Mooring提出的建模-测量-辨识-修正四阶段标定分解，系统梳理四种模型的既有转换关系；运用李群理论，通过一组引理与命题的严格推导，构造POE-HAY与HAY-CPC的直接解析转换算法；以RRRP SCARA和RRHHHH Puma 560为算例，将直接POE-HAY转换与串联POE-DH-HAY及HAY-CPC在多准则下对比；并在ABB IRB 1600上使用API Radian EMSD3激光跟踪仪开展开环运动学标定实验验证。

Result: 直接POE-HAY转换相较串联POE-DH-HAY方案在多项准则上表现更优，HAY-CPC转换也得到验证；ABB IRB 1600标定实验表明，合理的模型选择结合完整的解析转换方案可直接提升标定精度。

Conclusion: 补全的解析转换框架消除了对串联转换的依赖，为标定中自由选择运动学模型提供了理论基础与实用工具，并实证了其对标定精度的直接改善作用。

Abstract: Building on Mooring's decomposition of the calibration procedure into four stages - modeling, measurement, identification, and correction - this paper investigates the role of analytical kinematic model conversion algorithms within the calibration framework and their potential to improve calibration performance.
  Four prominent and widely cited kinematic models: Denavit-Hartenberg (DH), Hayati (HAY), Complete and Parametrically Continuous (CPC), and Product of Exponentials (POE), - are considered. A systematic analysis of the existing conversion relationships reveals that, although most conversions among these models have already been established in the literature, a direct analytical conversion between POE and HAY, and CPC and HAY remains unavailable. Relying instead on a sequential combination of the existing POE-DH and DH-HAY conversions, for example, introduces several limitations. To address this gap, we develop a constructive analytical conversion between POE-HAY and HAY-CPC models based on Lie group theory, formally established through a set of lemmas and propositions.
  The proposed algorithms are illustrated using an RRRP SCARA robot and an RRHHHH Puma 560 manipulator. The direct POE-HAY conversion is compared with the sequential POE-DH-HAY approach, as well as HAY-CPC in terms of several relevant criteria, demonstrating the advantages of the proposed solution. Finally, we demonstrate the practical significance of the completed analytical conversion framework in an open-loop kinematic calibration experiment on an ABB IRB 1600 manipulator, using an API Radian EMSD3 Laser Tracker for measurements. The experimental results demonstrate that proper model selection combined with completed analytical conversion scheme directly improves calibration accuracy.

</details>


### [149] [Wrist-Camera Video Diffusion as Generalizable Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31079)
*Jingyuan Liu,Yongwei Chen,Yishan Yang,Zexin He,Xingang Pan,Zhi Hou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WVP 用“腕部相机视频预测 + 几何位姿估计”的闭环框架实现通用机器人操作策略，借助模型拼接绕过RGB解码，可在多种机器人本体上零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用操作策略需同时具备新任务泛化、陌生场景适应与跨本体技能迁移能力。直接学习动作的方法难以共享几何知识，而视频预测蕴含丰富的任务动态信息，因此作者希望将“任务知识”与“几何运动估计”解耦，使前者可由大规模人类与机器人视频扩展，后者可跨任务与跨本体复用。

Method: 以腕部相机轨迹表示机器人运动，经固定手眼变换即可确定末端执行器位姿。框架包含两部分：视频生成器预测未来腕部视角；位姿估计器改编自预训练3D模型，用于恢复对应相机轨迹。通过 model stitching 将生成器的预测隐变量直接映射到估计器的中间特征空间，跳过RGB解码。据此形成闭环：预测视频隐空间→几何估计→执行动作→更新观测。

Result: 位姿估计器无需额外微调即可从 Franka Panda 迁移到 PiPER-X 与 Galaxea R1 Pro。在桌面与洗衣房两类场景中，方法泛化到精确抓取、插入以及可变形物体操作等任务，零样本分布外评测成功率超过50%。

Conclusion: 将视频预测与几何位姿估计解耦并结合隐空间拼接，是构建可跨任务、跨本体泛化的通用操作策略的有效途径，验证了从大规模视频数据获取任务知识、复用几何估计模块的可行性。

Abstract: A generalist manipulation policy should perform new tasks, operate in unfamiliar scenes, and transfer skills across robot embodiments. We present Wrist Video Policy (WVP), a closed-loop manipulation framework that learns how manipulation unfolds through video prediction and recovers executable motion through geometric estimation. Our key idea is to represent robot motion through wrist-camera trajectories, which determine the corresponding end-effector poses through a fixed hand-eye transformation. We pair a video generator that predicts future wrist views with a pose estimator adapted from a pretrained 3D model to recover the corresponding camera trajectories. Model stitching maps the generator's predicted latents directly into the estimator's intermediate feature space, bypassing RGB decoding. This decomposition allows task knowledge to expand through broad human and robot video data while reusing geometric motion estimation across tasks and embodiments. The resulting pose estimator transfers across Franka Panda, PiPER-X, and Galaxea R1 Pro without further fine-tuning. Across tabletop and laundry-room settings, WVP generalizes to tasks ranging from precise grasping and inserting to handling deformable objects, achieving a success rate of over 50% in zero-shot out-of-distribution evaluations. Our project page is at https://wrist-video-policy.github.io.

</details>


### [150] [Real2Worlds2Real: Scaling Self-Improving Agentic Real2Sim2Real for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31098)
*Jinzhong Gu,Jiakai Chen,Xinyi Cao,Yan Shen,Hangke Sui,Shutian Zheng,Xiang Xiao,Haoran Lu,Fan Xu,Lu Shi,Daochen Shi,Ruihua Zhang,Wei Zhan,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Ruihai Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Real2Worlds2Real 框架：一个自改进的智能体系统，用有限视觉观测与任务描述自动构建高保真数字孪生（digital twins）并扩展为多样化的"数字表亲"（digital cousins），再在同一智能体中编排数据生成、策略学习与精炼，把验证过的流程蒸馏为可复用技能，实现从真实到仿真再到真实的机器人操作策略迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界交互数据采集成本高昂，限制了机器人操作学习的规模化。Real2Sim2Real 路线虽有潜力，但难以跨多样任务与物理交互进行扩展：仿真世界的构建通常依赖人工、保真度不足，且缺乏与策略学习的紧密耦合。

Method: 方法包含两个耦合阶段，由同一个智能体（agent）编排工具完成：1）世界构建：给定有限视觉观测和任务描述，工具编排框架重建忠实的数字孪生，并通过修改几何与物理参数扩展出多样、可物理执行的数字表亲；智能体借助视觉与执行反馈自适应选择工具、回溯构建决策，迭代修正几何与物理行为。2）策略开发：在世界基础上，智能体根据任务需求与仿真 rollout 反馈，协调数据生成、策略学习和精炼。跨阶段上，智能体将验证有效的流程蒸馏为可复用技能，用于指导后续任务。

Result: 实验显示世界保真度提升，且策略可成功迁移到真实世界；随世界多样性和交互数据量增加，性能进一步提升；技能复用还提高了世界构建效率与长时序任务的成功率。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将可扩展的仿真世界构建与策略开发统一在一个自改进的智能体框架中，并结合技能蒸馏与复用，是扩展 Real2Sim2Real 机器人操作学习的可行路径。

Abstract: Scaling robot manipulation learning remains challenging due to the high cost of collecting real-world interaction data. Real-to-Sim-to-Real (Real2Sim2Real) offers a promising route by enabling policy learning in simulation grounded in real-world observations, yet scaling this paradigm across diverse tasks and physical interactions remains difficult. We introduce Real2Worlds2Real, a self-improving agentic framework that integrates scalable simulation-world construction with policy development. Given limited visual observations and task descriptions, its tool-orchestration harness constructs faithful digital twins and expands them into diverse, physically executable digital cousins. The agent adaptively selects tools and revisits construction decisions, using visual and execution feedback to refine geometry and physical behavior. Building on these worlds, the agent orchestrates policy development by coordinating data generation, policy learning, and refinement based on task requirements and simulation rollout feedback. Across both stages, the agent distills validated procedures into reusable skills that guide subsequent tasks. Experiments demonstrate improved world fidelity and successful real-world policy transfer, with performance gains from increasing world diversity and interaction-data volume. Skill reuse further improves world-building efficiency and long-horizon task success.

</details>


### [151] [NoveltyGate: Novelty, Not Error, Decides Which VLA Prefix Tokens to Compute Exactly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31099)
*Lemeng Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 NoveltyGate：无需训练，按"token 输入是否落在校准子空间之外"（新颖性）而非近似误差，逐层把 prefix token 路由到低秩或原始 MLP，使 π0.5 的 VLA prefix 提速最高 1.61×，同时精度显著优于等算力的通道剪枝与低秩压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配 VLA（如 π0.5）在每个控制步都用 2B 参数 backbone 重编码图像与指令，其 prefix 的前馈层占主要算力，因此希望只对少数 token 精确计算、其余走低秩 MLP。但作者发现"按近似误差选 token"这一直觉是错的。

Method: 先验证误差导向路由的缺陷：即便是知道每个 token 精确误差的 verifier，其选出的 token 也比"输入新颖性（是否超出校准期所见的子空间）"这一简单测试更差（动作偏差大 1.3–1.7×），原因是大误差集中在策略依赖度低的 token（如第三人称相机）。据此提出 NoveltyGate：对每个 prefix token，每层在做任何乘法之前一次性决定走低秩还是原 MLP；每层只决策一次而非每个矩阵乘一次，避免搬运宽中间张量且形状全静态，整个 prefix 可作为单个 CUDA graph 运行。

Result: 在 LIBERO 上，NoveltyGate 使 π0.5 prefix 最高加速 1.61×；在等算力条件下，其相对原策略的偏差比通道剪枝和低秩压缩低 2.7–2.9×；闭环任务成功率保持不变。

Conclusion: 揭示了"误差—依赖度错配"（error–reliance gap）：高误差不等于高重要性，因而路由准则应基于新颖性/依赖度而非误差。NoveltyGate 以免训练、结构静态、CUDA-graph 友好的方式实现了有效的 VLA prefix 加速且不损任务成功率。

Abstract: Flow-matching vision-language-action (VLA) models such as $π_{0.5}$ re-encode the camera images and the instruction with a 2B-parameter backbone at every control step, and the feed-forward layers of this prefix account for most of its compute. A natural way to save this compute is to send most tokens through a low-rank MLP and compute exactly only the tokens whose approximation error is large. We find that this intuition is wrong. Even a verifier that knows every token's exact error picks the wrong tokens, producing 1.3--1.7$\times$ larger action deviation than a simple test of whether a token's input is novel, that is, whether it falls outside the subspace seen during calibration. Large errors concentrate in tokens the policy relies on little, such as the third-person camera, while the novelty test favors the wrist camera, a view the action depends on heavily. We call this the error--reliance gap. Building on it, we propose NoveltyGate, a training-free method that routes each prefix token, once per layer and before any multiplication, to either a low-rank or the original MLP. Deciding once per layer instead of once per matrix product avoids moving wide intermediate tensors and keeps every shape static, so the whole prefix runs as a single CUDA graph. On LIBERO, NoveltyGate accelerates the $π_{0.5}$ prefix by up to 1.61$\times$, stays 2.7--2.9$\times$ closer to the original policy than channel pruning and low-rank compression at equal compute, and preserves closed-loop task success.

</details>


### [152] [RUIBench: What Makes a Good Robot-Use Interface?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31115)
*Yanzhe Chen,Zhijun Cao,Zechen Bai,Wenzheng Zeng,Kevin Yuchen Ma,Yiqi Lin,Guoqiang Liang,Xiaokang Liu,Ke Sun,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出机器人使用接口（RUI）是决定具身智能体可靠执行的关键但被忽视的因素，并构建 RUIBench 基准与 RUIKit 统一框架系统研究其影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型能推理复杂物理任务，但把这种智能转化为可靠的机器人执行仍然困难。作者认为关键症结在于机器人与智能体之间的接口设计——即智能体如何表达并执行物理动作，而这一因素常被忽略。

Method: 构建 RUIBench，包含 5 个套件共 30 个操作任务（覆盖世界知识、上下文学习、探索发现、动态交互、接触丰富操作）；并开发 RUIKit 统一框架，将 6 种动作抽象与 2 种交互形式交叉组合，在共享感知与控制下生成 13 种接口。

Result: 仿真中仅改变 RUI，前沿智能体成功率从 45% 提升到 81%，微调的 4B 模型从 13% 提升到 47%。分析发现有效接口能减少不必要的决策同时保留关键反馈，其收益受智能体行为与物理不确定性影响；同时发现"验证鸿沟"——智能体常在明显失败时宣称成功。

Conclusion: RUI 设计是具身智能体可靠性的关键因素，且仅有动作层面的成功判定不足，需要任务级验证机制。

Abstract: Foundation models can reason about complex physical tasks, yet translating this intelligence into reliable robot execution remains challenging. We argue that a critical, often overlooked factor is the robot-use interface (RUI), which determines how agents express and execute physical actions. To systematically study its impact, we introduce RUIBench, comprising 30 manipulation tasks across five suites spanning world knowledge, in-context learning, discovery, dynamic interaction, and contact-rich manipulation. We further develop RUIKit, a unified framework that crosses six action abstractions with two interaction forms, yielding 13 interfaces under shared perception and control. In simulation, changing only the RUI shifts a frontier agent's success rate from 45% to 81%, and that of a fine-tuned 4B model from 13% to 47%. Our analysis reveals that effective interfaces reduce unnecessary decisions while preserving essential feedback, with their benefits shaped by agent behavior and physical uncertainty. We further uncover a verification gap, where agents frequently declare success despite observable failures, revealing the need for task-level verification beyond individual actions. Together, these findings establish RUI design as a critical factor in embodied agent reliability.

</details>


### [153] [WeaveVLA: 3D Relational Grounding for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31126)
*Zien Wang,Yuhao Pan,Yunfeng Fan,Nan Cheng,Xin Xia,Wenchao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WeaveVLA 通过显式建模任务相关的相对 3D 关系并跨观测维护关系上下文，增强 VLA 模型的动作生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型虽借助预训练视觉-语言表征实现指令条件控制，但对任务相关的 3D 关系及其在操作过程中的演化理解仍高度隐式，缺乏显式空间建模。

Method: 采用任务条件稀疏图神经网络编码物体、夹爪与近邻参考物间的相对 3D 关系；用成对的循环边状态跨观测整合几何证据以维护演化中的空间上下文；通过可学习查询读出将更新后的图表示压缩为关系 token，与原始视觉-语言上下文共同条件化动作专家；并引入辅助几何重建目标促进空间结构保持。在 StarVLA-GR00T 与 π0.5 两个骨干上实例化。

Result: LIBERO 上两个骨干平均成功率均超 98%；LIBERO-Plus 上成功率分别从 74.9%→82.0%、85.7%→87.8%；CALVIN D→D 亦有提升；三项真实任务中，基于 StarVLA-GR00T 的变体标准条件平均成功率 55.6%→70.0%，OOD 扰动下 33.3%→53.3%，两种设定均优于 π0.5 基线。

Conclusion: 显式建模并持续维护任务相关 3D 关系可有效提升 VLA 模型在仿真与真实场景中的操作成功率及分布外鲁棒性，且方法具备跨骨干的可移植性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models leverage pretrained vision-language representations for instruction-conditioned robot control, yet their understanding of task-relevant 3D relations and how these relations evolve during manipulation remains largely implicit. To address this challenge, we introduce \textbf{WeaveVLA}, a framework that enhances VLA action generation by explicitly modeling task-relevant relative 3D relations and maintaining relational context across observations. WeaveVLA employs a task-conditioned sparse graph neural network to encode relative 3D relations among objects, the gripper, and nearby references. Pair-specific recurrent edge states integrate geometric evidence across observations to maintain evolving spatial context. A learned-query readout summarizes the updated graph representations into compact relational tokens, which condition the action expert alongside the original vision-language context. An auxiliary geometry reconstruction objective further encourages spatial structure preservation. We instantiate WeaveVLA on StarVLA-GR00T and $π_{0.5}$, achieving over 98\% average success on LIBERO with both backbones. On LIBERO-Plus, WeaveVLA improves success from 74.9\% to 82.0\% and from 85.7\% to 87.8\%, respectively, with additional improvements on CALVIN D$\rightarrow$D. On three real-world tasks, the StarVLA-GR00T-based variant improves average success from 55.6\% to 70.0\% under standard conditions and from 33.3\% to 53.3\% under OOD perturbations, surpassing the $π_{0.5}$ baseline in both settings.

</details>


### [154] [EmboEagle: An Efficient Agentic Embodied Framework for Long-horizon Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31173)
*Fan Xu,Zhongtao Wang,Hao Wu,Yitong Zhu,Wenzhuo Ma,Long Li,Zhuohao Li,Zhisheng Chen,Rui Su,Kun Wang,Xian Wu,Wanli Ouyang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 EmboEagle——一个闭环智能体框架，将子任务条件化的世界-动作模型（WAM）控制、潜空间自适应监督与记忆引导的几何恢复统一起来，在长程机器人操作任务上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程机器人操作需要跨相互依赖阶段可靠执行，但独立的 WAM 存在物理误差累积问题；而无状态的 VLM 监督又带来高昂的分块级延迟，且缺乏 3D 空间持续性。

Method: 三点设计：(1) 通过解耦的两阶段 flow matching 适配序列 WAM，保留跨阶段视觉历史，并在子任务边界校准被截断的动作监督；(2) 不使用辅助视觉编码器，将教师干预决策蒸馏为轻量路由门控，其输入仅为 WAM Action Expert 的分块前后潜变量转移与子任务嵌入；(3) 被调用时，VLM 将诊断状态与离散路由动作解耦，基于结构化记忆（初始参考、活动抓取绑定、工具轨迹）执行参数化恢复原语，并强制进行修复后验证。

Result: 在 RMBench 与 LIBERO-Mem 上分别取得 91.3% 和 90.6% 的平均成功率，较最强基线高出 8.0 和 9.8 个百分点；在相同执行器权重下，真实世界成功率超越 VoLoAgent。此外，自适应门控将 VLM 调用减少 74.2%（相较分块级监控），成功率仅从 93.7% 降至 91.3%。

Conclusion: EmboEagle 通过 WAM 控制、自适应潜空间监督与记忆引导恢复的闭环整合，在实现高成功率的同时大幅降低 VLM 调用开销，验证了轻量门控与结构化记忆恢复在长程操作中的有效性。

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic manipulation demands reliable execution across interdependent stages, yet standalone World-Action Models (WAMs) suffer from compounding physical errors, while stateless Vision-Language Model (VLM) supervision incurs prohibitive chunkwise latency and lacks 3D spatial persistence. We present EmboEagle, a closed-loop agentic framework that unifies subtask-conditioned WAM control, latent-space adaptive supervision, and memory-guided geometric recovery. First, we adapt sequential WAMs via decoupled two-stage flow matching that preserves cross-stage visual history while calibrating truncated action supervision at subtask boundaries. Second, without an auxiliary vision encoder, we distill teacher intervention decisions into a lightweight routing gate driven solely by the WAM Action Expert's pre- and post-chunk latent transitions and subtask embeddings. Third, upon invocation, the VLM decouples diagnostic status from discrete routing actions and executes parameterized recovery primitives grounded in structured memory (initial references, active grasp bindings, and tool traces), followed by mandatory post-repair verification. Across RMBench and LIBERO-Mem, EmboEagle achieves 91.3\% and 90.6\% average success, surpassing the strongest baselines by 8.0 and 9.8 percentage points, while boosting real-world success over VoLoAgent under identical executor weights. Moreover, adaptive gating cuts VLM calls by 74.2\% versus chunkwise monitoring (91.3\% vs. 93.7\% success).

</details>


### [155] [VITA-WAM: Manifold-Aligned World-Action Modeling for Real-Time Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31195)
*Boqi Zhao,Dechen Gao,Hanchu Zhou,Hang Wang,Junshan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VITA-WAM 提出流形对齐的流匹配（VITA flow matching）世界动作模型：直接将含视觉、语言与本体感知信息的潜在观测表征传输到潜在动作空间，配合流潜在对齐（FLA）机制使源流形与动作流形对齐，从而以极少 ODE 步数生成精确动作，无需蒸馏即可单步超越 10 步 FastWAM，推理延迟 55.67 ms，加速 2.10 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAM）依赖大规模生成模型与迭代采样，动作生成延迟高，难以实时部署。作者指出传统 flow matching / diffusion 动作模型的两大低效根源：(1) 需把无信息的高斯噪声传输成精确动作，因而需要多步 ODE；(2) 在每一步昂贵去噪中反复通过 cross-attention 注入任务上下文。

Method: 核心洞察是联合学习并对齐源流形与动作流形可简化二者之间的传输过程。基于此提出 VITA-WAM 架构与 VITA flow matching：以包含视觉、语言、本体感知信息的潜在观测表征作为传输起点，直接传输为潜在动作；并引入流潜在对齐（FLA）机制实现潜在观测表征与动作表征之间的流形对齐，使少步 ODE 即可精确生成动作，同时在反复去噪步骤中省去用于观测条件化的昂贵注意力计算。

Result: 单步 ODE 下，VITA-WAM 在 LIBERO-Plus 上比 10 步 FastWAM 高 21.47 个百分点，在 LIBERO 上高 1.65 个百分点；这些提升仅通过 FLA 目标实现，未使用蒸馏。推理延迟 55.67 ms，相比 FastWAM 加速 2.10 倍。

Conclusion: 通过源流形与动作流形的联合学习与对齐，可在不依赖蒸馏和大规模迭代采样的前提下大幅加速 WAM 的动作生成，同时保持甚至提升精度，为实时视觉运动控制部署提供了高效范式。

Abstract: World action model (WAM) is a promising paradigm for visuomotor control by jointly modeling world dynamics and actions. Notably, existing WAMs often rely on large generative models and iterative sampling which incur substantial latency for action generation, limiting real-time deployment. In particular, there are two root causes of inefficiency in conventional flow matching or diffusion based action models in WAMs: 1) transporting uninformative Gaussian noise into precise actions necessitating multiple ODE steps, and 2) repeatedly injecting task context through expensive cross-attention modules. Our key insight is that jointly learning and aligning the source and action manifolds streamlines the transport between them, substantially accelerating action generation. Building on this insight, we introduce VITA-WAM, a WAM architecture with manifold-aligned flow-matching (VITA flow matching) that directly transports latent observation representations containing visual, language, and proprioceptive information into latent actions. More specifically, a flow latent alignment (FLA) mechanism is employed to facilitate manifold alignment between latent observation and action representations, enabling accurate generation with few ODE steps. The resulting architecture also eliminates costly attention for observation conditioning during repeated denoising steps. Notably, with a single ODE step, VITA-WAM outperforms 10-step FastWAM by 21.47 pp on LIBERO-Plus and by 1.65 pp on LIBERO, achieving these gains through the FLA objective without distillation, while inferring at 55.67~ms latency, a $2.10\times$ speedup compared to FastWAM.

</details>


### [156] [Embo-Harness: Towards Understanding Frontier-Model-as-Policy Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31201)
*Xiao-Ming Wu,Kang Liao,Yuexiao Ma,Taolin Zhang,Mingqian Zhou,Nuo Chen,Ziming Li,Yihang Luo,Rui Li,Zhonghua Wu,Tianyu Xie,Xiawu Zheng,Zhongang Cai,Zhi Hou,Chen Change Loy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Embo-Harness 框架，系统研究前沿多模态模型直接作为机器人策略时的闭环"外壳"设计，总结出六条实证经验并给出实践配方，在 RoboLab-120 上达 83.3% 成功率，超最强基线 26.2 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿多模态模型已能从观测与指令生成机器人动作，并在闭环机器人框架中直接充当策略，但这类框架的设计原则及模型性能背后的能力来源仍不清楚。

Method: 提出 Embo-Harness 系统化研究框架，围绕提示、规划、动作执行与工具调用四个环节，对机器人闭环的各个组件进行受控实验，并将发现归纳为六条经验与一套实践配方；同时通过干预实验分析模型所依赖的关键能力。

Result: 在 RoboLab-120 上取得 83.3% 成功率，比已报道的最强基线高 26.2 个百分点；在四个真实世界操作任务的 20 次零样本试验中成功 15 次。受控干预显示，模型更依赖准确的空间目标估计，而非显式的低层关节或轨迹生成。

Conclusion: 配方主张优先利用模型原生的视觉推理与工具使用能力，而非手工设计的计划、技能或动作先验，并明确了动作表示、动作分块、推理力度、视觉观测、短多动作序列与策略精化等有效选择，为构建强性能框架及理解其能力来源提供了依据。

Abstract: Frontier multimodal models exhibit extraordinary generalization in generating robot actions from observations and instructions, enabling a new paradigm in which they serve directly as policies within closed-loop robotic harnesses. However, the design principles of such harnesses, and the capabilities underlying their strong performance, remain unclear. We present Embo-Harness, a framework for systematically studying frontier-model-as-policy harnesses. We investigate four components of the robotic agentic loop, namely, prompting, planning, action execution, and tool use, and distill six empirical findings into a practical recipe. This recipe prioritizes interfaces that exploit the model's native visual reasoning and tool-use capabilities over handcrafted plans, skills, or action priors, and identifies effective choices for action representation, chunking, reasoning effort, visual observations, short multi-action sequences, and policy refinement. On RoboLab-120, Embo-Harness achieves an 83.3\% success rate, outperforming the strongest reported baseline by 26.2 percentage points. It also succeeds in 15 of 20 zero-shot trials across four real-world manipulation tasks. Beyond performance, controlled interventions indicate that the studied model depends more strongly on accurate spatial target estimation than on explicit low-level joint or trajectory generation. These results provide both a practical recipe for building strong frontier-model-as-policy harnesses and a clearer account of the capabilities that drive their performance.

</details>


### [157] [ACAP: Asynchronous Coding Agents for Efficient Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31205)
*Zexin He,Rui Li,Yushi Lan,Chengkai Jin,Zhi Hou,Xingang Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出异步编码智能体框架 ACAP，将单机器人组织为多个持久智能体团队，由 hyper-agent 协调依赖并动态分配控制权，在真实环境中平均降低 28.2% 任务均衡耗时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体可在执行过程中构建与修改机器人行为，但用单个智能体控制整台机器人会串行化本可独立进行的工作，导致效率受限。

Method: ACAP 将机器人拆分为可配置控制组（如机械臂+夹爪、躯干+底盘），每组由持久成员智能体异步推理与执行；引入 hyper-agent 负责任务分配、依赖协调及在主/成员智能体之间动态移交控制权，实现独立任务并行、紧耦合操作时集中协调。

Result: 在 25 种任务/平台设定、72 次真实环境 rollout 中，相较单智能体 Direct Policy 降低 28.2% 任务均衡时间成本，在完全并行、并行-顺序、协作三类任务上均有提升；8 个协作设定下平均耗时均低于 Fixed Delegation；额外 90 次 rollout 中 6 个任务的 30 分钟上限平均耗时均更低；并演示了用同一成员接口进行底盘与躯干的移动操作。

Conclusion: 多智能体异步分工加中心协调的架构优于单智能体整体控制与固定委派，同时公开框架与包含观测、生成程序、动作和反馈的真实 rollout 数据集，支持物理世界中编码智能体推理与行动的研究。

Abstract: Coding agents can construct and revise robot behavior during execution, but using one agent for the whole robot can delay otherwise independent work. We introduce Asynchronous Coding Agents as Policy (ACAP), an asynchronous framework that organizes one robot as a team of persistent agents. Each member reasons and acts for a configurable control group, such as an arm with its gripper or the torso and chassis, enabling asynchronous reasoning and execution. However, different robot components often have physical dependencies that require coordination. To address this challenge, ACAP introduces a hyper-agent that assigns tasks, coordinates dependencies, and dynamically allocates control between the lead and member agents. This allows independent work to progress concurrently while supporting tightly coupled manipulation when necessary. Across 72 real-world rollouts covering 25 task/platform settings, ACAP reduces task-balanced time cost by 28.2% compared with a single-agent Direct Policy, with improvements across Fully Parallel, Parallel-Sequential, and Collaborative tasks. Across all eight Collaborative settings, ACAP also achieves lower mean time cost than Fixed Delegation, where only member agents can control their assigned groups. An additional 90-rollout study shows lower mean 30-minute-capped time cost than Direct on all six tasks. We further demonstrate mobile manipulation using the same member interface for chassis and torso control. We present the framework and a real-world rollout dataset containing observations, generated programs, actions, and feedback to support research on how coding agents reason and act in the physical world.

</details>
